# 第一章（script.rpy）逐 label 详梳

> 范围：script.rpy 全文（约 4 万余源行），按叙事顺序逐 label 精读。s=Sensei、a=Ami、m=Maya、ay=Ayane、c=Chika、r=Rin、f=Futaba、mi=Miku、mak=Makoto、q=未知声音。所有行号为 script.rpy 源行号；bonus/双版本差异除叙事性差异外不作展开。

## 第一章章节结构总览

第一章在文件内部实际分为四个结构层：

1. **开局序曲层**（start→timetojump→start2→nextstartt，[243]-[639]）：旁白对玩家的"出生前说教"+跳楼入世+教室中醒来。
2. **教学周模板层**（weekdaymorning / afterschool / saturdaymorning-afternoon-night / endof* / advance* 系列）：以 `day` 变量（1-5=周一至周五、6=周六、7=周日）驱动的周循环引擎；`weekdaymorning` [2937]-[3168] 是全书最大的主线事件调度表——按 `totaldays` 与前置旗标依次检查 day5 至 christmastwo1 等上百个事件门，本章全部主线节点由此触发。
3. **周回泛型内容**（各地点子菜单、call 系统、inviteover 邀请系统）：无剧情或支线性质，合并概述。
4. **大型主线节点**：day5-day154 的日常推进段、beachvacation1-16 海滩篇、trinity2/3、halloween1-14 万圣节篇、resetquestions/restofthereset/nextstart2 重置问答段、day214-220 崩坏五连、coolrectanglemachine 终端链与 hoorayanotherreset、christmas1-7 圣诞篇——均逐个展开。

关键计数器：`day` 在 start2 中被直接置为 4（[482]-[483]），即玩家醒来的第一天是"周四放学"→次日周五上第一节课；`totaldays` 为绝对天数。 affection/point/miss 三组变量共数十个在 init 块清零（[76]-[241]），其中包括 `god_love`（神的好感度，[93]）与宿舍战争计分（[238]-[241]）。

## 第一章逐 label 详梳

### 序曲：旁白、跳跃与苏醒

**start [243]**——全篇的元层宣言书。开场旁白独白："There are several things in life I have accepted that I will never understand"（[297]，人生中有几件事我永远无法理解）：其一是灰尘在光照下的显形（不可见之物被电磁波"made visible"，[299]-[300]）；其二是对死亡的浪漫化（[303]）；其三是"你"——"The third thing is you."（[305]）。旁白描述玩家的状态："Trapped in the dark, suspended somewhere between life and death...with eyes swollen shut"（[307]，困于黑暗、悬于生死之间、双眼肿胀睁不开）——强烈暗示接收者是濒死/昏迷的肉体，而"我为你照亮了灰尘"呼应其一。旁白自陈孤独（"It's lonely here"，[309]）、引用"一位著名诗人转述一位著名政治家"的诗句 "There is no beauty without some strangeness in its proportions"（[320]-[321]，培根名言的变体），并预告这个目的地"每一处比例都充满怪异"。旁白预先声明爱："Would it be a bit preemptive for me to tell you that I love you? Not romantically"（[326]-[327]），又预言玩家终将憎恨它：它强迫玩家进行的这些对话会像马蝇幼虫一样啃噬玩家（[330]-[332]，botfly 意象——卵产皮下、孵化食肉，与其后文"hate you"呼应）。自指身份："I was chosen to be your personal, mysterious and anonymous narrator"（[335]，我被选为你的私人、神秘、匿名的旁白）；并留下全书元层总悬念："Will we ever meet for real? Who knows? But I hope so."（[336]-[338]）。随后宣布规则："Nothing is real. Not me...not you...not anyone"（[356]-[357]）、"We're all just air. How about we breathe together?"（[358]-[360]，我们只是空气——来一起呼吸吧；呼吸意象直通 Ami"无法呼吸的女孩"暗线）；"For this world was made for you and you alone"（[363]，这个世界为你一人而造）；但"我没法告诉你它是谁造的，也没法说你凭什么特殊"（[364]）。菜单选"No"分支：旁白陪玩家闲坐、索要回应未果后给出"一次轻推"——不是字面上的推（"Not a literal push. That would be like, really bad right now. I think."，[392]——结合下一 label 的天台场景，这是对坠楼的黑色双关），而是勇气："We're a lot closer than you think."（[398]，我们比你以为的要亲近得多）。

**timetojump [406]**——入世仪式。"Jump as high as you can! As far as you can! Fly away, little bird! Spread those wings of yours! For what awaits you out there is far more amazing than anything you could ever find on this rooftop!"（[410]-[413]）——明确点出场景是天台（rooftop），指令是纵身跃下；"Become a better you! And remember that I'll be right here watching."（[415]-[416]）。菜单六个选项全是 "Jump"（[420]-[430]）：伪选择，玩家别无出路——入世=坠落。这是中层"重置循环"的入口仪式化表达：每次世界开始都伴随一次天台之跳。

**start2 [433]**——苏醒。"Welcome home."（[437]）之后紧跟十六进制密文 `6e 6f 74 68 69 6e 67 20 66 61 6c 6c 73 20 62 75 74 20 6d 65`（[438]），解码为 **"nothing falls but me"**（唯我在坠落）——与上一 label 的天台跳跃互文：世界里万物不动，只有"我"（玩家/Sensei 意识）在不断坠入。随后意识重组：先听见碎片音节 "...i?"（[456]）、"...sei?"（[460]）、拼合为 "Sensei..."（[465]）、"Sensei! Wake up already!"（[469]）——Sensei 这个名字是被外部声音"叫出来"的。Ami 唤醒他：已放学十分钟，校长来了会有麻烦（[475]-[477]）。此处 `$ day += 4`（[483]）：游戏内第一日从 day4 开始。

**nextstartt [490]**——失忆确认与关系网交代。Ami 说"我一直这么叫你啊，Sensei"（[494]）；要摸额头试温被拒。Sensei 内心爆发（也是替玩家发问）："What the fuck is going on right now? Who is this girl? Or, better question, who am I? Why can't I remember anything?"（[508]-[510]）；记忆尝试"每次拼图都绕回原点放弃"（[512]）。他推断对方若是学生就帮不上忙，随即得知真相："You're acting like you don't even recognize your own niece."（[539]，你连自己的侄女都不认得了？）——Ami 是侄女、同住（[552]"We literally live together"）。Sensei 自我解释框架："Some sort of...reincarnation type thing? Wait, doesn't this mean that I must have died, though?"（[556]-[557]，转生？那我是不是已经死了？）。Ami 发现他连名字也忘了："It's Ami, Sensei! Ami!"（[572]）；并自述"helping you is pretty much all I do in the first place"（[580]）。Ami 用漫画作比："Helping you round up your memories sounds kinda fun...It's like we're characters inside of a manga now or something."（[600]）；Sensei 反问结局时 Ami 说"失忆主角的故事到最后一卷通常挺压抑的"（[606]）——元层剧透式伏笔。收束于旁白式内心："Because no one would ever look at me the way she does if this is where I'm meant to be."（[625]）；以及旁白对读者的第二次直接喊话：他把"禁忌领域的幻想"按下不表——"If I did, I can't imagine you'd stay to observe me much longer...perhaps they don't need to be aimed anywhere at all."（[628]-[633]，也许这些念头根本不需要瞄准谁——也许我满足于想象有人在听）。

### 第一夜：世界观规则

**startsleepover [641]**——回家路上/家中。Ami 重新介绍城市："Kumon-mi! A super normal town just outside of Tokyo."（[660]）；抛出战争设定："We're not allowed to leave the city limits anymore because of the war"（[661]）、"Like 99% of the men were drafted into the space army and are off fighting aliens right now or something."（[668]，99%男性被征召进太空军打外星人）——刻意荒谬。Ami 直接打破第四面墙解释："It's an important aspect of the plot that is going to be used as comedic relief in the future, so don't try and focus too much on it or you'll wind up getting distracted from everything else."（[676]）——作者借角色之口告诫玩家别深究战争设定。其余规则：男女比例失衡（[682]）；"Rule number one is keep your hands off all of your students!"（[696]）且"Technically, the rule only applies to school grounds"（[703]）——为后续校外剧情开绿灯。

**amiwhattodo [711]**——可循环问询菜单。①"Tell me about my students"：全班十人含 Ami（[718]）；学校是全女校、Sensei 是唯一男教师、全天包班制兼生活指导员（[719][723]）；有学生长期旷课（[720]）、学期中途还会有新学生转入（[721]-[722]，为后期加人机制铺垫）；Ayane 和 Maya 是多年常客（[726]）；情报来源是房间里"that weird book thingy"（[727]-[729]）。②"Where are your parents?"——Ami 两连拒绝："Ask something else." / "{i}Ask something else.{/i}"（[739][741]）：本章埋得最早的硬暗线，Ami 对父母话题的条件反射式封口（关联一轮报告的 Ami 生母之谜）。③"What should I do?"：日常说明；Ami 自辩不是"只想陪叔叔的怪人"却越描越黑（[766]-[769]）。④"Tell me more about the war"——"Nope!"（[788]）：再次封口。

### 第一夜：日记与学生档案

**nextstart [798]**——离校前撞见 Maya：她全程独自坐在教室（[807]），与 Sensei 长时间对视（[832]-[841]"Sorry, who did you say you were again?"），明确敌意："I think {i}abhor{/i} is a better word"（[894]，用"憎恶"更准确）；要求明晚留宿时 Sensei 进屋后直接回房不许出来直到她离开（[892]）。Sensei 的动机自白值得全文记录："There are plenty of other girls I'm going to be meeting soon, and expecting all of them to like me right away would be...Well, let's just say it wouldn't be any fun at all."（[901]-[902]）；"Conveniences are only blessings in small doses...I want something to chase after."（[906]-[907]）；"And I have already found at least {i}one{/i} thing with a substantial head start."（[909]）——猎物清单思维的确立。到家后的关键对话：Sensei 怀疑 Ami 过度依赖，旁白反问："Is she really so dependent on me that she can't even begin tearing herself away? Or...is it maybe the other way around?"（[932]-[934]，还是说反过来才对？）——依赖方向反转是全书 Ami 线的核心命题。Ami 解释仍叫"Sensei"是因为叫了太久（[945]），并说"Just...we're playing our actual roles and not...creative ones."（[950]，我们在玩的就是真实身份扮演）。卧室描写：模型与游戏机、不像成年教师的房间（[976]-[977]）；Sensei 之问："I wonder what happened to the old Sensei? Or, better yet—I wonder what happened to the old me?"……"Probably nothing."（[981]-[994]）。随后翻开皮革日记（既是学生名册又是前任 Sensei 的日记，[1037]-[1039]），十份档案（非 bonus 版文本）：**Ami**——bonus 版提及"the accident"（事故）后才变成现在这样、"Ami doesn't like talking about the accident. Neither do I. It should be avoided at all costs."（[1018]-[1030]）；非 bonus 版恶搞性改为"注册会计师，父母死了所以搬来"（[1032]-[1033]）。**Maya**——"has never liked me and has always avoided me for seemingly no reason whatsoever...is even more deeply troubled by my existence. I should avoid her at all costs as well."（[1046]-[1049]）。**Ayane**——家里向学校捐过巨款、父亲是城中仅存的少数男性但她对父亲毫无依恋（[1062]）。**Yumi**——班级不良，常去疑似极道盘踞的游戏厅、征兵后不去了，态度日益恶化（[1068]-[1072]）。**Chika**——人气高、聪明但不努力、常逃自习去厕所玩手机；"has a good heart, but is often too caught up in her own world to notice when the one around her is beginning to come undone."（[1081]，注意不到周围的世界正在崩解——精确的死亡flag式评语）。**Makoto**——学生会长/班长/自称助教，成绩第一、零问题，"biggest flaw is that she goes above and beyond far too frequently, to the point where I believe it might be damaging to her."（[1090]）。**Futaba**——自卑、逃避体育、需要学会爱自己（[1096]-[1099]）。**Miku**——话痨、体育尖子足球王牌、其余科目需大幅补课、与 Makoto 极亲（[1105]-[1109]）。**Rin**——看似冷静实则易兴奋，"goes through short periods of time where she doesn't act like herself and has disappeared for days on several occasions."（[1116]，周期性不像自己+多次失踪数日——重要异常档案）。**Sana**——除了 Ayane 没人了解她；"it's less because she doesn't want to and more because she {i}can't{/i}"（[1125]，不是不想说话是不能说）。夜间收尾：Ami 备好热水澡和晚饭；Sensei 致谢，Ami 回答"Saying it like that makes it sound like you didn't exist {i}until{/i} today, Sensei. And we both know that's not true at all."（[1165]）——字面意义上的恐怖真话。就寝前 Sensei 决意："If I wake up in this world...I will start a new life. And I will do my best to cherish it."（[1180]-[1183]）。系统教程由斜体系统音旁白输出（ami_love+1，[1190]-[1207]），末尾威胁式幽默"This will come in handy once I start showing you how much I hate you."（[1202]）。`$ day += 1` 后进入 firstfriday（[1211]-[1216]）。

**firstfriday [1220]**——夜梦过渡。非 bonus 版自嘲："Just kidding! This is a family friendly game without any adult content...I have a completely normal dream about Ami."（[1226]-[1230]，开个玩笑！这是全年龄游戏……我做了一个完全正常的关于 Ami 的梦）；bonus 版为成人梦境（略）。设 `$ amiawake = True`（[1232]）。

**amiwakesupyeah [1234]**——次晨唤醒。非 bonus 版笑点：Sensei 醒来问"Are taxes due yet?"（[1269]，报税期到了吗）并坚称"那才是正常的梦"；Ami 催促十五分钟内出门上学、早餐已在桌上（[1277]-[1279]）。

### 第一节课与新方法

**thefirstclass [1309]**——本章最长主场景之一，三段式。（一）**上学路**：Ayane 登场，早安吻语式骚扰（[1331]"looking even more handsome than normal"）；听闻失忆后先确认"你还记得我是谁吗"，得到肯定答复便说"否则我就只能自杀了"（[1349]）；自封与 Ami 是"同一战壕的同志"（[1351]）；宣称周末与 Sensei 有约会，被 Ami 以"本周末我们要做重要的财务决策"拦下（非 bonus 版梗，[1362]）；Ayane 提出被收养方案（[1364]-[1371]）。此段结尾是全书最直白的堕落预告书，Sensei 内心独白：昨夜对两人想象后的自渎行为（[1396]）"But that's fine...soon, I'll be confident enough here to fully assume the role I've been given...to act in stark contrast to that and become who I think they need me to be and not what they want."（[1398]-[1400]）；并预言"These days where the bright sun shines down on my back will turn into days of melancholy. And then to days of depravity. To days of nothing but the feeling of flesh on flesh and the loneliness that accompanies the moments after..."（[1402]-[1404]，阳光之日将变为忧郁之日、放纵之日、肌肤相亲及其后孤独之日）——第一章基调宣言："I must assimilate."（[1410]，我必须同化）。（二）**教室**：Makoto 送讲义；Sensei 直呼"Makoto"令其失态（她此前一直是"Miyamura"，[1450]）；Sensei 内心定策："If I'm ever going to get to where I want to be with any of these girls, I'm going to have to start chipping away at the walls around them. The weakest point...is as simple as the way I address them."（[1457]-[1458]，攻城策略=称呼）。进教室后发现 Yumi 缺席（Chika 转告装病在家，[1507]）；Chika 申请翘掉晨间班去看偶像电视节目获准（[1515]-[1516]，Sensei 上任第一笔人情交易）；Makoto 抗议程序不当（[1538]-[1551]）；Miku、Ayane 插科打诨（Ayane 要求改称 darling/honey/hot stuff 并扬言"一笔捐款能摆平一切"，[1563]-[1566]）。Sensei 发布"新方法"演说：不再布置作业、白天自学、"I want you to see me as a friend first and foremost...and a teacher second."（[1627]）；Futaba 战战兢兢提问，Makoto 反驳"We...do need another authority figure, though..."（[1644]，我们确实需要一个权威人物）——两人核心特质首次正面对撞。Chika 返场补刀"Sensei 中年危机想跟我们做朋友"（[1660]）。最后一节测验后放学（Sensei 明言不会批改，[1709]-[1710]）。（三）**办公室**：Makoto 兴师问罪（"dismantle all that you have built"，[1751]）；追问业余爱好时 Makoto 支吾后坦白秘密：**打工**——学生手册 E 节明令禁止、可致退学（[1809]-[1815]）；家里店铺祖传三代、城市被封锁男性被征召后只剩母女经营（[1840]-[1846]）；"working and studying take up pretty much all of my time. I don't really have any hobbies"（[1854]-[1855]）。Sensei 听完让她闭眼——bonus 版进入按摩支线（makotoofficemassx）；非 bonus 版为纯搞笑彩蛋：办公桌下暗道、"Hogwarts School of Witchcraft and Wizardry"、分院帽，并借 Sensei 之口说出元台词"This event was never changed and has always been like this."（[1891]，这个事件从未被改动过、一直都是这样——开发者对旧版本遗留内容的自嘲）；"the event ends before she's ever sorted into a house"（[1896]）。`firstclass=True`、makoto_love+1（[1899]-[1902]）。

### 第一夜（二）：留宿派对

**slumparty [1910]**——两幕结构。**第一幕（Sensei 独白）**：自我评估为"史上最不合格的生活指导员"（[1916]）；确立攻略总方针——十个女生须雨露均沾，"Focusing too much on one will just wind up drawing attention to us"（[1926]），并冷静规划每人的底线探测（"I just have to figure out how thin or thick those lines are and the best methods to cross over them."，[1929]）。bonus 版回家发现 Ami 忘锁门，闪过"可能被强奸杀害"的担忧后决定不管（[1953]-[1963]），并有"背景中的齿轮已经在驱动一台为我造乐的机器"的旁白（[1969]）。**第二幕（Meanwhile…客厅女孩三人组）**：本章最重要的群像戏与广告恶搞密集段——Ayane 讲自己被便利店永久禁入的光辉史（[1990]）；DoorDash 广播剧式植入，旁白立刻切割"Lessons in Love is not sponsored by nor affiliated with DoorDash."（[2007]）；Ayane 家族财富来源=父亲发明"永不破的气泡膜"（unpoppable bubble wrap，[2016]），旁白顺势宣布资料片《A World Without Bubbles!》GameStop 预购送她爸三套服装（[2019]-[2025]）——开发者 Selebus 式商业恶搞。Ayane 称父亲为宿敌（只喝威士忌听平滑爵士，[2045]-[2046]）；Maya 质问她为何迷恋 Sensei："He's just some...normal old guy...Is that really all you look for in a partner? Age and...age?"（[2066]-[2067]）；Ayane 的回答是她的核心定义："Sensei...is everything I like in a person. He's smart...and he's kind...and he's funny..."（[2075]-[2077]）；Maya 吐槽"Twice your age."（[2078]）；非 bonus 版 Maya 的回应是一句惊人的元预言："Oh man. You just wait until the later chapters. You don't even know what you're talking about."（[2094]，等到后面几章你就知道自己多天真——Maya 作为重置循环知情者对"later chapters"的直视）。Ayane 幻想婚后七孩（[2098]），被逼急后掏出手枪："Then I end my life the only way I know how."（[2122]）；"Aren't guns illegal in Japan?"——"Nothing is illegal when you're rich."（[2129]-[2134]）。管家 **Geoffrey** 设定投放：美国移民、全家被害、一边做管家一边复仇，旁白自称签了 NDA 只能透露这么多，再推资料片《A World Without Geoffrey's Family!》预购送"Geoffrey 妻子的三口棺材"（[2139]-[2145]）；补充爱尔兰黑帮恩怨与"消失几天回来浑身是血"（[2151]-[2153]）。Ayane 起身热饭+按 Sensei 规矩爆米花（"看电影的没爆米花是罪过，我不想被上帝弄死"，[2170]）——三十秒后她推错浴室门，目睹 Sensei 自慰（成人内容从略；对话仅存两句冷笑话：ay"That...That's a really big penis..." s"Thanks. I appreciate that."（[2213]-[2215]）；ay"Okay. I'm gonna go die now."（[2222]））。走廊崩溃戏成为 Ayane 名场面："All I know is penis. All I know is penis."（[2248]）、请求 Maya 陪睡因为"它在我脑子里"（[2265]）；Maya："Shut up, shut up, shut up. I never heard this"（[2273]）。旁白盖棺："And that was the first time Ayane ever saw a penis in real life...Some say, she's still immobilized outside of that bathroom door to this very day..."（[2282]-[2283]）。此事件是 Ayane lust 线的原初创伤/启蒙双重起点。

**afterbrscene [2288]**——浴室事件收束+系统教程+人生宣言。Sensei 评估："even if nothing comes of it, I know for a fact that the image of me is now engraved into her mind."（[2296]，我的形象已经刻进她的脑海——主动利用偷窥后果）。宣言三连："I will make the most of this life. I will live for myself. And if others get hurt in the process, I will do what I do best and write them off."（[2309]-[2311]，若有人受伤，我会使出老本行把他们一笔勾销）。入睡前的三个声音："A voice in the back of my mind reminds me that none of this is real. A second one fights against it. A third carries me off to sleep."（[2315]-[2317]）——表层主角对中层世界本质的一瞬洞察，被睡眠吞没。随后斜体旁白进行玩法教程（周末三时段制、电话系统、事件锁链，[2322]-[2329]），并以两句元台词收尾："And if you get lost, remember that's exactly where I want you."（[2327]）；"This world was made for you. Please enjoy it to your heart's content."（[2331]-[2332]）。

### 周循环引擎与泛型模板（组合概述）

**advancetosat [2342] / endofsat [2766] / endofweekday [2800] / advancetosun-mon-tues-wed-thurs-fri [2828]-[2887]**——日期推进管道：`totaldays += 1` 配合 `day` 在 1-7 循环（advancetomon 中 `day -= 6`，[2840]）；各分支校验失败会显示 "ERROR ADVANCING TO XXX"（如 [2846]）——开发期调试残留原样保留。endofsat/endofweekday 仅播放固定就寝文案。

**saturdaymorning [2353] / weekdaymorning [2888] / saturdayafternoon [2529] / saturdaynight [2595]**——四时段枢纽。除地点菜单（上午：弓道场/咖啡馆/图书馆/泳池/球场/Ami 房等；下午：商店街/商场/神社/道场/钱汤/图书馆；夜晚：酒吧/成人用品店/Molly 咖啡馆/拉面店/女仆咖啡/便利店/教堂/潜水/宿舍；另可打电话、用电脑、邀请女生过夜）之外，真正的价值在于其**主线调度表**：`weekdaymorning` 从 [2937] 起按序检查 day5→day7→day8→day12→…→christmastwo1 共六十余个事件门，条件多为 `totaldays >= N` 加前置旗标（如 day14 需 `firsttimelibrary`、day36 需 `bar5`、beachvacation1 需 totaldays≥174 且十余项旗标齐备，[3041]-[3043]、[2432]）；`saturdaymorning` 另管 day24/day80/day102/beachvacation1（[2424]-[2433]）；`saturdaynight` 管 day130/day344/ayanespecial55 等（[2597]-[2607]）。这份调度表就是章节流程图的机器可读形式：本章主线节奏完全由"天数门槛+支线完成度"双闸门控制，意味着玩家可以用挂机天数强行推进部分节点，但海滩/万圣节/圣诞等大节点必须集齐特定支线。另外 saturdaymorning 开头批量结算跨章状态（harukanumber、makoto_virgin 等来自 halloween11/kirindate25 等后续事件的追溯旗标，[2371]-[2391]）——证明该模板被全书复用而非第一章专用。

**afterschool [3189] / afterschoolevent [3208] / afterschoolmenu [3226] / callmorning [3334] / callafternoon [3398] / callnight [3462]**——工作日下午/晚间枢纽与电话邀约系统（需先取得号码）；均为纯功能 label，无独立叙事，故并入本条。afterschool 按 chapthreeactive/christmas7 切换文案（[3191]-[3197]）。

### 日常推进段（day5-day28）

**day5 [3526]**——首个"完整一周"开始。晨路与 Ami 斗嘴（失忆=老年痴呆的 gaslight 玩笑，[3540]-[3543]"being gaslit by my niece"）。Sensei 的目标在此被最直白地写出："I believe that the best thing to do during times like this is to just let the seizure play out...To have sex with as many of his students as possible. And to not get caught in the process of that."（[3557]-[3560]——把青春期少女比作癫痫发作、最好的处理是不干预；人生唯一目标=睡到尽可能多的学生且不被抓）。Maya 加入晨路："If the devil existed, I do imagine he'd look something like you."（[3578]）；自述失眠加剧（[3602]-[3603]）、骂 Sensei 是她"睡得好的补偿机制"（[3605]）。**本 label 最关键的中层泄露**：Maya 说最近一直做怪梦，"It's like reality, but...Upside down?"（[3651]-[3652]），随后"This sort of thing always happens around the start of a cycle. I wouldn't worry too much about it."（[3661]，这种事总是发生在 cycle 开始的时候）——Ami 问"Cycle? Do you mean...semester?"，Maya 答"Yes, that."（[3662]-[3667]）：**cycle 被当场替换成"学期"**，重置循环概念在第一章第 5 天即已出口，只是被话术覆盖。附带设定：Maya 在神社当巫女（miko 服装之争，[3634]-[3641]），并说"Gods do have a history of creating asinine rules and then dishing out punishment to those who refuse to abide by them. Frankly, being smitten by them would solve a multitude of problems for me when my existence is nothing short of Hell to begin with."（[3641]-[3642]——神有立恶规再罚人的历史；我的存在本身就是地狱，被神砸死反而省事）。

**day7 [3734]**——双线。（一）Ayane 跟踪逃掉体育课的 Sensei：性地下室玩笑（[3758]-[3799]）、自称在学校有个没告诉过任何人的密室（[3786]"I actually already have my own secret room at school that I haven't told you about"）、"You'll never know when you need a secret room, Sensei. I learned that in the war."（[3789]——战争梗第二次被用作万能解释）。（二）Meanwhile 体育馆：Yumi 对 Futaba 的身材羞辱全景（"Fatass""beached whale"级辱骂，[3885][4097 前身]），Chika 护航并带 Futaba 撤退；细节：Yumi 被指出从初中就认识 Rin 却装不记得其名（[3858]-[3864]）；Chika 自曝不敢动手是因为付不起 Yumi 的医药费、"that girl ain't got shit on me"（[3967]-[3968]）。futaba_love+1。

**day8 [3993]**——Yumi 办公室对峙。Yumi 拒绝一切沟通："You hate me, I hate you. That's how this shit goes. That's how this shit stays."（[4041]）；Sensei 无计可施，自问"Just what was her impression of the last me like?"（[4046]——前任 Sensei 与她的恩怨是暗线）。非 bonus 版整场冲突被替换为"口香糖"闹剧（Yumi 讨厌口香糖），并配元台词："Woah, is that the secret truth about what happened in this office that will be alluded to for the next several years? Who could have envisioned it would be something so silly?"（[4060]——作者调侃双版本审查改写本身）。收束独白点题："People don't become that way overnight...Something must have happened to her."（[4206]-[4210]）——Yumi 创伤悬念挂起。另 bonus 版 Yumi 点破学校雇佣问题："I still can't believe this place even hired someone like you!"——旁白"To be fair, they kind of didn't..."（[4140]-[4142]，这学校其实根本没雇过"他"）。

**day12 [4229]**——喜剧回。Makoto 强制补课救 Miku（标准测验临近）；"pi 词源"与线粒体教学闹剧（Miku 害羞误以为是别的课，[4303]-[4328]）；Sensei 开溜撞见 Chika，被拽进校内禁地躲 Makoto（成人版暗示为女更衣室类场所，[4412]-[4418]"nobody ever comes here after school hours anyway"；非 bonus 版以"Chika mugs me and gives me a swirly and I cry a lot."（[4447]）一笔带过）。chika_love+1。

**day14 [4461]**——Futaba 咨询室事件（本章情感重头戏之一）。起因：Yumi 当着 Sensei 面继续霸凌，Makoto 代行教师职责（[4469]-[4472]）；Yumi 放学未到场，来的是 Futaba。Futaba 的自我厌恶剖白："I guess it's kind of hard to stand up for myself when I...don't really see anything worth standing up for?"（[4558]）、"Being heavy...Is really hard, Sensei..."（[4595]-[4596]）、对 Yumi 身材的嫉妒（[4585]-[4589]）。Sensei 的应对是精心计算的越界："In that case, I find you extremely attractive."（[4624]）并主动把话术设计成"灰色地带"："But I didn't say any specifics, so all you can really do is assume and file this under some sort of gray area."（[4648]）。Futaba 以气声回敬 "Attracted to you..."（[4670]）后慌忙逃走。收尾独白撕开温情假面："The sadder truth, however—Is that I want everyone. And that she's no more special than the rest of them."（[4718]-[4720]，可悲的真相是：我全都要，她并不比其他人特殊）。另注意 Sensei 对自身资格的自省："I'm not qualified to make anyone feel better about themselves—not when, more often than not, I can't even pinpoint how I personally feel."（[4600]）。

**day16 [4733]**——Ayane 装病计划+初吻。开场即幕后：Ayane 拉不会说谎的 Sana 排演晕倒戏（排练三次，[4751]-[4770]），扬言再等一天就"引爆炸弹和全校同归于尽"、Sana 回"My Switch is in there"（[4768]-[4769]）。体育课上 Sana 夸张报幕（"THE LIGHT IS FADING FROM HER EYES!"，[4823]），Sensei 公主抱送医务室——校医不在（[4871]，显然是局中局）。帘后 Ayane 卸下人格认真提问："When did you start seeing me as a girl and not just a 'girl?'...Then, when did you start thinking about me, like...sexually?...Was it after the bathroom incident?"（[4929]-[4947]）；并坦白核心转变："All this time, I've been saying all of that stuff about being in love with you in a joking manner because...the thought of anything ever coming from it was just a joke to me...But now? Now, any time I say any of that stuff, it's not really a joke at all. It's a wish."（[4976]-[4983]——玩笑变愿望，Ayane 线的定音锤）。Sensei 内心以寄生真菌意象解读她的爱恋（"hormonal lust consuming her like it's the physical manifestation of a parasitic fungus"，[5017]），接吻段落全程延续孢子/宿主隐喻，并写下全书最冷的一句内心话："I must focus everything I have on not killing the host body before the fungus does. If she does this to herself, it won't be my fault."（[5030]-[5033]）。Ami 到医务室找人，两人在帘后继续深吻直到她离开——"Ami could have opened up that curtain at any moment. But she didn't. I don't know if she's just that absentminded...or if she was afraid of what she would find."（[5058]-[5060]）。

**day20 [5077] + restofrin [5243]**——教室午后小品。Sensei 上课睡觉（"Please don't cause any trouble while I am gone. And, in the event that I wake up without my memories, please ask Ami to once again escort me home."，[5101]——失忆梗自嘲）。主戏为 Chika×Rin：Rin 被 Chika 搭话后宕机式慌乱（"You...of me?....Thinking? With brain?"，[5148]；内心"Relax, Rin. You fell asleep in class and this is all just a dream."，[5157]）；绿发周黑历史互曝（[5170]-[5184]）；条件分支按 rin_love 插入 Rin 对 Sensei 变化的观察："He's like...a totally different person lately."（[5202]/[5239]）以及 Chika 同感："It feels like the entire class has been different for the last couple weeks. It barely even feels like school anymore."（[5244]-[5245]）——全班层面感知到世界异变的最早文本。restofrin 结尾 Chika 正式邀 Rin 出去玩，Rin 事后问 Futaba"我刚才是幻觉吗"，并宣布"I am going to sleep now. But if I start making weird noises or talking or something, please hit me."（[5288]-[5293]）。

**day21 [5309]**——**中层浓度最高的日常节点，必读**。开场设问三周以来"Something seems off."（[5319]）。放学后的存在论滑坡：走廊无人，"{s}Not even me.{/s}"（[5328]——删除线：连我也不在）；四个问题"What am I doing? Why am I doing it? Where do I go? When will I get there?"如入侵物种繁殖（[5335]-[5340]，每走一步都像踩碎鸡蛋）；关键句："If I stay here, I risk losing myself—or whatever faint traces of me this body has managed to absorb during its brief vacation in an incorrect plane."（[5346]——这具身体只是在"错误位面短暂度假"时吸收了些许"我"的残迹）。随后的野心宣言以删除线系统腔呈现："This place was built for {s}you{/s} me."（[5381]——原模板句"这个世界为你而建"中的 you 被划掉改成 me！）；"Everyone else is just furniture. Everyone else is a part of the pile."（[5383]-[5384]）；配合"用完的少女堆成山"幻想（[5360]-[5364]）。然后出现**人行道尽头+电视雪花噪声发光通道**："a glowing passageway of TV static beckoning me forward...It's like it's trying to help me. And yet—I can not find it within me to move my legs."（[5373]-[5377]）——出口具象化但主角拒绝离开。巷子里的声音把他拉回来：是 Yumi。她点破异常："You've been acting really fucking weird lately...It's almost kinda like you're tryin' to remember something."（[5426][5484]）；并说出她的街头哲学"We're all just...trying to survive in our own ways"（[5464]）。结尾明示本事件的功能："But because she was able to prevent me from walking toward the light."（[5571]——Yumi 拦住了他走向光）。最后五条**带删除线的系统播报**依次闪现："{s}Congratulations!{/s} / {s}You have unlocked a special event with {b}[[redacted]{/b}!{/s} / {s}There is something buried underneath your feet.{/s} / {s}探してみませんか？{/s} / {s}Yumi's affection has xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx!{/s}"（[5572]-[5576]，含日文"要不要试着探索一下？"）——被屏蔽的隐藏事件通知；`$ roomwithclocks = True`（[5579]）正是后文 inviteover 中 specialclassroom（埋藏之物）选项的开关。Yumi 在此章的结构性身份首次显形：**世界的锚点/阻止者**。

**day24 [5584]**——周末清晨喜剧+Ami 暗线推进。Sensei 早醒挨饿，去敲 Ami 房门发现反锁；门内 Ami 正在自慰（成人内容从略；事后台词"He almost saw...He almost saw me...Why did I lock it?...I'll let you do whatever you want to me!"，[5676]-[5678]）；开门对峙（"Are you masturbating in there?"，[5697]；"I'M A GROWING GIRL, OKAY?!"，[5761]）。Sensei 的事后分析是本 label 的文学核心："The most perplexing part of all of this is why she locked the door...if I had to take a guess—It's because she's not all that different from me at the end of the day. And that sometimes—When things feel too good to be true—We convince ourselves they aren't."（[5783]-[5791]，太美好的事物我们会说服自己它是假的——把 Ami 与自己的自欺并列）。早餐转场市场摊位：耗子药笑话（Ayane 把全镇耗子药用光了，[5809]-[5815]）；罗密欧与朱丽叶之辩中 Ami 回忆幼年共读莎剧"Especially when M—"（[5856]，**被切断的"M—"**：Mother/Maya/Miku？一轮报告已注意到 Ami 生母之谜，此处是最早的话头掐断实例）；随即切菜割伤手指，叙事突然坠入恐怖假象："Ami bled to death all over the bathroom floor. I keep what's left of her thumb in a jar on my nightstand. I kiss it before going to sleep."……"Just kidding."（[5889]-[5896]——死亡叙述+拇指罐头+吻瓶子，再宣布开玩笑：对玩家信任的一次恶意操弄，也是"Ami 死亡"母题的第一次预演）。

**day26 [5916]**——Makoto 的"非告白"。开场撞破 Makoto 对镜彩排告白的独白（含 pornshop 分支吐槽 Sensei 夜闯成人用品店，[5942]-[5948]）；Sensei 用"汽车延保诈骗电话"梗拆台（[5969]）。Makoto 反复强调"This isn't a confession"却步步接近表白：动机是她的人生僵局——"Every day is the same thing. Over and over and over. It's like...I'm stuck in a loop or something."（[6099]，**loop 一词由 Makoto 说出**，与 Maya 的 cycle 呼应）；以及"If you can change your mindset and start changing yourself...why can't I?"（[6117]）。请求内容：偶尔在校外见面（坚持不是 date，[6124]-[6131]）。Sensei 内心冷峻复盘："A fulfilling life, huh? Is that really what this is? Taking control of someone else's body? Stepping in and single-handedly dismantling everything they built? Is this really what it means to be fulfilled?"（[6136]-[6138]）。结尾担忧若帮不了她逃离这种人生会怎样，"Here's hoping I make a suitable raft for her to float away on."（[6166]，木筏意象）。

**day28 [6179]**——Ami 本生灯纵火未遂+食堂 Maya×Yumi。前半：Ami 差点烧了教室，被罚一起打扫（[6186]-[6200]）。后半 Meanwhile 是 Maya 情报的重要投放：旁白插话"For her, loneliness means something different. Something much bigger. But that's something you're not ready to hear about yet!"（[6252]-[6254]——元层扣留 Maya 的秘密）。Yumi 盘问 Maya："where the fuck did you even come from anyway? I've lived here my whole life and I haven't even seen you until this year."（[6342]，**Maya 是今年才出现的"外来者"**——与重置循环直接挂钩的硬线索）；Yumi 断言 Maya 的安静人设是博关注（[6339]），Maya 全程字面化回应（"Oh no. Please don't go. If you leave, who will watch me eat?"，[6368]）；结尾笑点："I forgot chopsticks."（[6383]）。Ami 补充 Maya 设定："The amount that girl is able to eat is one of the greatest mysteries of this world."（[6404]）。

### 日常推进段（day33-day48）

**day33 [6678]**——卡拉OK回（纯喜剧缓冲层）。Ayane 每次必唱且只唱《Despacito》并"献给我的未来丈夫、最好朋友的叔叔"（[6797]）；西语是管家教的（学校根本不开西语课，[6831]）；Maya 被证实唱歌很好但平时只顾吃（[6733]-[6739]）；Ami/Maya 借口买食物溜走把 Sensei 留给 Ayane 独唱，结尾旁白戏仿："There is no one left in this world that I can trust...Despacito consumes me whole."（[6875]-[6877]）。三人 affection 各+1。

**day36 [6895]**——Miku/Sana 值日回，暗线密集。①隔壁班 Watabe 老师"给人发毛、房间总有蜡烛味"（[6983]-[6984]——神秘教师侧写，供后续世界拼图）。②Sana 的过去出现**记忆空洞**：Miku 称两人一年级就认识、Sana 却否认"We...only talked a couple times..."（[7051]-[7052]）；Miku 追问她当年和谁一起玩时话头被掐断："Who did you hang out with back then, Sana? I remember there bein' somebody, but I can't put my finger on—"（[7059]，Sana 立刻用发现杂物间转移话题）——与 Sana 档案"不能说"呼应。③Sana 顺口说"I can just text my mom and tell her I'll be late for work"（[6990]，学生打工+有母亲联络，与 Makoto 打工禁令并置）。④太空战争梗第三次出现（Miku："I'm going to have to join the space war..."，[6961]）。⑤杂物间"恰好开着门"的便利性再次被旁白点出（[7068]-[7069]）。

**day38 [7120]**——公园偶遇 Makoto（承接 day26 的校外之约）。元层玩笑开场：Sensei 白日梦"神秘转校生"，Makoto 查过花名册确认"我们没有任何转校生"（[7120][7128]）。**本 label 两条世界级线索**：其一，天气异常——"The weather here has been perfect lately...I can't even remember the last time it rained."……"it's been bright and sunny every single day since I woke up here. That...has to lead to some sort of environmental problems eventually, doesn't it?"（[7196]-[7201]，自我苏醒以来从未下雨：世界的气象停滞）；其二，城镇变异——Makoto："One of my favorite things about Kumon-mi is how it's always changing. It's like...every time you wake up, there's something a little different going on. We didn't even have the cafe until recently."（[7260]-[7262]，每次醒来城镇都有点不同；咖啡馆是不久前才"出现"的——重置循环的空间学）。系统腔"weebnote"科普少女漫画并自嘲（[7243]-[7245]）。私人辅导违规梗：手册 9C 条禁止学生进入教师住所，bonus 版 Sensei 直认"I am an exception to that rule...I do, actually."（[7302]-[7307]，答非所问的黑色幽默），非 bonus 版改为他"联系校长把自己加为例外"。Makoto 披露志向是将来当教师（[7352]），Sensei 自问"前任到底做了什么让她这么崇拜他？"（[7355]-[7356]）。被问及 Ami 是否爱他时，Sensei 答"I am her uncle after all."——紧接旁白一词："Kind of."（[7366]-[7368]，**"算是吧"**——叔侄关系本身被打上问号，全书最早的一处）。结尾：Yumi 在暗处目击二人（y"..."×5，[7404]-[7408]，直接衔接 day44）；对 Makoto 强颜欢笑的判词："And so I know that smile is no smile at all. But—It's still beautiful in its own backwards way."（[7394]-[7396]）。

**day40 [7424]**——Chika 晨间补习回。情报点：Yumi 整个周末心情极差（[7484]，即 day38 公园目击之后，时间线互锁）；Chika 替 Yumi 辩护"她过得不容易""需要比你更大的东西才能拯救她"（[7501]-[7520]）；**Chika 曾留级一年**而 Sensei 一无所知——"Wait, you didn't know that? I figured that would have been something they told you in the beginning of the year."…"Or...never learned it in the first place."（[7550]-[7558]，前任记忆缺口的又一实证）；chikadorm15 分支披露她在照顾 Chinami："It's not exactly easy taking care of a kid at my age"（bonus 版，[7569]）。结尾 Chika 问出那个危险的问题："What made you decide to get all...cool this far into the school year?...It's more like...you're a completely different—"（[7606]-[7610]）——上课铃打断，Sensei 逃过回答。

**day44 [7651] + restofyumibridge [7984]**——**本章最黑暗节点，Yumi 桥梁事件**。开场城市地理："This town has two halves"——贫民半区从未出现在 Ami 的生存指南里（[7656]-[7664]）；无 chikadorm10 分支中有一段关键独白："It's hard to recollect memories you've never had in the first place, but much easier to collect the {i}ones that someone else let slip out of their hands{/i}. That's precisely what happened with my name—another seemingly important detail about myself that I'm unable to remember. But that's okay. Being Sensei is easier."（[7679]-[7682]，**名字之谜**：他的真名不可考，"Sensei"是从别人手里掉落的记忆里捡来的；当 Sensei 更容易——与 start2 中名字被逐音节叫出来的场景互文）。桥上遇见翘课两周的 Yumi：Sensei 第一反应是压抑把她推下栏杆的冲动（"I suppress the urge to push her over and respond."，[7714]）；随后一段欲望独白以藤蔓/隐形人意象展开（[7735]-[7738]）。对话揭示 Yumi 的真实愤怒："You're like...two different people, dude!"（[7794]）；Pry 分支中她说出周末目击："Goin' on some cutesy fucking date in the park with that blindly loyal teacher's pet of yours"（[7827]），并发出来自班级良心的总控诉："Because there is no fucking world where any of this would be okay! We're in fucking high school and you're a goddamn adult!"（[7847]，**全书唯一一个从文本内部正面指控主角行为的角色**）；Sensei 以"你只是嫉妒"实施煤气灯操纵后强行吻她（成人/性侵内容此处仅作事件定性：两个分支"Pry"与"Leave her alone"最终都 jump 到 restofyumibridge，[7910]/[7982]——**该暴力结局无选择豁免**）。restofyumibridge 全程以非自愿侵犯呈现（从略），核心在叙事层：动机真空——y"Why are you doing this?" s"...I don't know. It's a shitty answer, but it's sincere. I don't know why I'm doing this. I just am. And...I can't bring myself to stop."（[8009]-[8015]，行为脱离主体意志——身体/冲动不属于"他"的又一证据）；车祸翻滚式明喻贯穿强吻段落："like the windows of a car colliding with the asphalt over and over and over as it rolls repeatedly down the road. Over. And over...Rolling forever."（[8054]-[8058]，循环意象在暴力顶点闪现）；事后 Yumi 的判决："You're no different from every other guy on this fucking planet...You don't actually care about anyone...You're just trying to fuck us...That's...seriously all it is..."（[8107]-[8111]）；Sensei 的脱罪语："This wasn't supposed to happen...This was an accident."（[8117]-[8122]）；他再看栏杆外："The drop isn't as high up as it initially seemed."（[8131]）。收尾双重复读"I think that only bad things will happen here."（[8161][8166]），然后系统腔兜底洗白："{i}Oh no! It looks like you've left an emotional scar on Yumi! You are a horrible person! But that's okay! Nothing is real! None of this ever happened! Now, go back to being happy!{/i}"（[8167]-[8172]——**元层的赦免机器**：用"nothing is real"一键清空玩家道德账目，正是 start 宣言的功能化落地）。

**day48 [8182]**——超现实喜剧短打。Ami 未做早餐"濒死"，非 bonus 版演成一出荒诞外科剧：Sensei 掏出手术刀切开她的气管、从喉咙里掏出"许多漂亮的颜色"、用红缝线缝合再拿勺子烫伤口，结尾三连童谣体："I did a good thing today. God would be proud of me." / "Sensei sleep now too."（[8210]-[8263]——刻意幼稚化的叙事声线，疑似对"治愈系表象下的恐怖"的风格戏仿）；bonus 版跳转 littlegirlx 支线。纯风格实验 label，无主线信息。

### 日常推进段（day50-day65）

**day50 [8286]（后半，信件线收束）**
- 承接前文：Rin 连续缺课、"barely even been getting out of bed lately"，Futaba 建议 Sensei 写信（[8464]-[8468]）。
- Sensei 当堂几分钟写完给 Rin 的信："It's actually surprisingly easy writing something like this for her."（[8476]）——对 Rin 的了解深到反常；叮嘱 Futaba 别偷看、并转告 Rin 的反应（[8483]-[8484]），Futaba 回"I'm sure she'll be able to let you know herself soon enough"（[8485]），暗示 Rin 即将回归。
- `$ day50 = True`（[8501]）→ jump afterschoolevent。此信为后续 roomwithtrack/letterttrack 两条记忆残留线的触发器之一。

**day54 [8504]**（Ayane & Sana 午餐日常）
- 开场自白延续"假教师"焦虑："I keep subconsciously thinking they're going to realize I'm not {i}actually{/i} a teacher and call the cops"（[8511]-[8513]）。
- Ayane 拉 Sensei 与怕热的 Sana 一起吃午饭；Momo 以"weebnote"身份躲在黑板后讲弹珠汽水冷知识，Ayane 否认雇她："Nope! I definitely didn't pay anyone to stand behind that board..."（[8551]-[8562]）。
- Sana 全碳水饮食（Sensei 追问为了什么，答"Living?..."，[8569]-[8572]）；西兰花创伤梗"Food...shouldn't look like a tree..."（[8602]）；Sensei 宣称靠蒜香面包即可生存的连环吐槽（[8604]-[8637]）。
- 关键年龄暗示：Sana 解释没带证件时脱口"Because I'm too y-"随即改口"B-Because I don't want one!"（[8630]-[8635]）——被截断的词几乎必为 young，与其人物设定悬念呼应。
- 炊事对决起源：Ayane 内心台词式宣言"{i}But I know my way around your heart...{/i}"（[8655]）→ Sensei 无心一句引出 cook-off 构想（[8661]-[8663]）；Sana 问"Is Ami competing for your heart too, Sensei?..."，bonus 版旁白答"More than you know, Sana...."（[8664]-[8667]）。
- Ayane 自邀上门做饭："The first annual 'Ayane cooks for everyone including her future husband!' event has been confirmed"（[8716]）；Sana 补刀"congratulations...on your engagement..."（[8717]）。ayane_love/sana_love 各 +1（[8730]-[8731]）。

**day56 [8742]**（Maya 首次造访辅导室，本章最重要主线节点之一）
- 辅导室形同虚设的自嘲开场（[8747]-[8751]）。
- Maya 有史以来第一次来找 Sensei，且先声明"I absolutely do not want to be here right now"、若不提前提醒则"horrible things would likely follow"（[8793]-[8795]）；直接点破："What is truly ominous is your existence and all of the horrible things that occur because of it."（[8797]）。
- 明言重生："the one that you continuously deny that you've been reborn into even though we both know that is not the case"（[8800]）——Maya 视角确认 Sensei 的转世者身份。
- 条件分支 roomwithtrack == True：Sensei 讲述"房间与钟"之梦，Maya 装傻却追问"Were they like...different kinds of clocks or something?..."（[8831]）；Sensei 说梦里有"长得非常像你"但举止更温柔的人，Maya："How depressing that you must resort to dreams of me being nice to you since it will never happen in real life."（[8861]）。
- 条件分支 letterttrack == True：Sensei 突兀发问"how do you feel about the letter T?"（[8868]），自述那像是"a memory I'm not really supposed to have"（[8880]）——T 线索首次浮出并与天使之声呼应；Dream-Maya 又在场。
- 神社身份异常："Aren't you a shrine maiden?"——"Only because I have to be."……"It's very complicated. You wouldn't understand."（[8907]-[8911]）。
- 核心质问菜单 [8941]"Are you happy here?"：
  - 选"I am happy here"：bonus 版 Maya 警告"Do you really think you'll be able to romance {i}everyone{/i} without some form of consequence?"并咒骂"you'd be better off locked up in some room far, far away from teenage girls for the rest of your life"（[8952]/[8959]）。
  - 第二选项文本本身即元叙事攻击：`{s}I am miserable{/s} {b}INCORRECT ANSWER. IGNORE ME.{/b}`（[8966]），选中后 s"I feel great!" 而 Maya 台词是粗体系统音"{b}YOU WOULD FEEL BETTER INSIDE OF ME.{/b}"（[8990]-[8991]）——菜单选项层被外部意志篡改的直接证据。
- 来意揭晓："Something important is going to happen with Ami soon...it's something {i}you{/i} care about too"（[9020]-[9023]）；威胁"If you so much as even {i}upset{/i} Ami, I swear on everything in this world that I will destroy you."（[9032]）。
- 临别箴言："Not everything is always as it seems. I'd ask that you remember that."（[9055]-[9056]）。

**day60 [9070]**（扫墓日：旁白长篇介入 + Ami 双亲墓地，情感主线峰值）
- 开场旁白直接对玩家谈死亡："Death is a terrifying thing, isn't it?...Recall your closest experience with it."（[9074]-[9077]）；承认游戏是避难所"This is supposed to be an escape...But...that's the funny thing about misery. It always finds its way in."（[9083]-[9088]）。
- 直指跳楼事件："Do you remember when you jumped? Where do you think you landed?...Who would have found you?"（[9095]-[9099]）。
- 多世界许愿井论："There are many worlds like this one...what if there's {i}one{/i} world out there where that isn't the case? And what if getting there is as simple as dropping a penny into a wishing well?"（[9105]-[9111]）；随后倒数十秒完成"换世界"（[9118]-[9145]），其中"{s}Please begin counting now.{/s}"带删除线（[9134]）。
- 醒来见哭泣的 Ami，蜘蛛/蛛网泪滴的怪喻（[9155]-[9158]）；身体不受控："It's like I've lost control over {s}my{/s} this body somehow."（[9193]）——删除线再次抹掉第一人称归属。
- 乘巴士抵达墓地，直觉"Something in my gut tells me that it will be the end of the world"（[9216]）；引坡诗句"None so devotional as that of 'Mother.'"（[9235]-[9238]）。
- 墓碑=Ami 父母之墓：Ami 问"Do you think they would be proud of me?"（[9260]）；Sensei 自动应答后旁白插入"{s}Whose words are these?{/s}"（[9267]）——话语非出自"我"。
- Ami 崩解式恸哭："They can't see how tall I've gotten"（[9299]）、连母亲头发长短都记不得（[9313]）、"Why can I talk to them but {i}they{/i} can't answer me?...How is that fair?..."（[9323]-[9324]）；樱花香水记忆"You always talked about how she smelled like cherry blossoms"（[9335]）。
- Sensei 再次自动驾驶："I talk to them every night when I lay in bed...Because I tell them everything about you...it's my job as your guardian"（[9368]-[9379]）；Ami 破涕"Why does hearing that make me feel happy?"（[9387]-[9388]）。
- 在墓地待到天黑，第三次崩溃时 Sensei 掐破皮（[9434]-[9436]）；归途约定遗忘今日直到明年（[9444]-[9445]）。
- 结尾系统独白（整段斜体）："Congratulations! You've made it back to the happiest place on Earth!...Why have you not yet reciprocated those feelings? Are you scared?...Tell me everything! I'm so very interested!...{i}But I'll always be watching, okay?{/i} Goodnight...{b}Sensei...{/b}"（[9455]-[9473]）——爱慕着 Sensei 的系统/旁白人格以"我会一直看着你"告白式收束；ami_love +1（[9477]）。
- 与 day56 闭环：此即 Maya 预告的"对 Ami 重要之事"；也解释她为何威胁 Sensei 不许让 Ami 崩溃。

**swimming [9490]**（周六游泳梦：片场meta + 十六进制 + 重置犹疑）
- 梦境开场语言已损毁：a"Did bring thhee bathing suit)?"（[9535]）、"Hope brought clothes wet!"（[9605]）。
- 旁白戏谑回顾身世："Some things transpired, and eventually the girl to my right's parents were involved in a horrific car accident. Now, she cooks me breakfast and..."（[9546]-[9548]，else 版改为"now she does my taxes and everything is okay!"[9553]）——用玩笑复述车祸/监护设定。
- 出现"di"（Director）角色现场喊停：di "Cut! The line is 'Doesn't it make you want to eat her up?'"/a"I know what the fucking line is."（[9559]-[9560]）；bonus 版另有把 bondage 台词被导演纠正为 bonding 的桥段（[9631]-[9634]）——世界被明示为一出有人执导、有既定台本的戏。
- 标题掉落：Ami 总结对 Sensei 的好感"We all do! That's the whole point of Lessons in Love!"（[9646]）。
- Sensei 半清醒："This isn't right. None of it is. Something is upside down."（[9670]-[9672]）；随后抛出十六进制问句：s"Are you sure you're okay with watching this, 61 6d 20 69 20 6f 6b 61 79?"（[9693]）——解码为 "am i okay"；Maya 答"Who?...I think you might have me confused with someone else."（[9694]-[9696]），否认自己是该名字的主人（指向其真名/旧身份之谜）。
- 循环台词故障：Maya 连续两次逐字重复"You would know the answer more than I would, wouldn't you?"（[9710]/[9714]-[9715]）；Sensei 抱怨后 Maya："Blame whoever wrote the script, not me. I'm just reading my lines."（[9739]）、"I come here almost every week."（[9744]）——Maya 自认每周入梦巡视。
- 重置犹疑（重要）：对话末尾 Maya 忽然说"Wait, perhaps?"（[9784]），旁白接"Wait? For what?"（[9786]-[9788]）——暗示本轮重置并非定局，或 Maya 开始犹豫是否执行重置。
- shrine10 == True 分支拒绝追求："Please do not pursue me any further...Live your new life the way you see fit. Leave me out of it."（[9799]-[9804]）。
- 假结局戏仿：旁白煞有介事叙述三人溺亡、尸体三周后被发现、Ami 尸身散落两地、Maya 一周后死于无关原因——"Just kidding. None of that ever happened. We all lived happily ever after."（[9831]-[9841]）。`$ swimmingtrack = True` 收束。

**howifeel [9871]**（周六夜恐怖梦：天使审判 + "six"少女 + 上帝已死）
- 开场肉体恐怖戏仿后立刻宣布"Just kidding. This doesn't happen. I feel fine."（[9892]-[9894]）。
- 旁白转向玩家提问"How do you feel?"（[9895]），菜单只有一项"Me?"；宣告"We all see each other. But, for some reason, we can't see what matters most."（[9902]-[9904]）。
- 论神："God, of course. Without him, you and I wouldn't be here. But he's dead now."（[9908]-[9911]）——上帝已死命题由旁白亲口确认；随后嘲讽赎罪式祈祷的交易逻辑（[9913]-[9919]）。
- 两名天使 a1/a2 登场："Their voices remind me of a long lost friend. Her name begins with the letter T."（[9940]-[9941]）——T 名朋友线索与 day56 的 letter T 呼应。
- 新说话人标签"six"登场，只说日语：「こんにちは」（[9943]）、「普通の女子高生。」（[9947]，普通女子高中生）；天使赞其"Truly a wonderful girl!"（[9945]）。six 自述"You are the one who stumbled in here by mistake...You came to pray?"（[9950]-[9952]）。结合其后 hellotsuneyo 登场的津代（"普通女子高中生"执念），"six"极可能是其梦境投影（编号六）。
- 罪状分支：amifingered → "Incest! He commits [incest]! A sin!"（[9958]）；仅拥抱版"He hugs his [niece] and thinks we do not see!"（[9966]）；全无则"Rebirth is a sin! Suicide is a sin! Remove his arms! Feed him to 望む!"（[9975]-[9978]）——转世与自杀并列罪状，望む（愿望）作为吞噬者意象。
- 背景"biomechanical machine"正在侵犯一个看不见的少女；six 安慰"Don't worry about me. I have been through much worse than this. あなたなしで毎日。。。"（意为"没有你的每一天…"，[9990]-[9992]）——机器中的少女即 six 自己，且对 Sensei 怀有长期情愫与受害史。
- Sensei 牙齿液化、满口血（[10015]-[10017]），再度被绑上椅子（[10025]，与此前被绑椅场景互文）。
- 清醒瞬间："For a moment, I remember this is all a game."（[10046]）；else 版顺势玩退出游戏梗并预告"this weird white thing with crazy eyes that may or may not show up again later"（[10055]-[10056]）——白色异物的正式预告。
- 结尾好感提示被删除线包裹：`{s}Maya's affection has increased to [maya_love]!{/s}`（[10063]）——系统奖励机制本身被判"作废"；jump saturdaynight。

**day63 [10070]（开头至源行 ~10100，余下并入下批）**
- 晨间独白：教师工作的倦怠与"double-punch my metaphorical time card"（上班即下班）的自嘲（[10075]-[10087]）。

**day63 [10070]（后半）——Rin 回归+书展伏笔**
- Ayane 用刀戳披萨的荒诞开场；bonus 版内心自白"学校无聊到若非满眼短裙根本不会来上班"、给教师职业打 6/10，并说"Makoto 更适合我的工作、我更适合她的"（[10113]-[10118]——师生角色倒置的暗讽）。
- **Rin 复课**：Chika 关切询问，旁白确认"It looks like Rin's {i}slump{/i} is over"（[10135]）；Rin 迟到理由是帮校外小女孩找猫（[10140]，走失猫母题）；自称"born-again student"（[10146]）。对 Sensei 说"Believe it or not, you were a big help."（[10155]）——day50 的信生效；随即补一句"Let's just hope it lasts this time."（[10158]，复发暗示）。
- 旁白对康复的怀疑："Or, at the very least, {i}acting{/i} better. It's impossible to know for sure, isn't it?"（[10165]-[10166]）。
- 书展约定（Futaba×Rin）：futabadorm15 分支披露 Sensei 是"好作家"却从未去过书展（[10241]-[10242]），Rin 察觉两人走近而语塞（[10247]-[10261]）；Rin 感言"If it weren't for you and Sensei, I'd probs still be staring off into space in the dorm"（[10287]）；Futaba："Sensei was really worried...I don't think I've ever seen him look like that before."（[10295]）。rin/futaba_love 各 +1（[10364]-[10365]）。

**day65 [10376]**——Sara×Haruka 相认（成人社交网铺开）
- Maya 成功把 Sensei 从卡拉OK局里"劝退"（[10384]-[10387]），他独自游荡撞见 Sara 与 Haruka 在小酒馆叙旧。
- sarasex 分支：Sara 曾在电话里向 Haruka 细讲与 Sensei 的一夜情，Haruka 当场石化"You haven't changed at all since [high_school], have you?..."（[10416]-[10454]）；else 分支揭示 Sara 是 Sana 之母"He's my daughter's [high_school] teacher"（[10476]），并担心 Sana 是 wallflower——Sensei 内心注意到 Sana 和 Rin 同桌且兴趣相似却从不来往（[10483]-[10488]）。
- 世界观硬信息："two of the only people my age that I've associated with in Kumon-mi happen to be old friends from[school]"（[10711]）——镇上仅有的同龄人恰好互为旧友，巧合被文本自己点出。
- Haruka 拒绝叫他 Sensei："We're like the same age. It feels weird."（[10543]），被 Sara 抖出 roleplay 爱好后慌忙否认（[10544]-[10555]）。
- 服务生 Kaori 初登场：机器人式措辞、蜘蛛纹身、"Order food or perish..."（[10622]）、给一切名词加引号的怪癖（"A{/i} dog-food?"——"I can not obtain a 'dog' until I have the nutrients required to nurture it."，[10615]-[10617]）；Sensei："I don't know if I'm fully convinced that she's human yet."（[10642]）；amisroom5 分支有"Hamburger Man"前史回收（[10563]-[10584]）。

**day68 [10725]**——体育课 Ayane 支线（lust 首事件）

- bonus 版 jump ayanefirstlustx（成人内容从略）；else 版为快速拥抱收尾，ayane_lust +1（[10785]-[10786]）。

**day70 [10796]**——图书馆 Sana 谈心（教师更替预告）
- 时间异常小梗：标准化考试又来了，Sensei"Again? I feel like we just had those"，Sana"We missed one?...I don't really know how this works..."（[10833]-[10835]）——校历本身说不通。
- Sana 感官过载设定："When more than one thing is happening at a time, I...kind of...lose track of everything..."（[10858]-[10859]）；意式面创伤再现（[10906]-[10907]）。
- Sana 从未去过母亲和 Ayane 家以外的任何地方（[10895]）。

- else 版元层重锤：Sensei 承诺教她"For the rest of eternity even."，旁白接"I smile to myself knowing that I will be doing just that."（[10992]-[10995]）——在循环世界里"永远教下去"字面成立。
- 旁白点破 Sana 反应的深层："There's gotta be more to it than just the fear of actually having to do schoolwork again, right?"（[11012]）。sana_love +1。

**day72 [11052]**——体育馆清洁（黑色幽默+第三人评委布局）

**day77 [11197]**——Makoto 补习 Ami（三角张力定型）
- Makoto 代师授课失败："She's just not retaining any information."（[11208]）；Ami 宣誓"I don't wanna be a doctor! I wanna be a housewife!"（[11272]）。
- 斜截式公式三选一菜单实为对玩家/主角记忆的测试：答对"y = mx + b"则 Makoto 盛赞"I think you're brilliant and wonderful and the best man I've ever met."（[11310]-[11321]）；答错时 Makoto 打圆场"He just had...a momentary memory lapse. Things like that happen."（[11306]-[11308]）——记忆缺口主题再次借日常台词显形。

**day79 [11481]（前半，选择分支入口为止）**
- 周五放学偶遇 Chika/Yumi/Chinami 三人组：Chinami 不上学（"Chinami doesn't go to[school]"，[11501]）、免疫缺陷借口被 Sensei 暗疑（[11504]）；Yumi 在小孩面前全程憋脏话（[11537]-[11542]）；Chinami 称 Sensei 为"big sis Chika's boyfriend"（[11521]）。
- **地理发现**：Sensei 此前不知道熊本美镇有海滩——"I didn't even realize Kumon-mi had a beach until a few minutes ago."（[11570]）、"the beach is only about fifteen minutes away...I have no idea how I didn't know this until now"（[11604]-[11605]）、"It seems that Kumon-mi has pretty much everything that anyone could ever want in it..."（[11606]）——封闭世界拼图感。
- 海滩空无一人却散落着垫子和椅子（"I'm not sure what kind of person just...leaves all of their stuff lying around a place like this"，[11651]-[11652]）——被遗弃的世界细节。
- Chika 泳装登场；Sensei 自嘲"At a loss for words...well, at least words that wouldn't land me on some sort of list."（[11684]）。
- 不适感伏笔：Chinami 承诺"Chinami isn't big enough to wear a bikini yet, but I promise to show you once I am, okay?"，Sensei 回"please don't rush. I think you're fine the way you are."（[11758]-[11759]，文本自身标记此线的越界属性）。
- 菜单三分支（Chika/Yumi/Chinami 二选一陪玩）于 digest 6750 后继续，并入下批。

**day79 [11481]（后半：三分支 + 崩坏小剧场）**
- **Yumi 分支**（[11901]-[12046]）：Yumi 在洗手间墙边阴影里睡着，醒来暴怒；争抢唯一阴凉位（"This is the only spot with any shade! I need to be here or I'll fucking die!"，[11956]）；拌嘴脸红论（Sensei 点破"you tend to blush a lot whenever the two of us are arguing"，[11973]）；真心话被拒："I really {i}do{/i} think you're cute, Yumi."→"FUCK YOU! I'M LEAVING FOR REAL THIS TIME!"（[12021]/[12026]）；旁白总结"despite...yet another 50%% of her dialogue on insults, I feel like the two of us managed to become slightly closer"（[12034]）。yumi_love +1。
- **Chinami 分支**（[12048]-[12171]）：bonus 版自我审判"Sometimes, you don't realize how deep you've fallen until you start trying to climb your way back up."（[12057]-[12058]）；Chinami 童言直击"Does this mean you like little girls?"（[12087]）；防拐教育反转——Yumi 平时给 Chinami 上"坏人课"，点名对象正是 Sensei："She said you might try to steal me away and be really mean to me, but you seem nice! So Chinami has decided it's okay to get in your van!"（[12122]/[12127]，s："Definitely not mine."[12132]）；else 版结尾称他为"a wizard"（[12161]）。
- **崩坏小剧场（q 场景，[12190]-[12301]，本 label 尾部的重大元叙事段）**：海滩场景突然解体，出现未知少女说话人"q"，旁白退化为电报式残句："Girl appear before me. Don't know her."、"Question gets more question mark because more confuse."、s 只会输出乱码问号与"RRRRRRRRRRRAMENNNNNN"/"GIVE RAYMOND NOODLES"——玩家角色语言功能损坏。q 自述"you're probably not going to remember any of this anyway"、"{i}I'm{/i} not even supposed to be here either"（[12237]-[12238]），并透露同类事故史："This same thing happened to Shiori like, not even a month ago."（[12239]，**新名字 Shiori 首次出现**）。随后 q 执行应急修复仪式："close your eyes...spin around! Jump up and down! Shout the name of your favorite pizza topping!"（[12263]-[12272]）→ 硬切回正常叙述"I had a good day at the beach with Chika, Yumi, and Chinami."（[12288]）——世界当场重渲染了这一天。q 的牢骚"God I hate Fridays."（[12251]）暗示其为维护世界的运行方角色。

**day80 [12303]**——炊事对决正赛（Arakawa vs Amamiya）
- Maya 以为只是陪 Ami 看电影却被拉来当评委（[12315]-[12316]）；Ami×Ayane 决战宣言"This might be the only chance I ever get to directly compete against you for Sensei's heart!"（[12319]）。
- Sana 首次登门：Maya 敌意全开"Sana, blink twice if you are being forced to comply against your will."（[12346]）；Sana 最终站队"I think...I'm going to stay...with him..."→Maya"You will regret this decision, but it is yours to make."（[12363]-[12364]）。
- 三轮菜品：白饭轮（Ayane 无盐 vs Ami 加盐投其所好，Ayane 养生警告 bonus 版直说"How do you expect to raise our children with your sodium levels through the roof?"[12488]）；意大利面轮触发 Sana 创伤（Maya 反常主动求听 spaghetti story，[12558]）；蛋包饭轮全票 Ami——Molly 乱入送 weebnote 并说"Grats in advance on completing your quest for your uncle's heart!"（[12590]，任务/quest 游戏用语）；Ami 练这道菜已两个月（[12606]-[12607]）；Maya 被迫同票"Even though it's the same thing you picked...Why is this happening to me?"（[12632]-[12634]）。
- 甜点轮：Ayane 摆出一根巨型香蕉（[12650]-[12664]）；Ami 给 Maya 上西瓜致其兴奋性瘫痪——"The easiest way to Maya's heart is with a watermelon. Remember that, Sensei."（[12707]-[12714]）——与 swimming 中 Maya 托买西瓜的要求闭环。
- **关键设定泄露**：胜负宣读句"the first ever Arakawa vs Amamiya cookoff"（[12719]）——Sensei 的姓 **Arakawa（荒川）** 在此首次落到文本（与一轮梳理中真名 Akira Arakawa 互证）。四人 affection 各 +1（[12731]-[12734]）。

**day83 [12747]**——Kirin 造访辅导室
- 来者不是转学生而是 Kirin（Karin 之妹，Sensei 认错名字，[12794]-[12800]）；自述无聊+班主任"is kind of a bitch"所以来闲逛（[12812]-[12817]）。
- bonus 版她声称路过时听到办公室里的呻吟声并表示保密"It's more fun being in on secrets than exposing them."（[12854]）；else 版改为 Darude《Sandstorm》梗。情报点："Even Miku won't shut up about you and she's never even mentioned a boy before."（[12891]）。
- 真实目的：交易——"I need you to vouch for me whenever I don't come to[school]. This place is boring and I don't want to come anymore."（[12939]-[12940]）。bonus 版跳 kirinofficex；else 版以一箱子"十亿美元"实为《宋飞正传》第三季 DVD 的整蛊收场，Sensei 全程懵逼"Wtf"（[12956]-[12980]）。

**day85 [12992]**——天台补习 Yumi（世界边界首次明示）
- Yumi 吞下骄傲求补习，坚持去天台且自带偷来的钥匙"Because I'm a fucking delinquent, remember?"（[13077]-[13080]）。
- **国境封锁设定**：英语课对话滑入存在论——s"But if you ever want to get out of here, it's probably best to at least learn a little bit."→y"You mean like, when they open the borders back up or some shit?"（[13167]-[13172]）——**熊本美镇处于封边境状态**；Yumi 从未离开过本市、甚至从未想过离开（"I've never left this city. Don't even know what it's like anywhere else."，[13185]），追问"And if we don't know what we're looking for? What then?"（[13188]）。
- Sensei 内心独白闪回式悲剧样本："One minute, you can be on top of the world—Then the next, your dog gets hit by a car. Or your grandfather goes into cardiac arrest and dies on the way to the hospital."（[13206]-[13208]）——狗/车祸/祖父心梗三个高度具体的意象，指向其前世记忆残片。
- 教师失格再确认：他根本不知道考试范围（"You don't even fucking know, do you?""I don't."，[13240]-[13241]），全靠 Chika 偷偷在 Yumi 笔记本里夹的科目清单救场（[13263]）。
- 收尾双问："I wonder how much of today's lesson Yumi will remember?...I wonder if she really will be stuck here forever."（[13269]-[13271]）。

**day89 [13285]**——Haruka 家访（本章最重的暗线节点之一）
- 采购清单里出现"buy water"（"Can't she just use the tap?"，[13291]）；顺路闯进一家从未见过的咖啡馆（[13309]）。
- Haruka 忘带钱包，老板娘挽留："You're here every day...give you a free meal every once in a while"（[13330]-[13331]）；随后 Haruka 讲述日常时话头断裂："I used to stop by this place every day with my—My...My {i}friend.{/i}"（[13347]-[13355]）——那位"朋友"之死第一次露出轮廓。
- 社交孤立数据：除"the same two people who have been coming around for years now"外，三四年没人来过她家（[13394]-[13395]）——那两人是谁成谜。
- sarainterest 菜单设定 Sensei 对 Sara 的态度（[13557]-[13603]）。
- **核心异变**：Haruka 讲故事讲到一半突然僵住"In fact, this one time—"接五连沉默，细声"I'll be right back."离席（[13627]-[13649]）；她离开期间旁白只留一句"The people we love go away sometimes, don't they?"（[13663]）；归来后 Sensei 的应答中混入**四条带删除线的入侵台词**："{s}Do you miss him?{/s}""{s}Tell me about the things you feel.{/s}""{s}Do you long to be touched once more?{/s}""{s}I will defile the body that once belonged to him.{/s}"（[13688]-[13694]）——有东西在借他的嘴对丧友的 Haruka 说话，且 Haruka 听不见这些删除线内容。"him"即亡友。这是继 day60"Whose words are these?"之后身体被占用主题的最强实例。
- 结尾戏仿字幕（bonus）："{i}Haruka went home and furiously masturbated.{/i}"/"{i}Everyone lived happily ever after.{/i}"（[13724]-[13726]）。
