**day102 [14415] + chooseyourartist/correctartist [14443]/[14455]**——旁白创世神话（本章元叙事顶点）
- 黑屏说书："Let me tell you a story. A long time ago, before you and I existed, there was a great, big ball of light floating through the universe. Let's call him Nozomu."（[14425]-[14427]）——神的名字 **Nozomu=望む（愿望/渴望）**，与 howifeel 的"Feed him to 望む!"闭环：这个世界的神即"愿望"本身。
- 梵高三选一菜单测试玩家记忆（答错重来，[14443]-[14453]）。correctartist 续讲：Nozomu 学会尖叫、发现凭空造物之力；bonus 版是亵渎版创世（造人只为替代自渎、发明语言禁令"Thou shalt not fuck each other anymore!"无人听从遂生万国语言，[14460]-[14487]）；else 版用海绵宝宝铅笔/Toaster Strudel 童话平行替换。
- 死亡预告式感叹："it feels like we're approaching death so quickly that we may not even live to see our children's seventh birthday. Life is a horrible thing. And we owe it all to Nozomu."（[14505]-[14507]）。
- 宣告："that horny light creature is actually who we call God now"（[14510]）→"God is dead. And he will never be back."（[14523]-[14524]）。
- **对玩家的邀约**："So it is up to you and I to write a new world...Come. Rewrite it all with me. We will fix this world together. Just you and me. The way it's always been. The way it always will be. {i}I love you so much.{/i}"（[14525]-[14537]）——叙述者向玩家求爱并提议共同改写世界，第一章meta线的最高点。

**mayadreamhj [14542] + happyloop1-4 [14573]-[14708] + ladvance 系列 + anotherhappyday [14810]**——空世界循环
- 标签名直白（bonus 版跳成人场景）；else 醒来后世界已空转进入 happyloop1。
- 循环结构：四层地点菜单（咖啡厅/图书馆/足球场/Ami房→街道/商场/神社/道场→酒吧/商店/宿舍），每处都是"No one is there. Time passes."；"Call someone"选项一律回复"The world is broken."（[14617]等）。
- 特殊出口：足球场在 connecttrack==False 且 soccer10==True 时跳 everythingisconnected（[14595]-[14596]）；Ami 房选项下亦藏 jump day103（[14614]）。
- 神社可祈祷刷好感度：`$ god_love += 1`→"{i}Your affection with GOD has increased to [god_love]!{/i}"（[14648]-[14650]）——给死神刷好感的黑色系统。
- 就寝句自我篡改："{s}Being around teenagers is exhausting{/s} Today was certainly an eventful day."（[14713]，真话被划掉换成场面话）。
- ladvancetomon-sun 六个推进器逐日 `totaldays += 1` 并跳 anotherhappyday；校验失败输出"ERROR ADVANCING TO MONDAY"等引擎报错文本（[14748]等）——世界以裸代码方式运转；anotherhappyday 显示"{i}[totaldays] Days have passed...{/i}"回到 happyloop1，形成无限一日循环。

**day103 [14819]**——全员消失 + 白色巨物 + 重逢 Maya
- 强颜欢笑叙事："everyone I know seems to have vanished. How strange! I'm sure they'll all be back soon enough."（[14834]-[14836]）。
- Ami 以损坏渲染体回归：超大字号"HOW THE FUCK ARE YOU DOING???"（[14852]）、"{s}It's so dark{/s} Heheh~ Did you miss me?"（[14855]）、会飘浮；Sensei 描述她"Your body is bent into an incredibly strange shape and you have text all over you"（[14896]）——角色意识到自己只是被错误渲染的模型。
- 双关硬信息："If this were a normal day, I would be run over by a car {s}like her parents{/s}"（[14912]）——**Ami 父母死于车祸在此以删除线顺笔确认**。
- 教室问候课桌、时间跳跃异常（"Is class really over already?"，按 day 变量判断是否周末，[14971]-[14982]）。
- 白色巨物"ho"登场："HELLO AGAIN. IT SEEMS THAT I AM IN {b}YOUR{/b} WORLD THIS TIME"（[14992]-[14993]）；自称 I KNOW EVERYTHING 却拒答（"YOU SEEK AN ANSWER YOU MUST FIND YOURSELF"），给出唯一线索："THE GIRL WITH THE BELL...SHE HAS BEEN RIGHT NEXT TO YOU THIS WHOLE TIME. SHE HAS SEEN YOU"，并喊出"FOLLOW YOUR HEART!!! TO THE TOP OF THE UNIVERSE!!! LOOK UP!!!"（[15016]-[15029]）——即 howifeel 里预告的"crazy eyes 白色东西"。
- 登顶：剧痛发作、天空星系交汇（[15039]-[15054]）；旁白呼吸母题："The higher up you go, the harder it is to breathe...But you and I can not breathe together. So what is even the point?"（[15055]-[15060]）。
- 屋顶上铃铛少女=Maya 等多时："You made it."（[15047]），允诺有问必答。

**resetquestions [15088]**——四问菜单（世界观总说明）
- 「我们在哪」：m"We're in Kumon-mi. The same place you were reborn [totaldays] days ago."（[15092]）；世界主观性演说："Your Kumon-mi is different from mine. And mine is different from Ami's. But they're still visions of the same Kumon-mi no matter whose eyes you're looking at them with."（[15098]-[15100]）。
- 「大家去哪了」：m"I don't know where they went. It's the same every time this happens."→s"Every time? This has happened before?"→m"Far too often, if you ask me...at this point, it's inevitable."（[15128]-[15136]）；埋钩子："Isn't there something {i}more{/i} important to you?"……"You can't miss something at all if you don't know that you're supposed to miss it."（[15143]/[15158]）。
- 「你是谁」：**全名公布"Maya Makinami. A normal teenage girl."**（[15171]）；经历次数"I've stopped counting."（[15177]）；否认神格："Of course I'm not god. I'm just a very unlucky girl. Nothing more...Nothing less."（[15183]-[15185]）。
- 「多少是真的」：m"Nothing is real. You already know that. You've been told time and time again."（[15197]-[15198]）；反问直指幕后："You've had your hand held through everything. I wonder who it is that's been holding it?"（[15203]-[15204]）；结句自我怀疑："Nothing is real. Nothing will ever be real. Or...At least that's what I keep telling myself."（[15215]-[15221]）。

**restofthereset [15230]（前半至源行 ~15341，余下并入下批）**
- Maya 宣布最爱环节"What comes next is my favorite part. The end of the world. Isn't it beautiful?"（[15235]-[15237]）。
- 身世暗示："Maybe everything about me {i}is{/i} some big secret? Maybe you're a little closer to the truth than you think?"（[15249]-[15250]）。
- 重置方案明示："We're going to reset the world. Well...{i}I'm{/i} going to reset the world. You're going to watch."（[15276]-[15278]）；权柄依据"This is our world. We can do whatever we want in it."（[15285]）；回应学年焦虑（[15291]-[15292]）。
- 进度保留承诺："If all goes according to plan, your progress won't reset at all."（[15309]-[15310]）；风险条款"Sometimes, plans fail and everything snaps."（[15332]）；众人应原样归来（[15333]）；bonus 版以"harem of misguided teenagers will emerge from the end of the world unscathed"讽刺收束该段（[15339]-[15341]）。

**restofthereset [15230]（后半）——重置执行与失忆重启**
- else 版玩笑拒绝语："My vision is too bad and you like circular fruits too much."（[15346]，西瓜梗）；Maya 宣示知情权"I know everything about you, whether you like it or not."（[15367]）。
- 重置需要拥抱：她抱住他的腰、脸贴胸口（[15379]-[15382]）；tsundere 名额已被占用梗（[15383]-[15384]）；"You're only saying that because the only other human being alive right now is you."（[15395]）。旁白刚起头"Wouldn't just holding my hand be enough to—"便被硬切（[15402]→[15427]）。
- **硬切即重置完成**：q（Ami）在教室叫醒他，他彻底失忆——"Who is this girl? Or, better question, who am {i}I?{/i} Why can't I remember anything?..."（[15451]-[15453]）。此段是 start2 的二周目镜像：由 Ami 口述重建设定（女校唯一男教师、含她在内十名学生、兼任辅导师、Ayane/Maya 是多年留宿常客、房间里有一本不许她看的"奇怪的书"=学生名册，[15659]-[15672]）。
- 双亲问题被硬性封锁："What about your parents?—"→"Ask something else."×2（[15679]-[15682]）。
- 战争设定注入："We're not allowed to leave the city limits anymore because of the war...Like 99%% of the men were drafted into the space army"（[15600]-[15607]）；Ami 立刻元层打补丁："It's an important aspect of the plot that is going to be used as comedic relief in the future, so don't try and focus too much on it"（[15615]）；Miku 被当作"别变成那样"的反例提及（[15610]）。规则一：不许碰学生，"既是法律也是校规"且只限校内（[15635]-[15642]）。

**amiwhattodo2 [15652]**——问答枢纽：学生情况可重复问；父母问题永远被拒。

**nextstart2 [15739]**——记忆回归
- Ami 拉他去打招呼才发现 Maya 一直独坐角落；四目相对瞬间记忆灌回："When our eyes meet, everything floods back into me."（[15788]-[15791]）。
- 定居宣言（本章核心独白）："There is no home for me anywhere else, so I will carve a hole inside of {i}this{/i} world and sleep inside of it, refusing to move out even if the world around me breaks. Because now I know that world will reset. And that nothing here is permanent. But...What I don't know is {i}how.{/i}"（[15792]-[15798]）；答案就坐在面前"pretends to hate me more than she actually does"，Maya 无声一句"{i}You're welcome...{/i}"（[15803]-[15808]）。
- 时间线跳跃确认：测试已经考完（[15836]-[15837]）、本学年才过几个月（[15850]）。
- 收束诗化记忆："I remember a rooftop. I remember a sky. And I remember the scent of watermelon shampoo as a small girl wrapped her arms around me and changed my life forever."（[15863]-[15867]）——**Maya 的气味印记=西瓜香波**，与 swimming 的买瓜请求、day80 的西瓜瘫痪三线合流。maya_love +=5（[15870]）。

**everythingisconnected [15886] + chooseahead/correcthead [15967]/[15984]**——足球场断头梦（黑色喜剧巅峰+meta劫持）
- 触发：happyloop 足球场分支。开场删除线前史自白："{s}When I was younger, I convinced myself that I'd be able to be an astronaut if I worked very hard.{/s}{s}This wasn't true.{/s}{s}In my rage, I took to doing horrible things as a hobby instead.{/s}"（[15894]-[15896]）＋"I know what the inside of a human body smells like...you could even mistake it for {b}HEAVEN.{/b}"（[15898]-[15899]）。
- 语言倒转故障："si enoyreve erehw rednow I""Everything is coming out backwards"（[15901]-[15904]）。
- 捡"假发"变捡头颅：头颅以花茎插地复活中（[15955]-[15959]），菜单列出 Kirin/Miku/Karin 三颗头；chooseahead 强制选 Miku，干扰项包括"K5r1n""m""Nothing is real"（[15968]-[15981]）。
- correcthead：成人版为亵尸直写、全年龄版为"{b}here comes the huggy boy{/b}"拥抱戏——反转是"这其实是西瓜"（[16026]-[16033]）；被 Maya 当场撞见（[16046]-[16056]）；s"?og yllamron ti seod woH"（倒写"How does it normally go?"，[16069]）；Maya："That's something I can't tell you just yet. You're starting to disconnect."……"Nothing I say will register in your memory. This {i}dream{/i} will be wiped away just like all of the others."（[16073]-[16077]）。
- 小屋（储物间）过渡：只有透过玻璃才能感到阳光"Why can't I feel anything directly? Why can't I feel?"（[16155]-[16158]）；点题："Everything is connected. That is something Maya taught me recently. But if everything is connected—Why do I feel so detached?"（[16164]-[16167]）。
- **假开发者通告劫持**："{i}Developer note: At this point in the game, all of your actions will be recorded via webcam and sent somewhere special.{/i}"→菜单"I accept"→"Thank you for accepting."；随后系统通知宣布进入"[FEMALE HUMAN BEING]"对话能力模拟测试、"////STOP MUSIC"、癫痫警告、"Thank you for playing Lessons in Love, an adult dating simulation game."（[16168]-[16187]）——游戏对玩家本人的直接戏弄。
- **跨作品串门**：醒来在糖果味的社团教室，遇到"少一个维度"的女孩（说话人 mg），她找的男孩名叫 **Yuu**（[16245]）；自称 **Saki**（[16326]），道别"Enjoy your trip back to Kumon-mi."（[16332]）——Yuu/Saki 正是一轮§10 悬念9 中 rainking 闪现名字组的成员，此处正式登场（开发者其他作品角色跨界）。`$ connecttrack = True`（[16346]）后跳回 happyloop1。

**day98 [16357]**——漫画社（Ami lust 线）
- 设定回溯：Sensei 是漫画社顾问却毫不知情（[16380]-[16393]）；Ami 支开社员只为在社里为他手淫（[16404]）。
- 婚姻疯话：bonus 版提议伪造结婚证书/假身份（[16480]）；**else 版竟答"It's not like we're related or anything."（[16482]）——全年龄版本公然否认叔侄关系**；旁白自省"She's been clingy since the get-go but talking about marriage might be a little...Insane?"（[16485]）。
- 成人内容从略；bonus 版 jump day98x。

**day86 [16556]**——医务室 Futaba
- Futaba 逃体育课躲进医务室；护士周五不上班（[16594]-[16600]，else 版"那有人受伤怎么办？""I guess they just...die?"）；bonus 版跳 futabanursex；else 版 Sensei 以荒诞效率当场招到新护士。futaba_lust +1。

**specialclassroom [16628]**——天堂之下的特殊教室
- 场景："situated somewhere underneath Heaven"，恶臭如常年发酵的脏衣却无阳光可达、入夜前一片黑暗（[16635]-[16638]）；双腿无法移动，低头发现根本没有形体："I am a shadow now. But whose shadow am I?"（[16641]-[16643]）。
- 那个总在他"{s}lost{/s} happy"时出现的少女现身（[16650]），用日语问「あなたは誰？」「だれ です か？」（[16654]-[16656]）。
- 梦中 Maya 的眼睛设定："Sometimes, Maya's eyes go away in my dreams. They're replaced with old TV static and memories of thousands of lives I have yet to live."（[16660]-[16661]）；点击鞋跟（绿野仙踪手势，[16673]）后 bonus 版跳 specialclassx，else 版宣告"The event ends. This is the wrong game."（[16680]-[16681]）。

**day110 [16690]（前半至源行 ~17007）**
- 开场"两条命"长文：第一条命（未成年）短暂却被理想化、被操纵如围巾御寒；第二条命拒绝被改变只能自我操纵，"Resistance is only a strength when we want it to be."（[16693]-[16709]）；人生清单 find a job/partner/family/die（[16708]-[16716]）；"Praise the {s}God{/s} god who wanted us to live these tragic, hopeless lives."（[16721]）。
- 脑内响起"reminds me of cursed birds and familiar sins"的歌（[16723]-[16727]）——跨作品的旋律引用。
- 重置后首个早晨：Ami 对重置一无所知，Sensei 直说"Maya reset the world for me."被当成新网游（[16815]/[16840]）；Ami 总结两人关系从"uncomfortable hate"变成"comfortable hate"（[16811]）。
- Maya 新戒律："Please don't say my name. It sounds disgusting when you do it."→"Then what am I supposed to call you?"→"Nothing."（[16957]-[16959]）。
- 走廊单独对质开启："Are we just going to ignore everything that happened the other day? The thing on the roof? The end of the world?"→长时间扫视走廊后 m"Yes."（[16996]-[17007]，余下并入下批）。

### 日常推进段（day110 后半—day130）

**day110 [16690] 后半——天台事件后的教室收尾**。Maya 对"世界末日谈话"的官方态度是回避："We {i}are{/i} going to ignore that. Please continue on with your daily life as if nothing has changed."（[17008]-[17009]）。但她随即留下两句信息量极大的话："Though, I would appreciate it if you'd make it to the roof much quicker in the future. It took you quite a while the last time."（[17010]-[17011]——她把"你上次花了很久才到天台"当作既定事实，暗示天台会面在循环中是反复发生的固定环节）；以及口是心非的"It's not as if I'm expecting to ever see you there again or anything."（[17013]）。临走补刀："The bell for second period is about to ring and I, too, do not want to be killed today."（[17021]——一个"也"字：被杀风险是两人共享的处境）。Sensei 内心消化："Maybe...Maya just doesn't want to talk about it during school hours?...It's not like that topic is something the two of us can avoid forever, though, is it?"（[17028]-[17029]）；而"最大的问题"竟以另一个名字落地："But, perhaps, the biggest question of all- ... Is if Makoto is going to let me live through the day to find all of those answers."（[17031]-[17033]——原文如此：此处 Makoto 疑为 Maya 之笔误，或有意混名；结合 day121 里正是 Makoto 开玩笑问出附身问题，此句无论正误都是不祥伏笔）。`$ day110 = True`（[17036]）。

**day114 [17045]——Kaori 边缘角色回（喜剧壳下的世界观投放）**。旁白开场直接讨论重置的能力边界："You'd think after resetting the world, Maya would have at least made it so the temperature isn't threatening to melt my insides every day. She mentioned being able to change things but...maybe something like the weather is out of her control?"（[17058]-[17059]，酷暑是重置世界的常量，天气或不在 Maya 权限内）。接着是纯游戏自觉："this also seems like a great chance to run into a side character. I wonder which one it will be this time?"（[17064]-[17065]，"这次会抽到哪个配角"）。Kaori 向披萨宣战（"This hot, circular cheese-and-tomato wheel is causing me anguish...Am I...allergic to shapes?"，[17083]-[17085]），坚持叫 Hamburger Man，并一本正经怀疑他干人口贩卖："None of the girls who have appeared beside you were ever seen again."（[17109]——玩笑外壳下是设定层的恐怖真相：那些女孩确实一个个"再没出现过"）；bonus 版旁白自嘲"Well, actually...it kind of doesn't."（[17114]，教师身份确实圆不上）。硬情报：她在约九家餐厅同时打工（[17152]）、22 岁（自称狗龄 154，[17225]-[17228]）、**从未上过高中**（"Would that be something I remembered if I did?...Then no. No high school."，[17241]-[17245]，青少年时代一片空白，只"大概去看过一次电影"[17248]——又一例记忆残缺居民）；"爱好"探索最终落在动物身上（[17200]-[17206]）。结尾误会式脸红交换电话号码（[17304]-[17345]），`$ kaorinumber = True`（[17367]）解锁通话涨好感系统。

**day120 [17377]——Rin×Sana 友情回**。Rin 忽然改口"Tall guy"，Sensei 抗议"It makes it sound like we don't know each other."（[17410]）。街道反常空旷："the streets {i}are{/i} eerily empty today...No, that can't be it. Both of them are wearing long sleeves. Where the hell is everyone?"（[17413]-[17416]，行人稀薄=重置后世界人口空洞的又一感知）。快餐小丑梗直接点破屏幕："He's got like, an actual name. I just don't think we're allowed to say it on-screen because of copyright stuff."（[17435]）；Sensei 崩溃"I am tired of being a hamburger-person. Take me out of this isekai right now."（[17444]，自称 isekai）。Sana 摸过 Rin 的头骨，Rin 记忆自动润色出哈姆雷特桥段又被 Sana 老实戳破（[17463]-[17474]）。Rin 险些自曝性向"There's a reason I'm so into-"急刹改口化妆（[17535]、[17554]），旁白注脚自带脚本残留符号："narrowly avoids revealing to her new friend that she's [[probably] sexually attracted to her"（[17563]）；Sana 被判"距对恋爱产生好奇心还有六百年"（[17564]）。师者元话语连发："Don't thank me yet, Sana. Your character arc has barely even started."（[17569]）；"We can't call it quits on your progression until you're friends with everyone."（[17575]）。离场口误："Good day, huma- Uhh...girls."（[17643]-[17644]，差点把她们称作 human）。rin_love/sana_love 各+1（[17663]-[17664]）。

**day121 [17677]——Makoto 天台回（日常层里暗线最重的节点之一）**。Sensei 忘带钱包，却被莫名冲动驱使上天台："I am suddenly overcome by a strong urge to try my luck on the rooftop...important characters always seem to be on the roof for some reason."（[17687]-[17688]）；门恰好未锁被归功于上帝（[17703]-[17704]，便利性的自我察觉）。旁白转述 Makoto 的孤独剪影，牢笼意象刺目："A diligent girl wraps her fingers around the chainlink before her, staring out at a city she isn't allowed to leave...Why try so hard if I can never escape?"（[17715]-[17718]——不被允许离开的城市，与后文边境封锁、Makoto 出国线同源）。对话滑入黑暗：她平静推测他或许怀有"hidden urge to throw yourself from the top of a building"（[17797]），再冷静补充日本自杀率数据（[17802]-[17803]），Sensei 内心失语"What the fuck is she talking about?"（[17807]）。随后是全章最锋利的一记玩笑："It seems as if you've felt compelled to do several things today...Perhaps I should be the one checking on {i}you{/i} instead? Your mind hasn't been taken over by someone else, has it? Hahaha~"（[17827]-[17833]——附身问题以玩笑形式精准命中）。旁白立刻替读者确认："I wouldn't be so sure of that if I were you, Makoto. In fact, it seems like the first person to notice was actually the one I'm {i}least{/i} close with. How's that for irony?"（[17846]-[17847]——第一个察觉异常者是与他关系最疏远的人，指向 day21 的 Yumi）。时间感异常："we've barely known each other for a year, but it feels like much longer."（[17853]）。全班成绩集体飙升："the last set of standardized tests were lifelong highs for almost everyone involved."（[17856]-[17857]——重置后世界数值整体上调的侧面证据）。"强项"问答：bonus 版答"Sex education."引发四连冷场省略号（[17871]-[17884]），else 版为合唱团与"{i}the incident{/i}"（[17874]，又一桩未命名旧事）。离开时 Makoto 锁门，且不知 Yumi 也持有钥匙（[17890]-[17892]）。makoto_love+1。

**day126 [17905]——Yumi 抢座回（桥梁事件余波）**。Yumi 罕见到校却抢走 Futaba 座位勒索午饭钱（[17910]-[17944]），羞辱步步升级。Sensei 介入即遭当众揭底："Last I remember, you were the one stalking {i}me{/i}. Isn't that right, asshole?"（[17979]），并预告"And that's just the beginning of it."（[17989]）；else 版追加神来之笔："He makes me juggle, too. Fucking asshole."（[17994]，Futaba 震惊"J-J-Juggle?!"——全年龄版把桥梁强吻改写成"逼我玩杂耍"，与 day128 的"juggling materials"互证：juggling 即审查替代叙事）。Futaba 追问时 Sensei 承认那是校外发生的事（[18059]-[18060]）。Yumi 反将一军："Bravo to you, dude. Completely ignoring your own problems and just pawnin' all of your bullshit off on me."（[18050]）后摔门而去（[18065]-[18067]）。Sensei 独白确认伤未愈合："I was beginning to think that Yumi had gotten over that...occurrence from a while ago, but it's clear to me now that this is most certainly not the case."（[18079]-[18080]）。

**day128 [18100]——Chika 强制和解局×Tojo Ramen 神秘女人**。开场旁白再谈重置气象学："I wonder if this whole {i}world resetting{/i} thing means that I'm going to be trapped inside perpetual-summer for the rest of eternity?"（[18109]-[18110]）。Chika 透露 Chinami 经常整日独守家中（[18135]、[18140]），并以"As long as she's not going to sleep alone, it's cool."（[18246]-[18247]）为由强行安排 Sensei 与 Yumi 单独晚餐；Yumi 两度惊觉挚友反常："What the fuck is happening to you?..."（[18249]）、"Do you really care about this guy that much?"（[18299]）。Tojo Ramen 女侍机械腔报幕："I will now return to the kitchen and pretend that I do not hear what you two talk about."（[18381]）。Yumi 摊牌链：咖啡店落聘（[18388]-[18393]）→"everything just feels fake as hell. You're a shit human."（[18419]-[18420]）→动机指控"You think I'm gonna fall for you? Is that what it is?"（[18422]-[18423]）→自贬式报价（bonus 版口交提议[18426]/else 版改为拥抱[18428]，且 else 版 Sensei 劝阻"You're allergic...You shouldn't."[18438]）→自我判决"I'm a loner. A degenerate. A fucking thief."（[18443]）→存在绝望"Life is fucking stupid. I just want everything to stop."（[18451]）→离席（[18462]）。**双版本关键对照**：同一地点回忆，bonus 版为"in some fucking ramen shop less than a mile away from where you stole my first kiss."（[18447]），else 版为"...where you made me buy all those juggling materials."（[18449]）；且事后独白 bonus 版问"Why {i}did{/i} I kiss her?"（[18472]），else 版竟是"{i}What{/i} juggling materials?"（[18474]）——"杂耍材料"连当事人都对不上号：审查改写在世界内部留下了记忆接缝，是 bonus/全年龄差异直接参与叙事的少数实例。随后皮夹克抽烟女人（q）坐进空位："Girl problems?"（[18486]），毒舌开局"You cheat on her or somethin'?"（[18490]）；else 版她自称通灵"I've got that clairvoyance shit or whatever is they call it. I kinda just know stuff."（[18515]）；生死观"We're all gonna fuckin' die someday. Might as well live it up while we still can."（[18522]）；唤女侍"Tsu-chan"（[18533]——hellotsuneyo 系列前置人物的首次露面）。两条忠告："Stick with your niece and don't ever have any kids of your own. Shit's not all it's made out to be."（[18557]-[18558]）与"This part of town ain't really the right place for people like you...Ain't nothin' good on this side of Kumon-mi."（[18565]、[18571]）。旁白以诀别笔触收束："Regardless, I wish her the best. Wherever the wind takes her."（[18583]-[18584]）。

**day130 [18596]——澡堂周六（短 label）**。全镇断水，深夜被迫去澡堂（[18601]-[18606]）；Ami/Maya/Ayane 留宿，一行四人（[18607]-[18608]）。bonus 版按 amifingered 分两套内心盘算（[18617]-[18624]）并 jump girlbathx（[18666]-[18667]）；else 版隔竹篱偷听无果："Oh well. Guess I'll never know."（[18671]-[18672]）。自我定位台词亦随版本切换：bonus 版"an idea that only the protagonist of an erotic game would have"（[18650]）/else 版"a dating sim"（[18652]）。`$ day130 = True`（[18675]）。

### 转学生篇（day138—day144）

**day138 [18684]——Ami 办公室回+转学生预告（新篇章发令枪）**。旁白自嘲开场："I'm entirely unqualified for virtually everything I do. Has no one in the staff really figured that out by now?"（[18691]-[18693]）；并埋黑色玩笑："If the[school] is able to hire new janitors every time Ayane destroys one, how come they can't hire a normal guidance counselor?"（[18690]，校工被 Ayane 反复"弄坏"的循环式招聘）。Ami 自报全名登场："I, Ami Arakawa, have come to your office..."（[18767]——Arakawa 姓再次由文本直接确认）。bonus 版按 ami_virgin 分支：非处女线直球提出办公桌性事（[18723]-[18733]）；处女线以渐弱假笑掩饰（"Hahahahah... Hahaha... Hah..."，[18745]-[18753]）。else 版藏了一个开发者彩蛋："Ugh...I already told you I have no idea how that subscription wound up on my bank statement. I don't even know who Komisari is."（[18760]——**Komisari 即本作开发组名**，被写进世界内当付款梗）。正题：校长注意到本班学生数明显偏少，将有**两名女生从 Watabe 老师班转入**（[18843]-[18844]、[18906]）；Watabe"closed-off"只肯透露部分情况（[18907]）。Makoto 被问及转学生是否知道他的教学法时反将一军："Are they aware of my...teaching methods?"→"Your original ones or your current ones?"（[18927]-[18928]——教学法的今昔之别由学生点破）；她包办全部手续并直言教职员"trust me {i}more{/i} than you"（[18946]）。Ami 恶搞独白双版本：bonus 版"a grown woman trapped in a [teenage]girl's body"/else 版"I never opened an accounting firm"（[18900]/[18902]，后者恶搞 Makoto 的个人履历）。结尾旁白的异常感知："Two new students out of virtually nowhere."（[18988]），随后两个状态位齐落：`$ day138 = True`、`$ ticktock = True`（[19001]-[19002]——**ticktock**：时钟滴答旗标启用，倒计时意象进入变量层）。

**day140 [19010]——Molly MacCormack 入班**。走廊初遇即元台词："So...{i}this{/i} is the place where wishes come true..."（[19095]，后自曝出处是动画[19138]）。旁白立刻察觉违和："Makoto says this girl isn't a {i}real{/i} transfer, but she definitely looks like one. At the very least, she most certainly isn't Japanese."（[19105]-[19106]，"转学"只是换班，但她看起来完全是外来者——爱尔兰人）。Molly 的存在论自白双版本：bonus"It is my dream to become the lead heroine of a harem, sir. I am prepared to put my body and soul on the line in pursuit of a love worth dying for."（[19150]-[19151]）/else"dating game"版（[19153]-[19154]）——**一个公开宣布自己要当 galgame 主角的角色**；附赠自评"Enough to ruin real-life relationships."（[19157]）。入班后径直走向 Rin 桌认亲："You dare look upon ME and feign ignorance?! How can you call yourself the commander of the undead with a backbone so {i}weak{/i}?"（[19260]-[19261]），Rin 死不承认"I've never even seen her before! I swear!"（[19258]）——两人有旧交而 Rin 否认（记忆/黑历史双重谜）。**最值得标记的是 Maya 的反应**：见 Molly 进班脱口而出"Oh dear god, no."、"Please no. Anything but that."、"I'll never have another peaceful morning again."（[19234]、[19236]、[19238]）；Ami 点破"You're just still mad about her calling you a nerd"，m"{i}I'm not a nerd.{/i}"（[19239]、[19244]）——Maya 对一个"刚出现"的角色怀有成型的过往记忆，是**循环残留/前周目共处史**的又一硬线索。Molly 单方面宣布 Sensei 为 master 并坐第一排（[19296]-[19321]）。molly_love 初始化+1（[19330]）。

**day142 [19341]——"How big is it?"误导喜剧+海滩旅行立项+Rin 的密谈**。全班反常凝视老师，Ayane 领衔" How big is it? We all want to know."（[19381]-[19385]），Futaba 自曝"I've seen it before so..."（[19419]-[19421]）把误会推向顶点，最终揭晓：说的是海边别墅的大小（[19468]）。机制情报：Ami 用学校留言板组织旅行并以暗语"educational field trip"避嫌（[19469]、[19499]）；Futaba 提供县内海滨旅馆情报，两三间房可挤下全员（[19512]-[19514]）——**beachvacation 系列在此正式立项**。床位争夺战预演：m"I refuse to sleep on the ground. I'll sleep in the bed with Ami and Ayane. Sensei can sleep on the bus."（[19527]-[19529]）；ay"{i}MY{/i} Sensei?...He's going to sleep in the bed with me."（[19533]-[19534]）；m"If he's actually going to be sleeping in the bed with you then yes, I will sleep on the bus."（[19535]——Maya 宁可睡巴士也不愿目睹床笫，醋意与回避并存）。Makoto 揽下订房："It's kind of like I'm becoming your secretary too~"（[19547]）。后半 Rin 单独拉他到走廊（[19579]），交底自己的药物与焦虑："being somewhere I'm not used to normally freaks me out a bit...if I start acting all...weird...Would you mind...maybe trying to calm me down a bit?"（[19609]-[19616]），方式是"sit with me and pat my head or something. Preferably away from everyone else."（[19622]）；并点名"Definitely Chika. Chika more than anyone...I'd never have a chance if she knew that side of me."（[19624]-[19625]）；chikadorm20 分支中他替 Chika 辩护，Rin 捻着毛衣线头酸溜溜收场："Sounds like...you're getting to know her pretty well, huh?..."（[19630]-[19636]）。rin_love+1（[19666]）。

**day144 [19677]+hellotsuneyo [19766]+hellotsuneyo2 [19782]——天台对峙喜剧与 Tsuneyo 登场**。Sensei 又是无意识地上了一天台："I don't know, I guess I kind of just wound up here without putting much thought into it."（[19683]-[19684]——天台对他的引力已成惯性）。发现沉默女孩后的**套娃菜单**是本章结构玩笑：Keep staring→Keep staring...→KEEP STARING!→Omfg 四层嵌套（[19708]-[19752]），任一层的 Say hello 都汇入 hellotsuneyo（[19755]等），hellotsuneyo 里被无视后又强制 jump 回 hellotsuneyo2（[19780]）——无论怎么选都殊途同归。期间一句冷冰冰的自我盘点："I've done much worse to different people and at least gotten reactions out of them."（[19749]）。hellotsuneyo2 正戏：姓名猜谜她答"Ramen."（[19785]）；报家门"Tojo Ramen. My family's restaurant. Old District. Thirteen minutes by bus."（[19812]，与 day128 的贫民半区地理互锁）；真名 **Tsuneyo Tojo**（[19820]），自评"Samurai of flavor."（[19823]）。人设速写：将继承父业"I plan to take over once my father passes"（[19843]）；父权管控"my father has forbidden me from associating with any male figure that has not received his blessing"（[20062]）、每日向父亲汇报行踪（[20070]-[20075]）；近期才入学"homeschooled until recently. But my father has become sick and now I must rely on Ms. Watabe."（[19983]-[19984]）；入读才几周故"don't know what is or isn't normal yet"（[19975]-[19976]），甚至没意识到 Sensei 是老师（[19964]-[19967]）；把 Molly 记成全名"Emerald Guardian of the Crystal Forest"（[20000]）。悲剧内核藏在笑话里："I have no friends. Just noodles."——Sensei 大笑夸其冷幽默，她纠正"That wasn't a joke..."（[20017]、[20028]-[20037]）。与 Ami 的错位社交：Ami 夸过她皮肤好，她当成求爱"我已经写好了拒信"（[20088]-[20097]），又托 Sensei 转告"tell Ami I think she has very nice legs...She looks soft."（[20105]-[20108]）。bonus 版旁白坦白动机"Of course I have ulterior motives. I pretty much {i}always{/i} have ulterior motives."（[19954]-[19956]），else 版反讽"You are right...I am once again very excited to teach."（[19957]-[19959]）。tsuneyo_love+1（[20135]）。

### 转学生收束与海滩度假·前半（day150—beachvacation2）

**（体例注：day139 的 label 位于脚本文件中 day154 之后，属反编译排序现象；由 day142 的 `if day139 == True` 判断可知其实际时序在 day142 前后浮动，下文按剧情逻辑归位。）**

**day150 [20146]——Yumi 道歉+双转学生入班**。开场制度性空洞再获实证：Sensei 自述"I've marked Yumi as present every day so far and no one's even questioned it. How would the staff even know?"（[20181]-[20182]，长期旷课无人核查）。else 版补上前文伏笔的答案——断水是 Yumi 干的："If you talk about vibes one more time I am going to turn off your water again."→"Wait, that was you?"→"Yeah."（[20198]-[20200]，与 day130 全镇断水互锁）。随后罕见的 Yumi 道歉："Sorry about freaking out on you the other day. I think the cafe thing got to me more than it should have."（[20209]），旁白元评"This counts as character growth, doesn't it?"（[20213]）；但一提到向 Futaba 道歉立即翻脸（[20225]-[20243]）。Molly 初见 Yumi：转述 Watabe 班对她的称呼"ghost delinquent"（[20261]），拜师学"dark arts"未遂被逐（[20291]-[20312]，"Leprechaun!"）。Tsuneyo 作为第二名转入生登场，报名字仍条件反射"Ah-"、籍贯仍答"Ramen."（[20381]、[20393]）；她主动求教"How do you introduce yourself...I would like to make a better impression this time around"（[20440]-[20449]，"请教我做一个更有趣的人"），得到建议"Just be yourself"。班级自我介绍名场面：开场白是"How does everyone feel about airline food?"（[20516]）；Ami 悄悄救场被旁白记为"That's "my" [niece], alright."（[20536]，**my 字带引号**——叔侄关系在叙述层再次被打引号）；披露幼年练过剑道（[20592]）；Molly 当场招募室友"I kinda can't move into the dorms unless I have someone to room with"（[20654]），两人火花四溅到全班起哄"Are they...going to kiss?"（[20667]）。molly_love/tsuneyo_love 各+1。

**day153 [20707]——帮 Molly 搬宿舍**。宿舍二楼窗台的小鸟支线引出 Maya 的毒舌护场："Being alone with you. That sounds terrifying and I don't want to subject her to that."→"You're alone with me all the time."→"Not willingly."（[20773]-[20776]，Maya 敌意线持续低温燃烧；另[20767]她自称"Molly is {i}technically{/i} a friend of mine"——又一段循环残留式的旧交情）。Molly 的元台词全面爆发："Because you're the protagonist! This is a scripted event that must occur if I'm ever to become the lead heroine!"（[20808]）；weebnote 科普 chuunibyou 后牵出 Rin 的"secret life"："stop bringing up my secret life when we're not in the club room anymore! I'm begging you!"（[20842]，社团房间=秘密生活的场所，供后文拼图）。海边恐惧三连以复仇宣言收尾："Make memories that only girls my age are able to make! Live life to the fullest! Find the man who killed my father and avenge him!"→r"Your dad is still alive, though..."（[20919]-[20920]）；被点破害羞根源时自曝"That's like, straight out of a dating-sim. I'm not yet prepared."（[20933]）。Rin×Chika 线推进：被问是否期待看 Chika 泳装，r"I'm going to actually die."（[20954]）；碰拳礼引发 friend-zone 长篇辩论（bonus 版[20968]-[20976]；else 版改成"按下拳头会激活机关让我们死或长高"的无厘头[20978]），判词"Welcome to the friend-zone, bud."（[20986]）与反转"We're already more than just friends in my eyes."（[20996]）；随后"homies"咒语三次令 Rin 满血复活"I can feel my strength returning!"（[21012]-[21014]——把好感度机制直接演成魔法）。旁白自省"even though I'm not the type to normally go out of his way to be kind like this"（[21041]）。

**day154 [21057]——帮 Tsuneyo 搬家+诅咒闹剧**。Miku/Futaba 胸部话题令 bonus 版旁白感慨"I've reached the point of my life where my students are no longer concerned with talking about their chests in front of me."（[21109]）；bonus 版对 Tsuneyo 的身材评价"rather...developed for her age"（[21126]）。核心喜剧链：Tsuneyo 从 Molly 处学了"F 词"，脱口"Fuck you."后陷入宗教式恐慌（"Oh no. What have I done?"，[21213]），摔落装有电脑的箱子，坚信诅咒成立并做俯卧赎罪（[21294]），二十个完成后宣布"The curse has been lifted."（[21307]）。Miku 发怒段落带出两处旧事钩子："The only time I've ever seen her lose her composure was after that ball hit the storage room that one time."（[21259]，储物间球击事件）与"I was lucky enough that Karin was around last time."（[21261]，**Karin 名字首次出现**）。结尾系统播报直白得惊人："{i}Congratulations! Molly and Tsuneyo are officially part of the class!{/i}{i}Both of them can now be visited at night at either of their workplaces or their dorm room!{/i}"（[21343]-[21344]），bonus 版追加"{i}Spend enough time with them and eventually they'll be cool with performing sex acts on you!{/i}{i}Praise be!{/i}"（[21347]-[21348]——好感度系统的裸露说明书+伪宗教感叹词）。

**day139 [21362]——Chika 办公室留堂（成人线入口）**。Chika 借"咨询"预约独处，进门即锁门且自称"out of instinct"（[21395]-[21403]），Sensei 一句"But the door to your dorm room is never-"被掐断（[21403]）。**本 label 最重的暗线是记忆矛盾**：Chika 转述 Yumi 说过被他留堂罚站盯着看（[21444]-[21449]），而 Sensei 内心确认从未有过此事："I've only seen Yumi in here a couple times throughout the year...And I certainly don't recall just making her stand there and staring at her."（[21452]-[21453]——他人记忆与自我记忆冲突：要么他失忆，要么那段经历属于别的周目）。chikadetention 分支：false 线她撒娇讨罚（[21464]-[21470]）；true 线回收更衣室旧约"I was kinda hoping you'd actually follow through with it...or whatever."（[21471]-[21475]）。bonus 版 jump chikafirstlustx（[21515]，成人内容从略）；else 版以纯爱式留堂收场，chika_lust+1（[21521]）。

**beachvacation1 [21532]+restofmayachange [21587]——特别篇开幕与床位政治**。睡过头开场，Ami 未做早餐即触发死亡推理"Is Ami dead?"（[21553]）；Ayane 在场（[21559]）。bonus 版此处 jump mayachangex（[21580]，Maya 更衣成人场景，从略）——**标题 restofmayachange 表明 Maya 是本段真正的隐藏主角**。后勤情报：Makoto 订房、Ayane 刷卡付费、订了两间房（[21606]-[21613]）；else 版第二间的用途是"keeping all of the worms in"（[21615]，蠕虫冷笑话贯穿 else 版床位戏）；Sana 抗议与男生同房（[21636]）；Ayane 强行安排 Sensei 与自己同房被 Ami/Maya 双双否决（[21644]-[21674]）， wink 已成"reflex"（[21674]）。巴士 15 分钟抵达（[21682]）。随后系统直接报幕："{i}Welcome to the first special update for Lessons in Love!{/i}{i}Over the next two days, all events will play in succession.{/i}...{i}enjoy this mostly-wholesome beach adventure!{/i}"（[21694]-[21697]——**游戏名在世界内被念出**，特殊更新的框架声明）。`$ beachvacation1 = True`。

**beachvacation2 [21706]——海滩首日：空滩、Tsuneyo 喵、Futaba 善意与勒索式风景**。盛夏周六的海滩却"surprisingly empty"："It's a Saturday in the middle of Summer, so why the hell isn't anyone around?"（[21824]-[21825]——人口稀薄母题在度假地复现）。Molly/Rin 的网络梗（Reddit 公会招人无赞[21854]）间夹硬情报："It's not often that Haruka gives us the weekend off"（[21868]，**Haruka**=Rin/Molly 打工处老板娘）。Tsuneyo 抱着猫图案泳圈出场，两声"Meow~"先赖给猫再承认（[21975]-[21999]）；来海滩的理由："I'm here to enjoy my youth. And also because the Emerald Guardian asked me to come."（[22008]-[22009]）。知识提问双版本：bonus 版"Please teach me what masturbation is."（[22038]，旁白"That might be the single most unintentionally erotic thing I've ever heard."[22040]）/else 版问 Gaelic（[22042]）；临走放话要让其他女生"beat you up"（else 版[22065]）。Yumi 段：Chika 上班换班今晚才来（[22122]-[22124]）；指控升级"you borderline stalk me in the streets"（[22130]）、"I can tell when someone is a pervert without having to fuckin' research 'em first."（[22133]）——**else 版把同一场对话整体改写为拥抱笑话**（"recharge my hug battery"[22139]），与全章"juggling/hugging"替代策略一致。Futaba 迟到即遭鲸鱼嘲讽（[22173]），却在离开前回敬一句"I think...you look really pretty, Yumi."（[22222]）——本章少有的无回报善意。洗手台后独处：bonus 版按 futaba_lust 分级展开看胸情节（昵称变量 `[futabamaster]` 于[22306]浮出；lust<10 只许一点[22319-22328]，否则全露[22329-22361]），并以 Futaba 的主动权宣言收尾"You can be in charge some other time. I'm here to have fun with my friends, not make my teacher's penis hard."（[22360]）；else 版改为邀请她当晚组队掷蛋比赛（[22363]-[22366]）。Makoto 哨声押送去旅馆办入住（[22375]、[22412]），沿海同行"occasionally bump arms, but this is entirely her doing."（[22460]，Makoto 的身体语言攻势；else 版自罚式祷告梗[22467]）。futaba_love+1、`$ beachvacation2 = True`（[22470]-[22471]）。

### 海滩度假·中（beachvacation3—beachvacation7）

**beachvacation3 [22478]——Makoto 旅馆房**。房间异常：Ayane/Ami 明明说有两张床，前台却说只有一张（[22490]-[22492]）；老板娘"reluctant to give up the key to the other one for some reason"（[22486]，第二间房被扣留——与 else 版蠕虫笑话形成"被藏起来的房间"母题）。Makoto 罕见地发自内心高兴："It's one of the first times she's looked truly happy to me."（[22506]）。**else 版埋下全书级预告台词**："It's probably just because I have a horrible case of depression that you won't find out about until way later on."（[22525]，她以玩笑口吻直接向玩家预告自己的抑郁症线）；bonus 版对应位置是成人行业玩笑（[22523]）。她宣布度假策略："doing my best to forget everything for the next 48 hours...Once Monday comes around, though, normal Makoto is coming back in full-swing."（[22533]-[22534]，连笔记本都没带[22535]）。醉酒旧事：和母亲喝酒后骂了一堆难听话、被父亲禁足一周（[22562]-[22564]）。**操纵关系的自白**：mak 指认"That thing you do where you get all perverted and force stuff out of me because you know I'm weak around you."（bonus 版[22579]/else 版改为抵墙抢午饭钱[22582]），继而"You know how to get what you want...Maybe it was your strategy all along to go from being a good[school]-teacher to a good...life-teacher or something."（[22585]-[22588]），Sensei 顺水推舟"Yeah, let's go with that. That was my strategy all along."（[22591]）。Makoto×Miku 情同姐妹的旁白确认（[22613]-[22614]）。bonus 版 jump makotoinnx（[22623]，成人内容从略）；else 版跳床喜剧收尾："Then we can sell the footage of us jumping on the bed."→"To who?"→"Miku. She will buy it."（[22626]-[22631]），最后一句叙述"I jump so high that I go into space and die. Please tell Ami I love her."（[22636]-[22637]）。makoto_love+3。

**beachvacation4 [22649]——Miku 海水场+神保姐妹乱入**。bonus 版旁白直书刚对 Makoto 做过的事（[22680]）。Miku 的进食焦虑与身份焦虑双联："if I eat more than 1300 calories in a day I'll get fat and undesirable...It's hard enough gettin' people to look at me as a girl to begin with. I've gotta keep up this tomboy thing as long as I can or I'll just fade into the abyss."（[22694]-[22700]）。**else 版两记重磅元弹**：其一，Miku 突然警觉"Wait, are we bein' watched right now?! This ain't some sorta Truman Show thing, is it?!"（[22727]-[22728]——被观看感/模拟论怀疑由喜剧角色说出）；其二，Sensei 台词直接点名作者与游戏名："And maybe people will look back on this line in ten years and think {i}Darn that Selebus guy for telling us everything way beforehand.{/i}"→mi"That's just the beauty of Lessons in Love, Sensei! No one ever knows what parts they're supposed to take seriously!"（[22815]-[22816]——**Selebus+《Lessons in Love》在对话内被点名，且点出本作"分不清哪句该当真"的核心叙事策略**）。Kanda 姐妹（Karin/Kirin）空降（[22824]，旁白称连同她们共十四人[22826]）；Kirin 自曝为旅行练仰卧起坐（[22864]-[22871]）；Ayane 又一次豪掷提出给她们单独买房间"Just how much money does Ayane have to spare?..."（[22886]-[22890]，其财富来源持续成谜）。Karin 被夸泳装后死机"C-C-C-C-C-C..."（[22908]）；Kirin 强索夸奖并按手摸腹（[22915]、[22927]），bonus 版升级为双人贴身摸索（旁白"It feels almost like she's ready to assault me...I don't know if it would technically be classified as assault because I'd be totally into it"，[22942]-[22943]；Miku 推开 Kirin 后加入[22983]-[23014]），Sensei 逃到洗手台后平复（[23041]）；**else 版则以平台审查自白收场**："I must remain in compliance with Patreon's community guidelines no matter what the cost!"→ki"REEEEEEEEEE..."（[23049]-[23050]——Patreon 审查制度被写进角色台词）。

**beachvacation5 [23062]——Kirin 追击与第二重第四墙**。Kirin 质问"Have you and Miku hooked up yet?"（[23105]；else 版改写为"hugged"[23123]）；交易回溯：她当年以身相许换的只是"Vouching for you if you ever decided to not show up to[school]"（[23155]-[23161]，替逃学打掩护——Kirin 缺课线的机制注脚）。bonus 版 jump kirinbeachhjx（[23181]，成人内容从略）；**else 版贡献全书最露骨的一次出戏**：ki"I got called into work."→s"Where do you even work?"→ki"I'm teaching inner city kids to read."→s"Wow. Maybe you are a good person after all."→ki"The censored version of Lessons in Love really gives both of us time to show our true colors, doesn't it?"→s"The what?"（[23185]-[23190]——**角色明确意识到自己身处"审查版"**，Sensei 一句"The what?"把问题原样抛给玩家）。注意 else 版同样落旗 `$ kirinbeachhj = True`（[23202]）——旗标层不区分版本。

**beachvacation6 [23212]——Futaba/Rin 亲近 Yumi 失败+"trinity"立旗（本章暗线核心节点）**。前半是 Rin 对 Chika 的连环口误与拙劣掩盖（"Who even is Chika?"[23240]；被抓包后改口"Chicken! Feathers! Wings! Beaks!"[23311]-[23315]），Futaba 全程看破不说破。随后旁白以培养皿意象定调："Three girls in a line on the beach. One calm. One callous. One concerned."（[23334]-[23335]）；结尾变奏更狠："Three girls in a line on the beach. Each involved in a different relationship with the same man. Each with a different view of him...Perhaps we are not all meant to be happy. And perhaps the girl in the middle of the line is meant to destroy herself in pursuit of what makes the other two whole."（[23493]-[23501]——**中间那个女孩=Yumi，预言她将自毁以成全他人**，全章最阴冷的判词）。对话层情报：Rin 与 Yumi 是中学同学（[23379]-[23384]），Yumi 与 Chika 的交情始于中学（[23385]-[23387]）。Yumi 总攻：指控 Sensei 在利用每个人（bonus 版性剥削控诉[23442]-[23443]+师生性关系同意论质问[23460]/else 版整体替换为马戏团杂耍版[23445]-[23446]、[23454]）；Rin 反驳"Sensei is a good guy and wouldn't ever force anyone to do anything they don't want to!"只换来"Oh, if you only knew fucking half of it."（[23456]-[23457]）。Futaba 以退为进结束冲突："Yumi wants to be left alone, so we should leave her alone."（[23482]）。段末四行伪圣歌式系统腔："{i}Praise be!{/i}{i}Not one god, but three!{/i}{i}The further we fall, the more we will see!{/i}{i}HOPE is the answer, but won't be the key!{/i}"（[23501]-[23504]），紧接 `$ trinity = True`（[23509]）——**"三位神明"与关键词 HOPE 首次以大写形式登场并与旗标绑定**（呼应 happyloop 系列的 shrine GOD 与后续 trinity2/3）。

**beachvacation7 [23519]——Chika 日落场+月色告白未遂**。Chika 披露 Chinami 不能同行是因为"Too many girls close together...unknown inn"（[23544]-[23545]）以及那只狗面具（[23550]，mall15 分支决定 Sensei 是否知道面具存在[23552]-[23557]）；并自述"This is the first time I've ever even been on vacation"（[23560]）。Yumi 来取钥匙，临走对 Sensei 说了句不带恶意的"Thanks. See you."，Chika 立刻点出分量："The way she said goodbye to you...Isn't that like, a huge improvement?"（[23630]-[23638]）。Chika 向 Futaba 道歉并自责（[23697]-[23706]）；Futaba 私下向 Sensei 摊牌她早知 Rin 的心思："Rin is basically my sister. I've had my suspicions for a while...she's been making things a lot more obvious ever since high school started."（[23783]-[23788]）。{i}Meanwhile{/i} 插叙再响丧钟："Oh, how horrible it is to be unloved. To love those who can not understand. And to forgo the love of oneself in pursuit of that. All three gods disappear and leave yet another poor soul to fend for itself as the day becomes the night."（[23818]-[23821]）。Chika×Rin 双分支：chikadorm20 版互相隐瞒暗恋对象并结成秘密同盟，Chika 透露自己也有喜欢的人（[23849]-[23890]，含中学诗歌回忆[23861]-[23872]）；else 版则是 Rin 出柜戏："You don't think it's weird that I basically just...you know...Came out to you?"（[23923]-[23924]），Chika 以 Niki 海报表态支持（[23932]-[23936]，**Niki 名字首次出现**）。月亮场景以日语文学梗收束：r"Yeah... The moon is beautiful..."（[23973]——"月が綺麗ですね"式含蓄告白），旁白随即宣判："But this is not a happy story. There are no happy stories. And so they will break apart. Just as all things do. But at least the moon is beautiful."（[23975]-[23982]）。`$ beachvacation7 = True`。

### 海滩度假·后（beachvacation8 后半—beachvacation11）

**beachvacation8（后半，[24245]起）——D&D 收场：DM 即作者**。Rin 的"历史检定"掷出 5，Molly 判定"You don't know what kind of tree it is."（[24250]-[24251]，Rin 全程手气垫底成为本段主笑点）。Sana 内心："Maybe the five of us have more in common than we thought?"（[24257]-[24258]）。Ayane 以盗贼身份带头冲锋，理由是"It fits my character."（[24263]-[24265]）。Ami 犯下**玩家/角色知识分离**错误——提前喊出对方角色名，Molly 纠正"That was out of character. Your character doesn't know it yet."（[24278]-[24285]，"角色不知道玩家知道的事"正是本作角色处境的镜像）。Sana 闻到树中怪味后冒出一句可疑的"That smell...it reminds me of home..."（[24291]）。Tsuneyo 与 Sensei 斗嘴否定怪物存在："Pretty soon, you're going to be saying stupid things like how dragons exist as well."（[24300]-[24301]），坚持树里只有"Branches. Leaves. Tree things...Wind."（[24303]-[24306]）。Rin 感知检定又掷 5（[24344]-[24350]）。怪物定为紫真菌（Violet Fungus），Ayane 以"I used to live in the forest when I was a girl"申请历史检定（[24361]），Molly 判词"Thanks to years of struggling to grow up and foraging for food in the wilderness..."（[24380]）；Ayane 问"In a tree? Why?..."，Molly 答"It will make sense eventually."（[24385]-[24386]）并两次怒吼"Silence!"（[24591][24606]）——**DM 的"以后你会明白"与"肃静"正是叙事者/作者姿态的戏内投影**。Tsuneyo 控诉 Sensei："That's cheating. You know more about the outside world than I do. It's an unfair advantage."（[24401]-[24403]——常识即外挂，深宅设定的自我注脚）。啤酒即睡梗：Tsuneyo 一口"Delicious."后当场"Zzzzzzzzzzzz..."（[24432]-[24441]），Ami 吃醋"Sensei just letting some girl who isn't me sleep on his [uncle]"（[24447]，[uncle] 变量复现）。战斗喜剧：偷袭加值被当庭驳回（[24491]-[24494]）、Sana 天然 20 成 MVP（[24562]-[24564]）、会流泪求救的蘑菇让 Ami 问"Why is she making us feel bad for the mushroom?"（[24600]）、Rin 的救赎回合以天然 1 的 Eldritch Blast 落空收场（[24652]-[24660]）。**戏中戏点题**：战斗结束后"the party reconciles and comes to the conclusion that all of them have been summoned here from different worlds...they decide to band together."（[24696]-[24697]——来自不同世界、被召唤到同一处、无渊源只能抱团，正是 Kumon-mi 全班处境的桌游复刻）。Sensei 中途退场："It's clear that this sort of thing just...isn't for me. But then again, it doesn't seem like many things are."（[24673]-[24675]）。结算：Ami/Maya/Ayane/Sana/Rin/Molly/Tsuneyo 七人 love 各+1（[24687]-[24693]），旁白"mostly-successful first day of vacation...hoping that tomorrow, something even greater happens."（[24681]-[24684]）。

**beachvacation9 [24703]——深夜海滩×Sana（本段最重的元叙事节点）**。失眠开场："The bed became a ship. And I became lost at sea."（[24706]-[24707]）；由"与船共存亡"引出价值观宣言"If you're going to die, do it for something wonderful. Like a girl you love or an idea you believe in."（[24717]-[24718]）。随后是**全书最直白的世界虚无独白**："Honor has no value in the real world. Nothing has value in the real world. But that's because there is no real world. There's only this place, whatever this may be. Wherever this may be. Which is likely somewhere at the edge of the world."（[24721]-[24726]——直接否认"现实世界"存在，把舞台定位为"世界尽头的某处"）；紧接一句"There has to be {i}something{/i} cold in this burning world."（[24740]，"燃烧的世界"母题）。bonus 版自嘲偷窥为"my game of 'Spy on the loli'"（[24751]，仅成人版）。Sana 身世披露：幼年超市走失，母亲"she went home without me"（[24807]），Sensei 判语"That's not lenient, that's just negligent."（[24808]）；Sara 哭着道歉一整天（[24818]-[24819]），而 Sana 当时的解读是"I thought she was just giving me away for being too rowdy..."（[24892]）；并自述"I wasn't always this quiet, you know."（[24885]），与 Ayane 相识于"a really bad part of my life"（[24880]）。**变色龙独白（双版本共用）**：Sensei 承认对每个人扮演不同的人——"I {i}am{/i} a different person when I'm with her. Just like I'm a different person with everyone else...You adapt. You change yourself to fit better into whatever situation you throw yourself into. Such is the way of the chameleon, and such is the way of me."（[24861]-[24867]——**身份无固定核心**，与"名字不可考""当 Sensei 更容易"[7682] 互文）；else 版 Sana 的请求正中要害："You know the truth about me, Sensei...so it's only fair that you...stop changing who you are when you're with me..."（[24858]）。saradate1 分支：Sana 直言担心 Sensei 与母亲交往（[24908]-[24923]）；bonus 深层披露 Ayane 心态——"it would mean she still has a chance. I think what she's most afraid of is you just...forgetting about her."（[24939]-[24941]，**"被遗忘"恐惧**，与失忆/重置主题同构）；Sensei 内心自判（仅 bonus）："No I'm not, Sana. You don't know half of it. It doesn't matter how nice you are to someone if your end goal is to sleep with them."（[24945]-[24947]）与"I'll be here for you until my veins run dry."（[24950]）。晚风掀发露出一双眼睛："a tiny glimpse of...emptiness? Longing? There's something she wants, but can't have."（[24968]-[24971]）。**季节设定披露**："There is no Fall in Kumon-mi. Just like there is no Spring. All we have are summer and winter."（[25001]-[25003]，与 day128"perpetual summer"[18109] 闭环——小镇只有两个季节，世界人工性的又一证据）；Sensei 旁白自述"ever since I was reborn here"（[25006]，**"reborn"用词**）。ayaneseepover 分支：夏/冬类比情话（[25012]）引出 Sana 半告白"So even if you like the Summer right now, you'll look forward to me eventually, right?"（[25041]），Sensei 追问"Was that a confession, Sana?"（[25049]）；随后是吃醋戏："I come home to find you cuddling with Ayane..."（[25087]）、"You never said anything about other nights. And I don't even want to ask because...I feel like it's just better if I stay in the dark..."（[25113]-[25114]），他内心承认"I feel that way too..."（[25116]——**双向选择性无知**）。else 版同一位置是冷处理："Don't ever flirt with me, Sana."（[25121]）。看星星后旁白反讽："I'm glad Sana was able to look past the situation with Ayane and get over it so easily...Without even shedding a tear."（[25143]-[25145]）。**未解异响悬置**：sa"You didn't hear anything while we were watching...the stars...did you?" s"All I heard were the cicadas."（[25162]-[25164]，她担心自己泄露了什么；文本永不揭晓）。段末**祈祷文**（仅 ayaneseepover 分支）："O world. In our final moments- Please remind us how it feels to be wanted without another unwanted in return. And when we sleep- Gift us happy dreams. Of places where nothing bad can ever happen. And of a love so pure- It destroys the world."（[25174]-[25181]——与 bv6 trinity 赞歌[23501-23504]同族的仪式腔；"纯粹到足以毁灭世界的爱"可作全书题眼）。系统腔当场拆台："{i}Sana's affection still manages to rise to [sana_love] despite spending the night worrying about what you do to her best friend while she is away!{/i} {i}Oh, the tricks our hearts manage to play on us!{/i}"（[25188]-[25189]）。`$ beachvacation9 = True`、sana_love+1（[25184]-[25185]）。

**endofbeachvac9 [25236]——假期硬闸门**。`$ totaldays += 1`、`$ day += 1`（[25237]-[25238]）；`if day == 7:` 否则直接向玩家输出字面量 "ERROR"（[25239]-[25243]——**度假必须恰好在周日晚结束，否则世界本身报错**，引擎级脆弱性的罕见外露）；随后按 ami_lust>=10 分流 jump amilust10 / amilust10skip（[25247]-[25250]）。

**amilust10 [25252] / amilust10skip [25291]——双胞胎晨间 label**。内容几乎逐字相同（Ami 叫醒未遂），bonus 版 jump amilust10x/amilust10skipx（[25272]/[25311]，成人内容从略）；else 版旁白自我交代："Ami leaves the room without doing anything because this is a family friendly game."（[25280]/[25319]——审查状态被旁白直陈）。唯一实质差异：amilust10 版落旗 `$ amilust10 = True`（[25284]），该旗标将在 bv11 被 Maya 的暗示台词消费（[25789]）。

**beachvacation10 [25327]——早餐群像**。Ayane 吹牛放跑宠物店小狗："You {i}can't{/i} kick me out! My father owns the place!"..."My middle name is danger"（[25334]-[25341]），Makoto 拆台"You don't even have a middle name. You're Japanese."（[25346]）。Miku/Makoto 就"狗该不该自由"展开正经犬类驯化辩论（[25347]-[25355]）。住宿情报：Rin 整晚抱住 Miku——"she was all up on me like I was her friggin' body pillow!"（[25372]-[25373]，Rin 的肢体依赖对象转移）；Kirin 睡在浴室门前且没醒过（[25374]-[25380]），Karin 承认半夜换衣时踢过她（[25385]）。姐妹猫名骗局揭晓：Kirin 以为抽签命名猫，Karin 坦白"The {i}real{/i} truth is that I made up all of the names in the hat and just let you pick whichever one because I liked them all."（[25420]），猫名 Pancake（[25432]-[25433]）。Makoto 的姐妹观暴露控制欲："always having someone to look after and...train, I guess. Kind of like a mini-version of myself."（[25441]-[25442]）。**Tsuneyo 双版本陷阱问**：被 Ayane 问"在想什么"，bonus 版答"Masturbation."（[25486]）/else 版答"The Gaelic language."（[25488]）；笑点揭底是 Sensei 的教唆——"I heard our teacher use the word yesterday and he told me it would be best to ask all of you about it."（[25524]），Makoto 骂"That son of a bitch."（[25533][25544]）；else 版平行轨道同样荒诞：Ayane 建议"ask Molly? I'm pretty sure she can speak it."（[25556]），Tsuneyo 追问"But what if Scott does not want me on his land?"（[25573]），Ayane 只能敷衍"Sure. Yeah. I know Scott..."（[25602]）。收尾 Karin 昏倒在早餐盘上（[25614]），`$ beachvacation10 = True`（[25624]）。

**beachvacation11 [25630]——遮阳伞下×Maya（else 版两记重磅口误）+西瓜殉难记**。Molly 以多把遮阳伞筑起防晒工事，自比被丢进"the depths of Orodruin"（[25646]，LOTR 典故），称 Sensei 为"Supreme Overlord"。Maya 自述躲避 Sensei 的计划是撑满"the full 48 hours"（[25657]，48 小时数字复现）。**游戏框架之争**：Molly 问"Are you two like arch nemeses or something?...every interaction you've had together since I joined the class has been pretty toxic. Even thought about reporting you to the GMs on one or more occasions for gameplay sabotage."（[25665]-[25666]——**GMs（游戏管理员）概念首次出现**，Molly 的世界观里连校园霸凌都是可举报的游戏行为）；Maya 反驳"This isn't a game. This is real life."，Molly 怼回"What do you know, Maya?!"，她答"A little too much, I'm afraid."（[25667]-[25673]——**坚称现实且承认自己知道得太多**）；又补刀"Molly is perhaps the only person I know who is more delusional than you."（[25681]）。Futaba 缺席确认：已回宿舍、"not feeling well"，Maya 补一句"It won't be an issue soon, though."（[25697]-[25702]，尾音不祥）；Molly 阴恻恻接"{i}Winter is coming{/i}."，Sensei 问"Why did you say that so menacingly?"（[25707]-[25708]——与"只有夏冬"设定[25001-25003]闭环，冬季=不祥季）。**else 版 Maya 口误（重置残渣又一实证）**：被问 Sensei 的爱好，她脱口"He used to hang out with me."，Sensei 惊"I did?"，她慌忙"Yes. I mean whoops. Who said that?"（[25719]-[25721]——与 day140 Molly 的"Oh dear god, no."[19234] 同族：**Maya 与 Sensei 存在被抹除的旧交往**）；bonus 版同位置是偷窥指控（[25716]）。毛巾拒绝戏，else 版爆出全书级台词：m"I'm afraid it might cause me to fall in love with you all over again." s"????????????" m"Oh. Crap. I have to keep up the facade."（[25773]-[25775]——**"all over again"暗示存在前段感情，"facade"承认敌意是伪装**；bonus 版对应位置是"皮肤会融化"式傲娇[25770]-[25771]）。若 amilust10 旗标在，Maya 暗示知道晨间之事，Sensei 内心警觉："Does Maya know about {i}that{/i}?...Maya has been known to understand a little more than she's supposed to at times. This is incredibly worrying."（[25793]-[25798]）。Molly 即兴存在主义发言："What is life if not an excuse to feel pain? To grow old and die...We're all creatures just struggling to find our purpose before our time runs out."（[25863]-[25864]），随后表演 Maya 的无表情脸（[25881]-[25883]），Maya 评价"Your personality can be mimicked by any prepubescent boy in Japan."（[25897]）。**名字消失玩笑**：Sensei 假装找不到 Maya，Molly 接"Maya? I've...I've never even heard that name before..."，Sensei 竟回"Wait a minute...neither have I."（[25900]-[25909]——**名字可被集体遗忘的轻量预演**，呼应 Sensei 自己名字不可考[7682]）。西瓜事件：Karin 抡球棒要砸西瓜（[25930]-[25944]），Maya 失态冲出"Oh no...No...This can't be happening. I have to go!"（[25944]-[25946]）；Sensei 解释她有"unique affinity for watermelons"，且"They need to be cut into perfect wedges or else she gets super cranky"（[25956]-[25980]，西瓜怪癖首次正面交代）。结局：Maya 最后一刻飞身挡瓜被球棒命中（[26018]-[26022]），留下遗言"Let this be a lesson on the...The proper treatment...of watermelons..."（[26030]-[26038]）；旁白接**戏仿式后日谈**："Karin spent the rest of her life wandering the world and educating youths in urban areas about how to properly treat watermelons."（[26044]-[26045]）"Maya died shortly after the incident. We spread her ashes over her favorite melon farm."（[26046]-[26047]——明显假死的滑稽讣告）。**元叙事收尾直接对玩家喊话**："And me? I bet you're wondering what I did. But this isn't my story. It's yours. So what will you do? Will you die for what you believe in? Or will you be the one bearing the {s}baseball bat{/s} axe?"（[26052]-[26058]——"这不是我的故事，是你的"+删除线现场改词 bat→axe）。`$ beachvacation11 = True`、maya/molly love+1（[26061]-[26063]）。

### 海滩度假·终（beachvacation12—beachvacation15）

**beachvacation12 [26072]——Chika/Tsuneyo/Miku 毯子组**。bonus 版旁白补完西瓜事件："no. Maya didn't actually die during the watermelon incident. Thankfully, Karin was able to hold back and only used a fraction of her true power."（[26085]-[26087]，以假死后日谈的口吻正式"复活"）。Tsuneyo 为昨天的教唆事件兴师问罪（[26120]-[26123]），Miku 点破"Sneaky move havin' us girls do yer' dirty work for ya."（[26112]）。Chika 站队 Tsuneyo："It's probably for the best if you just...don't talk about masturbation or sex or anything like that with her."（[26127]-[26128]）。bonus 版 Sensei 反手问 Miku "Any thoughts on reverse cowgirl?"（[26136]）→ jump cowboyx（[26147]，成人内容从略）；else 版维持"语言艺术课"框架，被驱逐时留下反讽式假道歉："I will use this opportunity to {i}not{/i} study where this disconnect comes from and simply employ better common sense..."（[26160]），Tsuneyo 补刀"And stay out, peasant."（[26161]）。`$ beachvacation12 = True`、chika/tsuneyo/miku love 各+1（[26164]-[26166]）；段末按 `ayane_lust >= 10` 分流 jump ayanelust10 / ayanelust10skip（[26175]-[26178]）。

**ayanelust10 [26180] / ayanelust10skip [26274]**。前者：Ayane 靠墙苦等二十分钟只为制造偶遇（[26195]-[26211]），称呼 Sensei 为"[ayanemaster]"（[26181]），Sensei 回称"If it isn't Ayane Amamiya, heiress to the Amamiya fortune."（[26187]——**家族姓氏 Amamiya 与"财产继承人"身份首次并列点出**，其财富线索[22890]的文本锚点）；成人内容从略，else 版仅余"我不背书靠毁掉别人劳动成果来接近人"的拥抱版说教（[26260]），落旗 `$ ayanelust10 = True`（[26263]）。后者（skip 版）全篇只有三行诗体失眠："I fall asleep, but do not dream. Everything is black. And nothing falls but me."（[26275]-[26277]——**下坠意象**，与后续坠落母题呼应）。

**beachvacation13 [26285]——Rin 深夜造访（rinbetrayed 分歧点，本章情感最重 label）**。Rin 含泪立于床边，为今晚向 Chika 告白而恐慌（[26325]-[26335]）；她已预设失败："She definitely has feelings for somebody. But it's a million-percent not me."，并追问"Do you know who it {i}is{/i}, though? Because I'm pretty sure I do. And I've gotta say, it's pretty surprising to me."（[26359]-[26368]——她已猜到 Chika 喜欢的是 Sensei）；更出现宿命论句式"Maybe I'm supposed to be rejected? Maybe that's just...what needs to happen in order for me to move on?"（[26374]-[26375]）。Sensei 摊牌自伤复发："Is that why you didn't tell me you started hurting yourself again?"（[26397]），情报源是 Molly（[26404]-[26419]）；Rin 辩称只是没涂遮瑕的旧疤并邀请验看（[26421]-[26423]），两人各说一句"...I'm supposed to trust you."/"And I'm supposed to trust you too."（[26426]-[26427]——**互信台词实为互疑宣言**）。Rin 自我定位："So do you...have any advice for a girl on her deathbed?"→"Mental death bed. I'm probably gonna...shut down for a little bit after tonight."（[26459]-[26463]）。她请求留宿的逻辑令人心碎："Because of my anti-pervert barrier. You won't touch me as long as I'm crying. Which is exactly why I want you to let me sleep here tonight."（[26518]-[26519]）；以及自毁预防条款："I won't really be able to do anything stupid as long as you're here with me. So if I try to get up or something, just squeeze me really tight and don't let me go."（[26540]-[26541]）。chikadorm20 分支里 Chika 昨晚误判 Rin 喜欢 Sensei（[26577]），else 分支误判为 Molly（[26581]-[26583]）。随后 Rin 问出核心问题："If there's anything...going on between you and Chika."（[26608]-[26609]）。**rinbetrayed 线**（chikadorm20==True）：Sensei 内心认罪"It's my fault for doing exactly what Rin didn't want me to do. I pursued a relationship with the girl she claims to love. And I did it all while calling the one sitting next me...A friend."（[26617]-[26620]）；Rin 连环逼问"Have you kissed her, Sensei?..."（[26632]；bonus 版直白升级[26635]-[26649]/else 版替换为"Have you two...Hugged?..."[26651]-[26653]）；他招认"We've...done some things..."（[26669]；else 版忏悔戏"I am bad, bad boy...I hugged her. Because I am the huggy boy."[26696]-[26705]）。旁白宣判："Before the end of the day, this girl will likely experience heartbreak a second time. The first was at the hands of me. A man who broke the one and only promise she ever asked him to make. And the second will be at the hands of the opposite end of that promise. The world is filled with horrible things- And I am among them."（[26728]-[26733]——**"唯一要求他许下的承诺"被打破**，指前章"不要追 Chika"之约）。bonus 版彻底自白："What more are relationships than tools to use to your advantage?...I was never going to reject Chika. I only said I would."（[26737]-[26749]）；接警句"We can't help who we fall in love with. But we can help falling in love. And love is such a confusing word. The worst word that doesn't begin with the letter T."（[26755]-[26758]）；段末八行斜体圣歌式排比以"Make your dreams come true."收束（[26763]-[26771]），落旗 `$ rinbetrayed = True`（[26773]）。**无罪线**（else）：他说"There's nothing going on."（[26676]）；但若 chikatownfirst==True，旁白承认"the truth is we did kiss. But Rin doesn't need to know that."（[26785]-[26791]——**谎言被叙事层盖章保留**）；随后是"The Five Hug Languages"式喜剧缓冲（[26819]）；bonus 版预叙"She laughs like a [young_girl] would with an older brother...In fact, the real struggle hasn't even begun. That doesn't come until later."（[26845]-[26849]）。收束双版本共用："the two of us are walking shoulder to shoulder down the shoreline. But only one of us will likely come back unscathed."（[26873]-[26874]）。

**beachvacation14 [26886]——Maya 星空对话（重置设定的正面总陈）**。开场即异常：Sensei 走失、忘记目的地"Where was I going again?"（[26906]）、在沙地上爬行（[26900]-[26902]）；对树描述发生**素材级错乱**——先呈现一幅树图，随即"Wait. No. That isn't right. This tree was happier."（[26910]-[26913]，画面与记忆不符后现场换图）。精神崩解排版："{s}WHO WOULD JOKE AT A TIME LIKE THIS?{/s} THIS IS VACATION! THE HAPPIEST POSSIBLE TIME. So why is everything breaking?"（[26941]-[26944]）。随后出现**未知声部（说话人标签 av）**："Come to the shoreline. Reach for salvation. Reach for anything you can grab hold of. {s}Unless it's me.{/s}"（[26946]-[26949]——av 为全脚本罕见标签，删除线自我修正暗示引诱与警告同体）。视觉炸裂行："A GIRL STANDS {s}ALONE{/s} ON THE BEACH WITH HER HANDS CLASPED IN PRAYER POSITION."（[26962]）；"But why would someone who despises {s}GOD{/s} praying so much be doing just that at a time like this?"（[26963]）；Maya 的祷词直接显示为"m [[redacted prayer. god is real.]"（[26965]——**方括号红字"god is real"替代了祷文本身**）；Sensei 听不懂，强行合理化为外语（[26967]-[26972]）。ayanelust10 分支再现 Maya 全知："How is Ayane?"..."She didn't look so great when she came back from the inn. I wonder what could have possibly happened in there?"（[27001]-[27007]；bonus 版"Not this time. But I'm pretty sure I can guess what happened."[27011]-[27012]；else 版用沙堡谎言遮掩[27016]-[27018]）。**重置机制总陈（全书最明确段落）**：Sensei 问"You're talking about your reset button or whatever it is. Can't you just fix whatever you don't like?"，Maya 答"Clearly not if you're still here."（[27051]-[27053]）；关键三连——"I'm not the one resetting anything. I'm just asking someone else to do it."（[27060]-[27061]，**执行者另有其人**）；"it's not like I can do it whenever or however I want either. There are some things that work and some things that don't work."（[27062]-[27063]）；"There are still several months left until we need to worry about that again."（[27065]，**重置周期以月计**）。她还劝他辞职："You know you could always just quit if you don't want to do it anymore...I wouldn't complain."（[27074]）；对其师生关系评价"Quite frankly, I find it abhorrent."（[27081]）；amilust10+bonus 分支再度展示全知（"Because I was, you idiot. I don't know what she is to you, but to me, she's my best friend."[27094]-[27095]）；else 版收在"You {i}are{/i} the huggy boy..."（[27112]）。**两次测试**：星星观感测试须答"fine"才算过关（[27121]-[27127]）；信仰测试引出她的信条："If there are things this beautiful...anything is possible. It defies all logic. It defies all reason. But I still believe it while my head is raised. Of course, those feelings go away as soon as I close my eyes. But for a brief moment in this seemingly infinite timeline, I doubt nothing."（[27161]-[27168]）；并断言"Those people are wrong, of course"（针对"神在星间注视"[27135]-[27137]）。**记忆法则**："Memories persist through resets. Well, most of the time at least. Our physical forms may revert to the way they were months prior...But the things we see and the things we hear do not."（[27191]-[27194]）；bistro 重逢场景即例证（[27195]），且她承认自己也不知为何"That's normal. Neither do I."（[27197]）；Sensei 问"can't you just ask whoever is resetting the world then?"，答"You can ask anyone anything, but it doesn't mean you'll ever get an answer."（[27198]-[27199]——**重置者不可问询**）。她对前景的判断："How many years worth of memories can we fit into such short time periods without going absolutely insane? It will all come tumbling down one day."（[27208]-[27210]）；最终警告"So the next time you suggest that nothing is permanent, keep in mind that some things very much are. Some things are too permanent."（[27212]-[27213]）。Sensei 内心三问收尾："I wonder how many times she's reset by now? And I wonder- How many more resets will there be until I'm gone? How easily can someone else steal back the body I stole myself?"（[27224]-[27231]——**"我偷来的身体也可能被人偷走"**，主角对自身存在的元认知）；"And I think about praying, for the first time in my life- That I am one of those things."（[27232]-[27234]）。

**beachvacation15 [27249]——Chika×Rin 月下告白（trinity 计数减一）**。Rin 披露寄养儿童身份与游泳创伤（foster mom 的"Trial by fire"教学法[27267]-[27273]，Chika 惊讶"That's like, another super big surprise about you."[27276]）。旁白恋爱病理学："Love is the only sickness that is socially acceptable to go out of your way to contract...Yet still, we yearn for it."（[27317]-[27330]）。**Chika 自我说漏时间线**："Even ran into Sensei on the way here a few months ago or- Wait... When...was that?..."（[27339]-[27345]，她对自己的记忆日期产生困惑——记忆异常再添一例）。家庭情报：Chinami 是妹妹、"like a mini version of me"（[27354]-[27360]）；Yumi 待 Chinami 出奇温柔，"sometimes I feel like Chinami is the only person out there who's able to get the {i}real{/i} Yumi to come out."（[27378]）；Chika 母亲中学时去世（[27389]-[27399]），Rin 连当天是星期三穿校服都记得（[27394]-[27395]）。两人年龄差源于留级/跳级，"Maybe even closer to two"年（[27464]-[27466]）。Chika 人设自白："I spent a lot of time refining my personality into like, this ideal sort of girl. But I can't help but feel like that's not really me at times"（[27433]-[27434]），Rin 反驳"None of it's fake...that would make you brave."（[27441]-[27443]）；Chika 的铠甲逻辑："I just don't let them break me because, if they do, it's not just me that's going down."（[27457]-[27458]）。月亮诱饵："I kind of wish it would last forever. Don't you?"（[27489]-[27490]）——Rin 以"I'm in love with you."作答（[27494]）。旁白计数骤变："{s}Three{/s} Two girls in a line on the beach. One crying. One confused."（[27507]-[27508]——**bv6 的"三个女孩"被删除线划成两个，三神结构当场折损一员**）。Rin 慌忙撤回："I changed my mind and I want to go back to being friends."（[27556]）；Chika 试图坦白自己的心上人（[27565]-[27566]），被 Rin 制止（[27567]-[27569]）。结尾两记冷锤：旁白"And the latter locks her eyes on the water as she, in a moment of role-reversal- Drowns."（[27605]-[27606]）；系统播报"{i}Rin's affection with Chika has been reset to 0!{/i}"（[27607]——**关系数值被"重置"**，与世界观 reset 术语撞车）+"Life moves on."（[27608]）。（bv16 已于本读段末开始，归入下批。）

### 度假终局：旁白崩解（beachvacation16）与收束、trinity2、万圣节开幕

**beachvacation16 [27617]——旁白彻底崩解（本章元叙事最高峰）**。开篇祷词续完：在"{s}God{/s} god is dead"的世界向谁祈祷（[27626]），"For the Xth time."（[27645]，**次数以变量 X 交付**）；"Perhaps they, too, have become stars."（[27653]）。旁白转向 Rin："experiencing that pain for the first of many times to come"（[27662]，**"第一次"却已预告"无数次"——循环论再次压在具体角色身上**）；bonus 版自嘲偷窥（[27667]）/else 版"the huggy boy"（[27669]）。洪水-重启意象："Tears can get you anything if there are enough of them. And if you cry with all of your might, there's a chance you could even drown the world. Drown the world and start again."（[27674]-[27676]——**以泪水淹没世界并重来=重置的神话化表述**）。Chika 寻 Rin 而略去缘由，旁白点破"the one she destroyed is in the arms of the one that she loves"（[27698]-[27703]）；判责归神："No one is at fault here other than {s}God{/s} god. If he created the world, why did he feel the need to give it so many flaws?"（[27706]-[27707]）。**时钟布道（ticktock 主题的正面展开）**：等待-遗忘-入睡的循环"Does that remind you of anything?...It's kind of like a clock. Isn't it? Tick tock."（[27729]-[27736]）；随后是全书对"重置"最狂热的比喻——"Perhaps we can fight time with more clocks!...We'd gather so many clocks that {s}God{/s} god would be forced to listen to us!...We can simply turn back a clock. And if he doesn't notice, we'll turn back {i}another{/i} clock. And another and another until he's forced to listen! Praise be! Destroy the things you hate! Safeguard the ones you love!"（[27739]-[27753]——**把钟拨回去=重置**，与 day138 的 `$ ticktock` 旗标[19002]、day220 崩坏段形成关键词链）。旁白直接对玩家估价："just look at the girl on the right. How long do you think she will live for?...her time here is shorter than even yours- But I guess you're a little special when it comes to that sort of thing, huh?"（[27759]-[27763]——**暗示 Rin 寿命更短、玩家角色"特殊"**）；"But you keep your hopes up because this is all 'just a game,' right? If this is a game, then so is life. Because nothing is real. Shatter the barrier!"（[27767]-[27770]）；"Just like how everyone you pretend to love managed to survive the weekend."（[27780]）。**对玩家的连环质问**："Did you have a good time? What was your favorite part?...What do you believe in?...TELL ME EVERYTHING! TELL ME EVERYTHING!!!! HELP ME LEARN TO LIVE!!! WATCH ME GROW, AS IF I AM A TREE! And cut me down the second you no longer have a use for me."（[27783]-[27801]——旁白自比被玩家栽种/砍伐的树）；"If all men were created equal, then surely you and I are something else. Because the two of us are below it all."（[27802]-[27803]）；"Will you blur the line between fiction and reality with me? Will you, too, become a tree?...We can show each other our branches. Our leaves. The wide part. And our roots."（[27817]-[27823]，与 bv14 的树描述[26922]-[26924]逐件对应）。现实层收尾：r"Nothing...will ever hurt this bad again...will it?" s"There are plenty of things that will hurt just as bad as this. But I'll be here each and every time."（[27829]-[27833]）；旁白插话"Sometimes, things are just meant to break...The clock is ticking! Tick tock!"（[27843]-[27849]）；**Rin 也听见了**："I...I can hear something...It's like...a ticking noise..."（[27856]-[27858]——滴答声从叙事层渗入角色听觉）。`$ beachvacation16 = True`、`$ rin_love += 5`（[27881]-[27882]）；系统腔收束"Your last night on the beach vanishes as quickly as the prayer of a normal [teenage]girl alone on the shoreline."（[27885]）。

**endofbeachvac16 [27890] / endofbeachvacation [27904]——假期结算与"特别更新"谢幕**。前者：`$ totaldays += 1`、**`$ day -= 6`**（[27894]-[27895]，把星期日拨回星期一——引擎层的"拨钟"），失败分支输出"ERROR ADVANCING TO MONDAY"（[27900]）。后者：Rin 清晨先行回家、只留字条（[27924]-[27932]），Sensei 对 Ami 谎称"不知情"（[27937]）；ayanelust10 分支中 Ayane 精神萎靡，旁白联想 Kirin 昨日的冲击（[27941]-[27953]）。回城安排暴露世界意识："I give the girls a bit of a head start so it doesn't seem like I arrived with all of them at once...I'm pretty sure {i}someone{/i} would wind up getting the wrong idea"（[27992]-[27994]）。系统腔谢幕词直接报出游戏名："{i}Congratulations! You've completed the first ever special update for Lessons in Love!{/i}...{i}I hope you had a good time!{/i}"（[28000]-[28002]），jump afterschool（[28007]）。

**trinity2 [28009]——献祭经济学、HO 实体与凯撒密码（全书最密元叙事 label）**。触发式插曲：以"I want to go somewhere fun!"开场（[28010]），"Fun things happen. Please listen to them."（[28029]-[28030]）。红天："It's awfully red out today."（[28035]），bonus 版念头带删除线暴力："It puts me in the mood to {s}[rape] and kill the first woman I see{/s} throw a party!"（[28038]）。**手机开口说话**：yt 标签"Beep."（[28046]），"The phone speaks to me for the first time...what it really means is AS FOLLOWS"（[28048]-[28049]），讯文即游戏机制神学："This world is going to burn before our eyes. The only way to escape from our inevitable misfortune (IE: Death) is to accumulate as many affection points with as many girls as possible. If we offer them all up as tribute, everything will turn out A-Okay! Our {s}GOD{/s} friend demands them as tribute!"（[28053]-[28056]——**好感度=献给神的贡品，攻略机制被转写为献祭仪式**）。人肉记忆闪回（"It's more like biting the cushion of a sofa if the sofa was made of skin."[28058]-[28060]）与自毁插语"{s}Please just fucking kill me{/s}"（[28061]）。菜单唯一项"Maya, that clever bitch"（[28066]）。西语场景自带舞台说明："a language we have both decided to master right now for the sake of {s}the{/s} this scene"（[28083]），Sensei 结尾蹦出日语"ありがとう"（[28086]）。地狱层数设定"as many layers as you want it to have. As long as that number is 23"（[28122]-[28123]），他用钢笔把 23 刻进手臂（[28127]-[28129]）。名字故障喜剧：s"So, Maya." m"Hm? Who?" s"Sorry. I meant to say Maya."（[28131]-[28133]）。三神话题被 Maya 挡回（[28148]-[28150]）。bonus 版裸体感谢仪式：嘶声"ssss.../kkkk..."献给"He feeds off of happiness!...And also lust!"的神（[28195]-[28201]）；amifingered 分支直呼"[amimaster]...You'll even fuck your [niece]!"（[28204]），else 分支以事件锁威胁："He must! Or face being hard-locked out of future events!"（[28211]——**锁事件=神罚**）；else 版整段替换为机器红字："/////////////////////////REDACTING SEGMENTS OF THE EVENT FLAGGED AS PROBLEMATIC...PROCESS COMPLETE"（[28219]-[28224]——**审查被演成一场仪式**）。地鸣中"真神"将返，众眼之问（[28230]-[28237]）；Maya/Ami 触碰如砂纸、探入胃部（bonus 版肠管比喻[28252]/else 版香蕉[28254]），"Hope is lost."（[28256]）。**HO 实体登场（说话人标签 ho，全大写）**："HELLO AGAIN"（[28274]）；"It has beautiful eyes, but I'm unfortunately only able to see two of them."（[28277]）。对话以**凯撒移位 7 的密码文**进行（Sensei 台词亦然）：s"dov hyl fvb huk dof kvlz aopz wshjl ibyu tf zrpu"＝"who are you and why does this place burn my skin"（[28280]）；ho"THE PLACE YOU SEEK DOES NOT EXIST / BUT THEN AGAIN / NEITHER DOES THIS PLACE"（[28291]-[28293]）；"STRANGE THINGS CONTINUE TO HAPPEN TO YOU HERE, DON'T THEY?????"（[28294]）；s"ovd kv fvb ruvd aoha"＝"how do you know that"（[28295]）；s"hyl fvb tl"＝"are you me"（[28298]）；ho"OF COURSE NOT, YOU FUCKING IDIOTTTT"（[28299]）；核心自述"I AM THAT WHICH BREATHES / AND YOU ARE THE GROUND BENEATH ME / FEED ME EVERYONE YOU LOVE / WATCH ME GROW"（[28300]-[28303]）；密码三连（解出）："AOLYL PZ VUSF VUL NVK"＝THERE IS ONLY ONE GOD、"DOPJO VUL DPSS FVB JOVVZL"＝WHICH ONE WILL YOU CHOOSE、"AOLYL HYL ADV DYVUN HUZDLYZ"＝THERE ARE TWO WRONG ANSWERS（[28304]-[28306]）。怪物治愈他的伤（[28308]-[28310]），临别预言直指后续终端线："YOU HAVE SPENT FAR TOO MUCH TIME HERE FOR A MAN WHO DOES NOT BELIEVE / PERHAPS YOU WERE LOOKING FOR SOMEONE ELSE / SOMEONE WITH MORE WIRES / WIRES AND WIRES AND WIRES AND WIRES AND WIRES"（[28312]-[28315]——**"更多电线的某位"=USER 终端存在体的预告**）。场景跳转至坟墓："significantly more color, but also less color at the same time"（[28333]）；身旁忍泪的女孩是 Ami（[28335]）；"Her parents."（bonus 版"My brother and his wife."[28346]——**Ami 亡父母=Sensei 之兄夫妇**）；关键记忆存疑段："I suddenly remember them. Or do I?...All I have are lingering memories that may or may not have been placed here by someone else. I can't act on my own anymore."（[28348]-[28352]，**记忆可能系他人植入+行动权丧失**）。乐园反讽："This is Kumon-mi! The land of happiness!...There is only one god! But there are three gods! And also no gods!"（[28368]-[28373]）。"something about this feels more like a memory than a dream. A painful memory."（[28383]-[28384]）；死亡预告"We're going to die, just like my brother and his wife did...And, within a month, there will be no more tears shed for us."（[28395]-[28399]）。三神论："They will always love us. Or at least they'll pretend to. So it is in our best interest to keep them alive. To water them."（[28410]-[28413]；bonus 版以亲人遗体喂养[28417]）。创世神话 bonus 版："The world was created when a ball of light fucked a fleshlight too many times, and now {i}we{/i} are the fleshlight."（[28418]）；else 版直接显示"[[REDACTING INFORMATION ABOUT THE WORLD'S CREATION. THERE IS NO BONUS.]"（[28421]——**红字自曝版本差异本身**）。神格崩溃四连问："But who are they? Am I one? Are you one? Is Maya one? Is Ami one? WHAT IS A FUCKING GOD?!"（[28425]-[28430]）。绝望瀑布以钟收尾："I'm envious of clocks...I wish we were clocks."（[28444]-[28451]）。**天空说话（说话人标签 sky）**："One day, you are going to be happy. It might be a long time away, and it might be hard to get there, but it will happen. I promise." s"Fuck you."（[28460]-[28463]），旁白"I curse at the sky because it lies to me. Happiness isn't real. Nothing is real."（[28465]-[28467]——**天空的承诺可读作作者对玩家的承诺，被角色当场诅咒**）。"And then I die."（[28477]）。旗标收束：`$ trinity2track = True`、`$ trinity2 = False`（[28513]-[28514]——**清零自身旗标以便再次触发**），随后"I change my mind...decide to go to Tojo Ramen instead."→jump ramenshop（[28516]-[28518]，trinity2 实为插入式过场）。

**halloween1 [28521]——万圣节宣布（第二个"特别更新"开幕）**。Molly 在校自办游戏秀毁了午睡（[28528]）。Ayane/Ami/Sana 夜访宣布：万圣节派对、Ayane 家、明天购物；管家 Geoffrey 已许可（[28579]-[28582]，**Ayane 宅与管家 Geoffrey 首次点名**）。Sensei 给 Sana 塞猫耳想法（[28590]-[28594]"Cat-girl Sana is my number-one pick"）。Ayane 自曝因海滩之旅超支被限制月度零花钱（[28608]-[28609]）。Ami 的分尸玩笑（[28631]）。Sana 提出根本性质疑："it's a little strange to me how much they yell when they're...supposedly best friends."（[28643]-[28644]）；Ayane 对单恋的自我解释："I realized there's no good way to hide it, so I've just added that trait into my personality."（[28658]-[28659]——**把感情编译进人格**）。留宿要求（[28669]）与 Sana 挤手臂秘闻（[28700]-[28711]，Ayane 自称"future-husband"[28708]）。Ami 的独占宣言"Look at me. Only me."（[28726]；else 版以会计口吻[28731]）。Sana 的房间抱怨"It smells like Sensei in here and it makes me feel funny."（[28752]-[28753]）遭 Ayane 放大（[28754]-[28757]）。三连"I love you"实验：Ayane 索取成功（[28792]-[28795]）、Sana 当场昏厥（[28804]-[28820]），Ayane 解说"Sana isn't ready to accept your love yet! That comes much later in the story!"，Sensei 答"How much later? I feel like I've been waiting for eons."（[28821]-[28822]——**角色讨论自身故事线的解锁进度**）。Ayane 对昏迷的 Ami 耳语一个未知秘密（[28841]-[28863]，内容永不揭示）。系统腔预告线性化："{i}Welcome to Lessons in Love's first special Halloween update!{/i} The following events will play in succession from start-to-finish. {i}You will not be able to choose how you spend your time during the events.{/i}"（[28886]-[28890]——**剥夺选择权被明示**）。`$ halloween1 = True`（[28896]）。

**halloween2 [28898] 前半——商场采购日（周六）**。旁白对海滩结局致歉插语"(Sorry, Rin)"（[28918]）；`$ totaldays += 1`、`$ day = 6`（[28949]-[28951]）。Ami 决定自制服装（[28968]-[28969]），备用方案是找 Molly 帮忙（[28983]-[28984]），并自嘲只能 cosplay 动画角色因为"Halloween shop doesn't carry those because of copyright stuff"（[28985]-[28986]）。Chika 在自家精品店出场；**Yumi 生日=万圣节**情报（[29021]-[29022]）；Chinami 的狗面具话题意外引出**Sensei 牵着戴狗面具的小女孩逛街**的暴露（[29029]-[29044]，Chika 惊讶 Chinami 居然让他牵手"She's normally super careful about touching people she's not familiar with."[29050]-[29051]）。Ami 刷走信用卡（[29063]-[29069]"The power of [niece]s"）。归途偶遇 Sara/Haruka：性感护士装+Maki 的猫装（h 一声"Nyaa~"[29125]），二人邀 Sensei 今晚去 Sara 家酒吧（[29112]、[29158]-[29176]）。（halloween2 后半续于下批。）

### 万圣节·前半（halloween2 后半—halloween6 前半）：Sara 亡子、Kaori 巷道崩坏与 USER2 现身

**halloween2 后半（[29177]-[29305]）——Sara 的亡子（else 版重磅设定）**。bonus 版 Sara 只担心找临时酒保（[29180]）；**else 版同一位置抛出炸弹**："Of course, if my son was never murdered you wouldn't be the {i}only{/i} boy. But that's a story for another day."（[29182]——**Sara 有一个被谋杀的儿子**）；else 版祝酒词变体"I want to get drunk and eat candy and feel pretty and talk about my son!"，Sensei 回"You can't talk about that until later in the game."（[29204]-[29205]——**剧情解锁进度再次被角色台词明示**）；else 版补刀"sar I miss him every day."（[29238]）。bonus 版是护士装坐腿调情+Haruka 吃醋戏，harukafirstlust 分支旁白暗示旧事（[29213]-[29236]）。**else 版旁白自曝未来视**：Sara 忘提找酒保的事，"but I know she needs one since {i}I am from the future.{/i}"（[29285]——叙事者承认自己来自未来）。结尾 Sensei 忘买 Chika 的午饭且忘了要什么（[29289]-[29291]，记忆小故障）。`$ halloween2 = True`、sara/haruka love+1（[29294]-[29296]）。

**halloween3 [29306]——Kaori 快餐店误会喜剧**。旁白自嘲镇上只有"like, twenty people I know"却总能偶遇（[29314]-[29316]）。Rin/Futaba 在 bistro 自制服装——去年的衣服尺码变了："Our sizes have, umm, kind of changed...so we were adjusting them." r"I have boobs now! It's sweet!"（[29345]-[29348]）。Kaori 以"Friend!"登场（[29381]），语言风格仍是异星人腔（"wisdom juices"[29391]）。关系误会大爆发：Kaori 一口咬定"We are in a relationship."（[28431]处应为[29431]），并宣布"All I will tell you is that it involves a lot of meat."（[29436]），进而"Only I can handle this man's meat. If you attempt to challenge me, you will be destroyed."（[29443]-[29444]）；Rin 兴奋失控要求"Now show"又自纠（[29470]-[29484]），Futaba 问到语无伦次。谜底只是汉堡上桌："feast your eyes upon the magnificence that is the Queen of Spiders!...The man is mine! The meat has come!"（[29524]-[29526]）。随后是罕见的温柔收梢：k"I just don't want you to get too many friends and forget about me."，旁白"Oh no. I think my heart might have just...felt something. I didn't realize it could do that."（[29566]-[29570]）。Kaori 披露曾开过 instant gram 又删号（[29584]-[29592]）。万圣节科普段：k"Why would we celebrate the dead? They are dead. They can not dance or consume dizzy-drinks."（[29633]），f 问"Is she even human?"，s"I honestly haven't figured that out yet."（[29634]-[29636]）。Kaori 应聘代班，报酬只要一次散步，并精确报出"In seventeen minutes and twenty-three seconds when I get off of work."（[29657]-[29664]，倒计时式时间感）。`$ halloween3 = True`（[29684]）。

**halloween4 [29690]——养鸡巷崩坏（USER2 终端事件，本章最大暗线节点）**。散步开场即异常伏笔：Kaori 说要"hundreds of years from now when I am finally prepared"才学会人类对话（[29712]），Sensei 以狗年数调侃（[29713]）——她的寿命尺度远超人类。医院果汁论（[29806]-[29813]）、贩卖人口玩笑（bonus"trafficking of the tiny girls"[29833]/else"trafficking"[29835]）。巷中鸡群真相是餐厅自养待宰，Sensei 冷冷补刀"The girl you met at the ramen shop did the same thing when she was little."（[29919]）。Kaori 问出全书级问题："If the chickens are going to be killed, why don't they just run away? Do they not fear death?"（[29915]-[29916]），再一句"Look harder."（[29947]）后——**不可归属的全大写声音接管文本**："PLAY ME A SONG THAT REMINDS ME OF BETTER DAYS...ALLOW ME TO TAKE ALL OF THOSE MEMORIES FROM YOU. I CAN FILL YOU WITH SUCH BEAUTIFUL THINGS. BETTER MEMORIES. BETTER EVERYTHING. ALL YOU NEED TO DO IS LISTEN. LISTEN TO ME AND NO ONE BUT ME. STOP LOOKING FOR ANSWERS. PLEASE. IT WILL ONLY CAUSE YOU PAIN. YOU'LL RELIVE ALL OF THOSE HORRIBLE THINGS. THE SCREECH OF TIRES. YOU SEE NOTHING."（[29954]-[29972]——**原始车祸创伤闪回：刹车声、"他跪下从'她'体内拔出钢条"**；同时是记忆替换的诱惑/威胁双关；四个肯定句与四个否定句对撞=记忆被反复改写的体验本身）。紧接机器红字六连："//////EXIT PATH NOT FOUND FOR CURRENT ROUTE / //////USER2 IS NOW REWRITING KEY EVENTS TO PREVENT APPLICATION FROM BEING TERMINATED / /////RESET REQUIRED / /////WORLD CAN ONLY BE PARTIALLY REVERTED / /////PLEASE CONTACT ADMINISTRATOR WITH ANY QUESTIONS / /////USER2 HAS LEFT"（[29973]-[29979]——**USER2 首次具名现身，正在"重写关键事件以防程序终止"；世界只能部分回滚；ADMINISTRATOR 概念首次出现**）。苏醒后 Kaori 称他昏倒了 23 秒（[30018]，她数的）、头着地很响却"没有 life-juice"（[30000]）；他自己"don't feel much of anything"（[30004]，痛觉缺失）；事后感受"There's a strange sensation somewhere in the back of my mind that feels like it's trying to claw its way out."（[30055]）。Kaori 决心用工资买下鸡群放生（[30027]-[30028]）。旗标三连：kaori_love+1、`$ halloween4 = True`、**`$ trinity3 = True`（[30065]-[30067]——trinity3 的旗标在此提前落位，正式 label 在[33739]）**。

**halloween5 [30075]——海豚装之夜**。开门见海豚（do 标签）：s"Oh dear God no."→do"Welcome home."（[29988]处应为[29992]附近，[30088]-[30092]）；他认真考虑脑震荡幻觉的可能性（[30096]-[30098]）。海豚摘头露出 Sana——**"She looks kind of like Sana, but with one extra eye."（[30123]——第三只眼意象一闪而过，随即归因为刘海）**；露脸的 Sana 让他一时认不出（[30132]-[30136]）。海豚装来源链：Sara←Makoto 的妈妈（Maki）（[30151]-[30155]）。Ayane 穿的是 Geoffrey 从墙上取下的中世纪铠甲（[30235]-[30236]）；Ami 是美少女战士，且**双马尾的起源是幼年时 Sensei 说美少女战士可爱**（[30214]-[30216]），Ayane 意识到这是求爱策略（[30221]-[30222]）。海豚装印有"S-S-Sexy Land"标志（[30269]-[30278]）。今晚还有恐怖片留宿，sanadorm30 分支里 Ayane 警觉"How...do you know that?
