# 第二章（ch2script.rpy）逐 label 详梳

## 第一章章节结构总览

ch2script.rpy 源文约 48,764 行、120 余个 label（含微型分支），叙事时间从**第三次重置之前的冬学期**一路推进到**第四次重置完成**。全章分八大块：

1. **冬学期日常与转学生潮**（day237—day318）：市区高中整体塌陷沉入地下（第一座天坑），学生分批转入；Uta、Io 先期入班，其后 Niki 相关线与 Touka、Yasu 等入住宿舍二楼；Wakana Watabe 从背景板升格为常驻同事角色。
2. **第一届宿舍战争**（dormwar1—17）：Uta 提议、Makoto 组织的十项对抗赛，中途插播 USER2 终端夺取事件。
3. **战后日常**（day333—day344）。
4. **第三次重置**（thirdreset1—3＋endofreset3loop＋kindergartenclass，内嵌 Survive!/Grow! 终端菜单）——本章最黑暗段落。
5. **重置后冬学期后半**（day351、dressingafter、day355）。
6. **第二次海滩旅行**（secondbeach1—18＋goodnightp2，穿插 kirinlust20／chikalust20／makotolust20 成人分支入口）。
7. **万圣节特别篇 II**（halloweentwo1—13＋amilust20）＋**goodboy** 童年隐喻特别篇。
8. **圣诞节特别篇 II**（christmastwo1—20）→ **第四次重置**（returntosummer1—3＋resetfour* 密室系列＋specialbonusamiscene）→ chap4intro 单句过渡。

体例注：以下方括号 [N] 均为 ch2script.rpy 源行号。另按任务要求，**christmas1—7**（第一次圣诞特别篇，位于 script.rpy [37773]起，叙事时间在 day220 之后、本章 day237 之前）一并定点核对展开，见第二章逐 label 详梳相应小节。

## 第二章逐 label 详梳

### 冬学期开幕与 Uta/Io 入班（day237—day247）

**day237 [1]——Wakana Watabe 登场与转学制度说明**。旁白交代背景：市区校塌陷后学生有两个选项——线上课堂或转入 [kumon_mi_high]（[11]-[14]），并吐槽两校同在 Kumon-mi 却只有一所叫这名字的荒诞（[15]-[16]）。Wakana 开场白即"I want to fucking die."（[35]），其虚无主义宣言"Things would have been so much easier if everyone just died when that stupid[school] sunk into the ground."（[54]-[55]）；两人当场缔结（玩笑式）自杀契约（[69]-[70]）。**姓氏回避再现**：她问"And how should I refer to you? Arakawa?"，Sensei 答"Just Sensei is fine."，随后四人份的省略号冷场（[104]-[112]——Arakawa 之姓由他人说出时他选择退回化名）。bonus 版她讽刺年轻男教师当老师的动机不言自明（[119]-[122]）。Makoto 抱来更大摞转学文件；else 版 Sensei 内心："does {i}everyone{/i} think I'm the huggy boy?"（[194]，huggy boy 已成全校谣言）。Wakana 自曝曾想挖走 Makoto 未遂（[201]-[203]）；班级规模对比 12 对 30（[204]-[205]）。预告第一名转学生 Uta Ushibori"not...the brightest"（[316]、[323]）；塌陷损失"Millions of Yen"，某富豪家庭已在重建母校（[348]-[349]）。`$ day237 = True`（[365]）。

**day239 [374]——Maya 的"从未如此变化"警告＋Uta 空降**。冬日异寒开场，"there's someone I know who reminds me a lot of that very specific and particular feeling"（[387]——那个人即 Maya）。Maya 主动约谈：圣诞后再无深谈（[396]）、天台之夜的自信毫无结果（[408]）。**本章第一条世界观硬线索**："Things have {i}changed{/i} before but...never like this."（[446]）；"An everlasting[school] year filled with the same old faces and the same old seating arrangements- That's all I've had for god-knows how long at this point."（[447]-[448]——**Maya 亲口承认自己经历过不知多少次"永恒学年"**）；自嘲"a glitch in the matrix"（[481]）。Sensei 把 reset 挂在嘴边："Is it really possible for things to go back to normal again after the next reset?"（[515]——**师生二人在日常闲聊中已把"重置"当作常识词汇**）。Maya 的鱼缸隐喻：新校会重建、人会像买新鱼缸填鱼一样回流，"metaphors...They're one of the few things that make everything seem more real."（[525]-[529]）。旁白紧随："Which is a strange thing for me to say given that nothing about this place is real..."（[532]-[533]）；以及断头句"Entire[school]s don't fall into the ground. But I guess when you don't know what's beneath you-"（[536]-[538]——**地下有什么，话到嘴边被切断**）。Uta Ushibori 高调登场（[544]-[549]）：认识 Maya，脱口"prin-"被喝止"Don't call me that"（[567]-[568]，女仆店称呼残留）；靠"Ami 的姓是 Arakawa……排除法"认出老师（[575]——**Arakawa 姓再次借角色之口复现**）；自曝见过 Sensei 与 Maya 在女仆咖啡厅密会并被托付保密（[604]-[605]）；bonus 版"My dad is like 15 years older than my mom"（[613]，年龄差家庭出身）；直球爆料"I'm pretty sure Ami has the hots for you"（[639]）。旁白收尾："I'd probably kill for her if she asked me to. That's an incredibly dangerous thing to admit"（[733]-[735]）。uta_love+1（[740]）。

**day240 [751]——Uta 班级自我介绍与 FLAVOR BEAM**。眼镜问答：她解释眼镜让眼睛显大、怕被当"bug-eyed Kansai freak"（[795]-[796]）；Nara 出身梗"I SHOULD HAVE STAYED IN NARA AND MARRIED A DEER!"（[834]）；正式介绍补充履历：数年前从 Nara 来 Kumon-mi 照顾爷爷、爷爷去年去世、现因 Kumon-mi 封锁回不了乡，只能打工女仆咖啡厅（[930]、[942]-[943]）。Yumi×Chika 暗涌值得记录：Chika 点评"He clearly likes more {i}mature{/i} girls."（[908]-[909]），else 版 Yumi 回敬黑色玩笑"Too bad you had your feet removed when you were little."（[914]）。招牌技"FLAVOR...BEEEEEAAAAAAAAAAM!!!!!!!!!!"（[993]）放倒全班（[1001]-[1019]，Tsuneyo"我在这头都能感到风味"、Makoto"I am now delicious"）。Yumi 应战腕力对决并以碾压获胜，Uta 手臂肌肉拉伤收场（[1032]-[1095]）。`$ day240`、uta_love+1（[1103]-[1105]）。

**day244 [1114]——Io 钱汤初见与"腹部的眼睛"**。Uta 以戳人战术求 Sensei 把好友 Io 转进本班（[1119]-[1148]）。Io 住在女仆店与学校之间的钱汤、阿姨是老板（[1253]-[1255]）。初见即被打量"Am I...being appraised right now?"（[1345]）。**关键记忆点**：Io 记得他曾包场男汤、身边有"that one blonde girl"（[1349]；bonus 版旁白补叙那是 Ayane，且当晚她状态不佳、两人只是背对背聊了两小时——[1466]-[1469]）。**else 版全书级异常描写**：Io 说"I really liked the secret eye on your abdomen and I want to learn more about it."（[1455]），Sensei 应"Oh great. So that's a thing you saw."（[1460]）；她解释是通过钱汤监控看到的（[1462]、[1471]"someone with an eye like that walks through the door"）——**Sensei 腹部有一只"秘密之眼"被监控拍到的设定在 else 版正面抛出**，与后续身体异变线互证。笔友起源：幼年网络笔友、中性网名导致互相猜错性别（[1503]-[1516]）。Io 自述随身带工具金属、可保护怕黑的 Uta（[1522]-[1523]）。`$ day244`。

**day246 [1592]——天台雪窗谈心**。午餐省钱计划顺带披露 Sensei 代付"the Chosokabes' phone bill"（[1600]——**Chosokabe 姓首次出现**，代付对象成谜）。三人转学交涉喜剧：Wakana"I have no pen. I will sign in my blood."（[1617]）；自封称号"Wailing Witch, Wakana Watabe"（[1668]）。宿舍情报：二楼空房极多却无人上搬（[1680]）、六号房 Molly 夜半喊叫、走廊永远一股拉面味（[1678]-[1679]）。Sensei 上天台找 Makoto 却撞见凭窗看雪的 Io（[1710]-[1722]）。Io 秘密论核心段："Stuff that eats away at you. Changes your perspective. Keeps you awake at night. Ruins your life, even."（[1808]-[1809]）；"keeping secrets is tiring. And seven hours is a long time."（[1831]）；喜欢 Uta 恰因她毫无秘密（[1840]-[1841]）。对 Sensei 的破防句："It's harder to erect walls when you're around."（[1868]-[1870]；else 版接"saying {i}walls{/i} made me think of your extra eye."（[1879]——**extra eye 第二次点名**）。自评"incredibly high maintenance in the lowest maintenance way possible"（[1858]），旁白存疑至段末（[1921]-[1927]）。io_love+1（[1932]）。

**day247 [1942]——"看我 assimilate"：Io 入班日**。入班前走廊课：Sensei 自述教学法就是"Show up and don't be an asshole"（[1952]），并透露 Yumi"has been getting better"而自己什么都没做（[1955]）。Io 大段自我否定："I'm trash. This[school] is trash. Everything is trash."（[2012]）；预告表演型融合："Assimilation isn't optional...I'll giggle and gossip the way good girls are groomed to...And never realize how broken I actually am."（[2042]-[2051]），随即"Watch me. Watch me assimilate."（[2053]-[2054]）。**旁白对玩家直接喊话（第二人称）**："If that were you up there...would you be happy? If you had knowingly informed someone that you were uncomfortable with yourself as a person and that someone immediately said 'Go show everyone who you really are,' you'd hate them, right?"（[2086]-[2087]）；接意象自白"Because I can not resign myself to a small wooden box. I must watch a lovely caterpillar squirm. Trying desperately to cocoon itself. And become something beautiful."（[2090]-[2098]）；"I give you another poem."（[2103]）；"Does this ever happen to you? If not, you live a life to be envied."（[2116]-[2117]）。自我介绍现场崩溃三连："I'm Io. I don't feel good. See you."（[2133]-[2135]）。事后修正案："Io specifically does not like girls."（[2186]）；project 论：了解人=接下他人的负担，"I think it's easier to just stay away from people like that."（[2207]-[2225]）；最痛一句"You can throw me away. I'll probably cry for a day or two...Worse things have happened to me."（[2246]-[2248]）；请求"Don't let me break."（[2302]-[2305]）。**旁白定性判词**："Io Ichimonji does not exist. She has already lost herself."（[2314]-[2315]——姓氏 Ichimonji 首次出现，"不存在/已失去自我"为 Io 线定调）。系统播报 Uta/Io 入住二楼七号房（[2323]-[2325]）。io_love+1。

### 转学生潮主体：Wakana 日常、Kanda 姐妹与 Nakayama 姐妹（day261—day271）

**day261 [2339]——Wakana 办公室日常与 Osako 揭底**。开场三连"norm"自白（[2346]-[2348]）。新一批转学生情报：Wakana 班里被塞进"an anarchist and a girl who does nothing but write haiku"（[2406]——无政府主义者与俳句少女，后者即后续 Nodoka）。旁白对 Makoto 的存在性自信："I'm just so confident in her being around forever that the thought of her somehow disappearing has never even occurred to me. Obviously not counting when the world ends...But pretty much {i}everyone{/i} disappears for that"（[2412]-[2415]——**世界终结=所有人消失被当作日常常识随口说出**）。核心爆料：Wakana 正与空手道教练 **Osako**（女性）交往，Ayane 目击过光天化日之吻（[2514]-[2528]）；道场即将被"the other...rich girl"买下（[2600]-[2609]，Ayane 淡定"if her dad thinks owning a dojo will make her happy...I can't blame him for trying"——富豪家再添一笔）。班级人数"Fourteen is a weird number"（[2665]）。bonus 版结尾旁白总结三条 takeaway（[2699]-[2704]）。ayane_love+1。

**day263 [2727]——Kirin 宣布转入（蓄谋已久）**。Kanda 姐妹放学"打劫"喜剧开场（[2732]-[2772]，Karin 全程社恐装死、"soccer sisters"称号诞生）。Kirin 自曝转学轨迹：年初在 Wakana 班被立刻踢出→C 班故意捣乱→"by process of elimination"轮到 Sensei 班（[2807]-[2809]）；宣言级台词"I, Kirin Kanda, have been intentionally provoking teachers to try and wind up in your class since the first time I heard Miku talk about you."（[2818]）。Karin 临别反常：不再逃跑而是长久凝视，只留一句"Please watch over her."（[2895]-[2912]）。Kirin 的自我怀疑段：被 Sensei 点破"总在一起会腻"后失神"Holy shit. What if you're right? I didn't even think about that."（[2968]-[2976]），随即恼羞"Stop understanding me all of a sudden!"（[2984]）；kirinlust5+bonus 分支补刀"You've uncovered several parts of me before."（[2990]-[2993]）。宿舍计划：入班后搬宿舍与 Miku 同楼（[3108]-[3109]）。kirin_love+1。

**day264 [3166]——Karin 的质问（三分支抉择 label）**。诗化开场：长椅、少女、猫、草，"And underneath the grass, things we can not fathom...Dirt and worms."（[3171]-[3177]；bonus 版补刀"the younger one, the one who is not here, buries hers in something else"[3181]——不在场的 Kirin 被暗示性描写）。Karin 为等妹妹/等他在寒风里站了两小时（[3323]）。旁白直接点破"(She loves me.)"（[3410]），并留删除线自白"{s}I wish I had someone like that.{/s}"（[3378]）。核心问题"Sensei...are you...{i}involved{/i} with Kirin?..."（[3446]）。**三分支**：①谎称无兴趣——bonus 版系统腔"{i}Congratulations! You are a liar!{/i}{i}Let's hope Karin never finds out you came in her sister's mouth!{/i}"（[3525]-[3526]）/else 版"Congratulations! You are the huggy boy!"（[3528]），`$ karinlied = True`（[3531]）；②坦白——"Chances are they've already happened."（[3551]）、"'With' her. Not to her. That's a completely different thing."（[3564]-[3565]），Karin 崩溃"How is that fair?...I...haven't even..."（[3603]-[3605]），旁白道德判决"honesty is the best policy- They're going to really hurt someone one day...too high up on the pedestals they craft of their comrades' bones to ever even notice."（[3613]-[3615]）+系统腔"{i}Karin's affection somehow increases...{/i}{i}But she cries the whole way home.{/i}{i}She really liked you.{/i}"（[3661]-[3663]），`$ karinlied = False`（[3656]）；③逃跑——摔一跤溜走，"likely ruining the context of future events in the process"（[3673]-[3677]，**旁白明说选择会破坏后续事件语境**）。

**day269 [3693]——Noriko Nakayama 登场（本章最大炸弹之一）**。开场旁白即定调："the result of an unfortunate twist in an otherwise unbending cycle"（[3699]）；Sensei 前一天把 Kirin 转班的事告诉 Maya，"just as expected, something like this has never happened before"（[3701]——**"从未发生过的事"接连出现，循环世界的异常化**）。Kirin 入班介绍（ayanelust10 分支：Ayane 质问未获预警，Kirin 把锅甩给批准转班的 Sensei，[3748]-[3783]）；与 Io 的初次对话灾难（[3805]-[3830]，Io"Please don't. I am not worth it."）。随后**粉发少女（q 标签）冲入**，直呼 Wakana 名字、对 Sensei 连环发问"When? How long? Why?...I missed you so much..."（[3884]-[3909]）；"When was the last time we saw each other?"——"Almost three years ago. It was on a Thursday. But you disappeared once before that, too."（[3960]-[3963]）。**旁白的存在论自白（全书关键）**："This has to be someone Sensei knew before I took over and started ruining all of his relationships. Or fixing them, depending on your ethical standpoint..."（[3950]-[3952]）；"If I was the person I'm supposed to be- or if {i}he{/i} was the person {i}he{/i} was supposed to be instead of some memory floating out there in the ether- This would be a touching reunion."（[3953]-[3955]——**叙述者亲口承认"我接管了这个身体"**）。Sensei 决定打失忆牌"That's been my ace in the hole so far"（[3970]-[3971]）；Noriko 的反应不是崩溃而是"Nothing hurts anymore...If your memories are gone, I'll just figure out a way to get them back."（[4005]-[4007]）。自报家门："My name is Noriko Nakayama and I have been in love with you since I was six years old."（[4022]）；**Chika 听到姓氏的失态**："{i}Nakayama?...{/i}{i}...No. There's no way.{/i}"（[4028]-[4029]——Chika 与 Nakayama 家有旧）；Ami 认出她"like, [niece] Ami?!"（[4044]）。**Maya 的爆发（本 label 顶点）**：小字"Why. Why. Why?"渐强到"WHY ARE YOU HERE?!"（[4066]-[4075]；else 版"I AM DESTINED TO BE THE HUGGY GIRL! NOT YOU!"[4078]）；归因于塌陷的学校"That stupid...{b}FUCKING{/b}[school] that decided to {b}SINK{/b} and turn everything on its {b}GOD DAMN{/b}...{b}FUCKING HEAD!!!{/b}"（[4086]）；逐客宣言"You were unwanted {i}then{/i}, and you are unwanted {i}now{/i}. No one will ever...in this timeline or others...{i}ever{/i} love you."（[4133]-[4134]）——旁白当场卡住："Timeline?...Then-"（[4136]，**"timelines"复数从 Maya 口中泄出**）。Noriko 随即以转学生身份正式入班（[4170]-[4174]），Kirin 立刻表示欣赏（[4175]）；Wakana 为她申请"emergency transfer"（[4182]）；旁白预言"Proclaiming your love for me in front of everyone is probably not the best way to introduce yourself...I'm sure she'll pay the price for that in the future."（[4190]-[4191]）。`$ noriko_love += 50`（[4201]——**+50 的异常增量**）。

**day270 [4214]——Noriko 的往事陈述＋Maya 的警告（重置信息密度最高的日常 label）**。开场旁白："when I say 'my' life I mean 'Sensei's' life because, as I was reminded via brute force yesterday, it is very much not my own."（[4222]）。Noriko 办公室戏：眼泪存货论（[4252]-[4255]）、内裤藏刀（[4305]-[4307]）。**往事三层披露**：①" We were neighbors for a really long time. Back when you lived in the old district."（[4330]-[4331]）；②"You came over to hang out with my sister."（[4372]——他当年是为了姐姐来的）；③"after that...thing happened, you just stopped coming over"（[4381]），旁白自动补全"She's talking about the accident, right? The one that forced Ami's parents out of this world long before they were meant to leave."（[4386]-[4387]）；但 Noriko 纠正："No, I saw you plenty of times after that. That was just the {i}first{/i} time you disappeared."（[4396]——**"第一次消失"**）。家教师往事（[4397]-[4399]）；主观时间膨胀"I can't help like feel it's been way...{i}way{/i} longer. Almost like I've been searching for you for an eternity."（[4407]-[4408]）；宣战词"You stole my heart all those years ago and now I have to steal yours."（[4421]）。**Maya 强行入场后的正面对决**：她先赶走 Noriko，再要求 Sensei 两三个月别来学校，理由冷得发亮"You'd get it back once the world resets. Consider it a vacation."（[4495]——**reset 再次被当作日常词汇**）；"She's not supposed to be here, so I never considered {i}having{/i} to explain myself!"（[4504]）；Sensei 反问"What's so dangerous about me learning more about the old Sensei's backstory when I'm so clearly not him?"（[4506]）。Maya 的三句核心自白："Spending so much time inside of loops clearly has some adverse side effects."（[4523]）；"You made it to winter. Do you have any idea how many 'you's' have made it to winter?"（[4538]——**历届 Sensei 存活计数暗示**）；"I'm trying to preserve {i}your{/i} life. Noriko can ruin everything much quicker than you think she can."（[4552]-[4553]）。被逼问后罕见认怂"I'm very scared."（[4565]-[4568]）。旁白的盲fold-悬崖比喻收束："we don't even know the cliff is a cliff yet...I am a man who understands the sheer harm that simply knowing something can cause."（[4578]-[4584]）。Maya 离场前留下斜体箴言"{i}Sometimes, forcing people to do things they're not comfortable with is the best way to move them forward.{/i}"→"My own philosophies spell my downfall once again."→"It's a spiral."（[4605]-[4608]）；她的手"the closest I've ever seen her hands to shaking"（[4615]）。maya_love+1。

**day271 [4633]——Niki Nakayama：偶像、旧情人与电话号码**。旁白黑暗微笑开场："I'm excited to watch everyone grow. To watch them shrink. To watch them bend. And break. And bleed."（[4645]-[4649]）。与 Ami 同行：Ami 模糊记忆"might vaguely remember a play date with some girl when I came over to visit once...I think her hair was a different color, though."（[4714]-[4715]），Sensei 以"it's physically impossible for a girl to change her hair color"反讽带过（[4716]——**发色变化暗示**）；"Mega Ami"自我命名（[4737]-[4749]）。Noriko 拽他去看姐姐——录音棚里正拍可口可乐广告的**Niki Nakayama**（[4913]-[4924]）；见面即暴怒三连"WHAT THE FUCK ARE YOU DOING HERE?! ### 俳句少女与"绑架任务"（day280—day281 前半）

**day280 [5130]——Nodoka Nagasawa 登场**。开场黑色幽默：又一名校工死于体育课，女生们早已脱敏、借机延长社团时间（[5137]-[5140]——**校工死亡=循环世界的日常损耗，与 day138"每次弄坏都能换新校工"互证**）。Futaba 引荐 **Nodoka Nagasawa**（[5162]；else 版她连初见都忘了"I forget."）；即 day261 预告的俳句少女（[5204]-[5206]，因一天俳句被 Wakana 永久记恨）；**Kumon-mi 全镇最高标准化考试分数**持有者（[5219]）；以 [incest] 属性自居并引 Poe《Annabel Lee》称 Sensei"my dearest Annabel Lee"（[5290]）。**操纵论对话（角色间少有的直球）**：Sensei 公开承认"it's a little unreasonable to rule out manipulation as a means of getting things you want from people...Manipulation is a way of life."（[5327]-[5345]），Futaba 失语，Nodoka 反手质疑"whether or not you've been manipulating my darling Futaba this whole time"（[5361]-[5362]）；Futaba 仍辩护"He genuinely cares about all of us."（[5364]-[5365]）。预告"mission"需要一支"squadron"（[5253]-[5255]）。nodoka_love+1。

### "绑架任务"与 Otoha/Yasu/Touka 转入（day281—day318）

**day281 [5479]——"Kidnapping"：Nodoka 的任务说明书**。开场 Sensei 自嘲教学观就是"放养"，bonus 版甚至幻想最终条件"could just be sleeping with her or something...Thanks again, Kirin."（[5494]-[5496]）。else 版他预想条件是"A hug."（[5519]）；年龄梗回收："If it's my age, I'm delighted to inform you that I know it now."→no"Congratulations, Sensei. I'm very proud of you."（[5535]-[5536]——**day269/270 之后他终于"知道自己的年龄"，由 Nodoka 一句祝贺盖章**）。正题抛出只有一个词："How menial?"→no"**Kidnapping.**"→s"No."（[5561]-[5563]）。目标：绑架 **Otoha Okakura** 入班，把学校改造成"the ultimate yuritopia"（[5626]，r"Yuritopia!"）——Rin 的执念首次成体系登场。动机层：Otoha 被"困在笼中"——else 版明写"her parents tricked her into signing away her rights via conservatorship to them, kind of like what happened with Britney Spears."（[5670]——**现实事件指涉**；bonus 版改为 homeschooling 拖垮 GPA）。Noriko 闻讯即入伙"I know Otoha is involved and that's enough to count me in."（[5617]），并自曝与 Otoha"used to be in a band together"（[5635]）。Nodoka 的 Plan B："If she vehemently refuses to comply, I am not against binding her and tossing her into the back of your car."→s"I don't have a car."（[5676]-[5677]）。五条电话线梗再现"My plan maxes out at five lines and I'm already there."（[5717]）。收尾发明"Nodoka points"货币系统："The Nodoka currency. You can use them to redeem Nodoka rewards."（[5764]-[5765]），余额五点；旁白"And with the class on the brink of being entirely full- I'm sure things will only get weirder..."（[5787]-[5788]——**"班级即将满员"=本章转学生潮的终点预告**）。`$ nodoka_love += 1`、`$ day281 = True`（[5791]-[5792]）→jump afterschoolevent。

**day282 [5802]——雪中公园策反 Otoha**。Rin 因预约缺席，Sensei 独与 Nodoka 同行，自我安慰"This is the most exciting answer, so it is the one that I will go with."（[5814]——叙述者公开承认按"最有趣"而非"最真实"取用现实）。Nodoka 点破处境的荒诞："You're a completely normal adult accompanying a [young_girl] miles away to meet up with another [young_girl] and potentially kidnap her."（[5832]——变量标签直接嵌入台词）。她的天才论/自白："We're just two cute girls who find interesting ways to rearrange words or sounds, and yet we're regarded as geniuses by many of our peers."（[5850]）；被问"Who are you then?"答"I don't know."（[5854]-[5855]——又一个"不存在/未定义"的角色）；自称 matchmaker 或 seer，使命是"To observe the dynamics between others. To write them down."（[5905]-[5908]——**Nodoka＝观察者/记录者定位，与 Io 的秘密论、Maya 的循环观测互为镜像**）。Otoha 登场（o 标签）：Nodoka 开场诗"I'm just here to help a bird with broken wings out of its cage."（[5968]）。策反话术核心＝成绩保险："Transfer into my class and you can do whatever you want, whenever you want."（[6062]）；s"If there is one thing you can trust me with, it is your grades. That's the one area I've yet to let anyone down in so far."（[6146]-[6147]）。Otoha 提到 Rin"messages me like ten thousand times a day...Kinda like a...puppy waiting for its master to come home"（[6027]、[6031]——puppy 意象再添一例）。Nodoka 补刀洗脑"Mend your broken wings and fly higher than you've ever flown before."（[6127]）直接逼签："Okay! I'm very eager to meet her and knock her off her high horse."（[7115]）；Sensei 泄露已了解 Yumi 过往引发 Rin 惊讶"Has anyone other than like...Chika ever done that before?"（[7129]-[7130]）。Futaba 提出健身房计划（为海滩旅行瘦身，[7166]"even if it's the winter and there won't be any bathing suits"——冬季海滩二次确认）。Nodoka 伪造杂务室绯闻逗弄 Futaba（else 版改为擦地板，[7223]-[7225]）。**Nodoka points 世界观扩展**："Everyone has a secret currency for everyone else whether they're aware of it or not...I was just smart enough to assign a separate numeric value to mine."（[7384]-[7386]——**好感度系统的 diegetic 化宣言**）。四女集体提出"你也可以向我们倾诉"的干预式关怀（[7353]-[7358]）。结尾旁白 bonus 版"Even if it's a change I've been feeling time and time again lately...Life sure is getting exciting, isn't it?"（[7414]-[7416]——"一次次感到同样的变化"=循环疲劳闪现）。四 love 各+1、`$ day288 = True`（[7422]-[7426]），系统解锁"Nodoka 和 Otoha 校外时间"。

**day295 [7440]——Tsubasa Tsukioka 到访：最富家族的委托**。Chika 坐桌闲聊引出转学生 gossip；Makoto 带访客入场，其人对 Sensei 的称呼是"The 'WAP' man?..."（[7575]；else 版 Chika 释义"It means {i}wonderful and polite.{/i}"[7580]——**全年龄版把脏话缩写改写成 wholesome 词**）。来者即 day269/283 铺垫的最富家族之母 **Tsubasa Tsukioka**：因天坑事故决定让女儿转入，且点名只要 Sensei 班（[7630]、[7637]）；理由是宠物动物园长谈中女儿对他"the way you make her feel"的评价（[7664]-[7670]）。人物设定补完："My daughter does not have a very easy time...opening up to people."（[7676]）、已为女儿在本地购置第二处小宅（[7718]-[7720]）、不知"宿舍"为何物（[7724]-[7726]）。Makoto 自嘲"I guess the days of me being the smartest girl in[school] are over now, huh?"（[7656]）。`$ day295 = True`→jump day295parttwo。

**day295parttwo [7761]——雪夜白裙少女：第三位神使（sg3）初遇**。本 label 是本章第一个纯元叙事重头。开场即异常：路灯蜂鸣让他想起最近听过却记不起的声音；抬头走路不看脚下；随后旁白直接对玩家布展："Actually, since we're on the topic of art, there's something I'd like you to look at. To spread the word of {s}GOD{/s} {s}god{/s} {s}GoD{/s} God and to bathe in his light."（[7987]——三连删除线＝名称本身不稳定）；"The things I do with my body and mind are not of my own choice. **day297 [8116]——Touka Tsukioka 入班：名字战争**。开场 Ayane 养鸡 Todd 轶事＋Nodoka 占座发呆一小时（[8129]）。Uta 定价玩笑"I am far too [young]to get involved with a man like you unless the price is right."→"Six million US dollars."（[8142]-[8147]）。正题：Uta/Ayane 预告"需要 Sensei 帮忙的计划"且必须等"playing field"拉平（[8240]-[8243]——宿舍战争伏笔）；Uta 授予他"cute girl sense"，系统腔盖章"{i}You have maybe(?) acquired Uta-chan's second secret ability, cute girl sense!{/i}"（[8258]）。**Touka 登场即称其"the commoner I ran into at the dojo months prior"**（[8270]——道场初遇回收）；Otoha 情报：其家族拥有全区约 10%地产、正在出资重建塌陷的原校（"until our last[school] was...unearthed or rebuilt or whatever"[8320]-[8322]——**富豪家=重建母校的金主，接 day237 伏笔**），并断言"I give it like a week before she's gone again."（[8328]）。Sensei 以故意叫错名字（Tooru/Tomoko/Toshiko/Tama）反击特权感，核心台词"Being treated like everyone else isn't {i}abuse{/i}, Tooru."（[8389]）；Touka 爆破式自报"TOUKA TSUKIOKA OF THE TSUKIOKA FAMILY! THE WEALTHIEST FAMILY IN KUMON-MI!"（[8401]）。Tsubasa 现场观摩两分钟即满意离去，托付司机接送（fencing 课程地点"remote location that only our driver is familiar with"[8538]——神秘课程再添一笔）；Touka 小声致谢后炸毛逃进座位（[8546]-[8553]）。Ayane 以"rich girl senses"完成接纳（[8559]-[8560]）。`$ touka_love += 1`。

**day302 [8582]——Touka 的抗议面谈＋亚里士多德瞬间**。Touka 投诉一周无教学；秘书梗："I had my secretary run a background check on you and confirmed that you have an extensive history in literature"（[8648]——**旧 Sensei 的文学履历经背景调查正式落地**）；Tsubasa 补刀妹妹 Tsukasa 也有秘书，s"Well it makes sense for Tsukasa because she is a 5,000 year old wizard."（[8657]——Tsukasa 第二次被怪诞化引用，见 day304 再现）。**全书级小事件**：Tsubasa 引 Aristotle 劝导，Touka 反问出处，s脱口"Aristotle."→内心"How {i}did{/i} I know that?"（[8693]-[8703]——**旧人格知识渗漏的自察时刻**）。Tsubasa 教养宣言"{i}express yourself like the common people, but think like a wise man.{/i}"（[8688]）。宿舍方案抛出：二楼仅剩一间，"you'll be the only person living there until the final student transfers in"（[8811]——末位转学生=Yasu 官宣预告）。Touka 以"understand the laymen"为由自愿入住（[8774]-[8775]）。`$ day302 = True`、touka_love+1。

From the moment we are born to the moment we get cancer and die, every day is a test to see if we're fit to survive."[9649]-[9650]）；Io 吐槽教学现状"We're still two of the newest girls in the[school] and even {i}we{/i} should know that teaching is at the very bottom of his list of interests."（[9616]-[9617]）。正题：Ayane/Uta 提出楼层对抗赛，Makoto 先拒后被动出任一楼队长（[9829]-[9855]）；奖品揭晓"THE WINNER GETS SENSEI!"——胜方楼层可在海滩旅行期间留他过夜（[9871]-[9876]）；Maya 第一反应"Is it too late to drop out of the contest?"、Otoha"Yeah, I can sit this one out too."（[9873]-[9874]）；Noriko 参战宣言"I'm {i}going{/i} to win and I'm {i}going{/i} to get all up in his business"遭 Chika 威慑"Lay so much as a finger on him and you will die."（[9887]-[9892]）。else 版 Ami 抛出"contract clause"说辞（[9906]）。元层系统公告明示规则可控性："{i}Welcome to the first annual Super Mega Ultimate Dorm War!{/i}{i}Some events will have predetermined outcomes, but several of them have outcomes that you will be able to choose!{/i}"（[9931]-[9934]）。`$ day318 = True`→jump dormwar1。

### 第一届宿舍战争（dormwar1—17）：Sensei 被收回行动权的两天

十项一对一对决贯穿周六/周日：每项由 Sensei 任唯一裁判、胜方楼层得一分，奖品是海滩旅行同房夜。表层是恋爱喜剧锦标赛，底层埋着三件事——**Yasu 的邪教神"祂"、Rin 的自伤伏笔、以及结尾揭示的"赛事期间 Sensei 一直被系统代管"**。

**dormwar1 [9936]—开幕式·十项赛程公布**。抽签分组后公布全部十项：wallflower 合作赛（Io/Yumi）、知识赛（Makoto/Nodoka）、试胆（Maya/Tsuneyo）、运动（Kirin/Miku）、哥特萝莉时装秀（Sana/Yasu）、friend zone 战（Rin/Otoha）、说唱（Uta/Ayane）、桌游（Futaba 代打 Sana 的独臂兽人"Zagull Throat Spear"对 Molly）、约会大赛（Chika/Touka，Chika 以 seedy shop 把柄半胁迫 Makoto 放行）、妹妹战争（Ami/Noriko）。**关键暗线：宣布 Uta/Makoto 年龄时出生年份双双被 [[redacted] 音效屏蔽，而 Rin 当场问出 "did anyone else hear them redact the year they were born?"（[9973]-[9974]，else 版）——redact 是在场的部分角色能实际"听见"的声音事件**。else 版另有一处直呼平台名的众筹玩笑。

**dormwar2 [10105]—赛前造势·假新闻双线**。Molly/Ayane 在宿舍自办"无插电麦克风新闻采访"，两楼各推候选人；Noriko 对镜头玩笑"I am going to kill Ami."。Yasu 完整头衔现身："Priestess of the Night...Great Old One - He who sees with no eyes at all, yet all of the eyes in the universe."（[10652]-[10653]）。Ami 参赛宣言近乎设定直述："If it involves Sensei, I will win. That is my role in not only his life, but my own."（[10858]-[10859]）。Io 开始失踪。

**dormwar3 [10913]—妹妹战争·咖啡下药回合**。开场 Sensei 之梦（本章最重要梦象之一）：异色天空下无边花海坟场，夏与冬在一念间交替、墓碑随之风化，最大的一座坟里埋着 "someone very important to me...Or was buried here."（[10921]-[10931]）；被呼唤声 "...sei?" 唤醒。早餐回合 Ami 现场编造孤儿院身世博同情"It's just like at the orphanage...that mean family wanted to adopt me and use me for all sorts of gross things...but you stepped in and saved me..."（[11074]-[11079]，真伪不明）；Noriko 在 Sensei 咖啡里下药反击（else 版明写 sleeping potion，[11129]；bonus 版另有分支去向，不展开）。菜单选择支持 Ami 或 Noriko，败者以 pizza rolls 自嘲收场（[11166]-[11174]）。

**dormwar4 [11180]—公园运动会**。Karin 裁判三段制：短跑/腕力/plank。Kirin 坚持腕力轮并以舔手阴招获胜（[11393]-[11416]）；plank 心理战反噬——Miku 一句耳语承认"I have. Just once, though."令 Kirin 当场崩盘（[11590]-[11601]）。支线：Otoha 向 Yumi 剖白对 Sensei 的隔膜"there's something about him that almost everyone else sees that I just...don't see yet"（[11475]）；Molly 反常沉默疲倦（[11485]-[11487]，伏笔）。一楼得分（[11642]）。

**dormwar5 [11650]—cafe·friend zone 战**。Sensei 需让 Rin 或 Otoha 动心：Rin 开局即逐客"Any of {i}that ass{/i}. Now get out of here."（[11852]）；Nodoka 献策"Promise her something she {i}does{/i} want."并自称"extremely in tune with the heart of the bisexual [adolescent] girl"（[12106]-[12108]）——Sensei 冒充 Otoha 提议"三人拥抱"，Rin 秒答"When and where?！"当场认输，还顺带承认对 Sensei 有好感、理想对象本是 Kaori（[12156]-[12163]）。插叙：Uta 苦劝 Io 参赛无果，Io 反刺"If you really want to sleep with Sensei, there are much easier ways of doing it!"（[11884]-[11904]）；Haruka 发现 Molly 没法看这场比赛、单独带她出去谈心（[11985]-[12014]）；Ayane/Touka 谈家族史——Ayane 家靠父亲的气泡膜专利脱贫（[12038]-[12040]）。二楼得分（[12212]）。

**dormwar6 [12220]—图书馆知识赛·Nodoka 碾压**。Ami 的选手介绍全是胡编（蹼趾、"Nodoka 怀了 Futaba 的孩子"，后者被 Nodoka一本正经认领，[12275]-[12277]）。Nodoka 先故意连错（"Chuck E. Cheese？""Portobello mushrooms？"），随后火力全开：抢答 Makoto 题目的世界最老盆栽学名 encephalartos altensteinii（[12448]）、口算 584÷39=14.974（[12545]），最终以"脑冻结"学名 sphenopalatine ganglioneuralgia 终结比赛，9:4（[12593]、[12567]）。Otoha 解释她一贯藏拙、其实酷爱聚光灯（[12462]-[12465]）；Makoto 认输感言"She played with me before wiping the floor with me."，并留下重要台词——Sensei 的时间"essentially its own form of currency"（[12407]-[12410]，与 Nodoka points 的货币化互文）。Ayane/Touka 已把圣诞旅行住过的旅馆整栋订好（[12636]-[12637]）。二楼得分（[12647]）。

**dormwar7 [12655]—maid cafe 说唱战**。Ayane 先手（"A butler named Geoffrey, a chicken named Todd. A boyfriend named Sensei, who's more like my god."[12910]；"so many guns that you'd think I'm from Texas"[12915]——军火库梗）；Uta 接管话筒后词汇量暴增、押韵密度陡增，Maya 当场指出异常"More people should be talking about Uta's sudden extreme increase in vocabulary. That was weird."（[13083]），Uta 自称"a reincarnated rap god that's finally set free"（[12987]）。Ayane 大度认输（[13080]）。支线：Wakana 正式邀请 Sana 转入自己班级（[12818]-[12820]）。分支门：若 ayanelust10==True 且 ayane_lust≥15 且 kirin_lust≥15，Kirin 把 Ayane 拉到一边耳语提议"Just the once and I won't bother you about it anymore."／"That's what you said last time."（[13172]-[13173]）→jump ayanelust15（成人向分支，不展开）；否则 ayanelust15skip=True→jump dormwar8。

**dormwar8 [13246]—旅馆首夜·"太阳系"独白**。Touka 五件行李与其母的住宿规矩；房间分配：二楼 24 房、一楼 25 房、Sensei 被分到走廊尽头的 44 房——Noriko 听他猜 23 号房时回"I can see why you would have guessed that, though."（[13366]-[13368]）。Maya 连发警告：说他是"dead opossum walking into a vultures' nest"（[13473]）、自己只是想"have some fun before the next reset plunges Kumon-mi into the ocean"（[13478]——**再次对 Sensei 半公开谈论重置**）、睡前记得锁门（[13490]）。当夜 Sensei 独白急转直下："Their lives belong to them and them only...they've centered themselves around me. Each one of them is a celestial body orbiting around my existence. I am the center of the solar system. I will take. And I will take. And I will take. And I will grow....Time is unlimited. Until it's not."（[13752]-[13767]）——与 thirdreset1 的 Survive/Grow 与"callous"主题同源。Tsuneyo 收尾一问"What does it mean to be human, Green Onion? What does it mean to be?..."（[13708]-[13709]）。Ayane、Ami、Kirin、Io 四人集体失踪过夜（[13688]-[13690]）。分支：同 dormwar7 条件→jump ayanelust15；否则 jump dormwar9。

**dormwar9 [13792]—深夜游荡·镜子与 Yuki**。开篇镜像独白："I look in the mirror...what I see is no reflection of myself. It's a pair of disembodied hands, sifting through the hair of someone who does not exist. They look soft. They smell like cherry blossoms."（[13817]-[13820]）；挥拳砸镜后"I run it under hot water for 23 seconds and discover that there is no cut and no blood. There is nothing."（[13849]-[13851]——**"There is nothing."正是 chap4intro 的全文**，且"23 秒"再踩终端 23 数字）。bonus 版补刀式旁白：等自己"recycled into some other person's body"、或等世界停止重置（"you'd have to ask Maya"，[13839]-[13840]）。街头偶遇 Yuki：刚参加 NA（戒断互助）会、明早要去一家"快倒闭的酒吧"面试（[13925]、[14085]-[14086]——即 Sara 的店，dormwar16 揭晓）；她谈 Yumi 童年只能一个人玩、"she just wants to be included in something"（[13998]-[14000]），并断言"They're just [teenager]s."（[14057]）。else 版收束旁白竟以守护者口吻+self 祝福词收尾："I'm just a guy following closely behind them on some wooded trail, making sure they make it home without getting hurt."→"Praise be."（[14070]-[14072]）。Nodoka/Futaba 夜访旅馆房间（Nodoka 已从 Futaba 处听说两人关系，放话若伤到 Futaba 就"wakizashi 剖腹"，[14208]）。分支：futaba_lust≥15 且 nodokadorm5==True→jump futabalust15（成人向分支，不展开）；否则 futabalust15skip=True。日期推进代码出现错误兜底串"ERROR ADVANCING TO SUNDAY"（[14292]-[14298]）。

**dormwar10 [14302]—Io 潜入·Chika 早间事件**。晨起独白："The higher something is, the safer it is. So we should all ascend and ascend..."（[14319]-[14321]，与 Yasu 的末日登高布道同构）；"It is not wonderful to be. But I am."（[14327]-[14329]）。Io 谎称"他是我爸"从前台骗到钥匙、睡了一夜地板（[14345]）；被问如何维系这么多人际关系时 Sensei 脱口"Easy. Time is unlimited."，Io 反驳时间有限、"Cherish every second. Celebrate what it means to live. Vote Io."（[14426]-[14431]）。Chika 一早来敲门（自称"being your girlfriend gives me a preeeeeetty big advantage"于今日比赛，[14583]），隔墙被 Io 听见"girlfriend"；Io 爆发"{i}Her?{/i} {i}That{/i} was your choice? A generic tanned, bleach blonde gyaru?"（[14645]-[14647]），话锋扫过"that other girl who you already-"被打断（[14653]）。事后旁白自嘲"walk the tightrope that is adultery (Or at least partial adultery since we're not dating in my eyes)"（[14767]）；else 版以"腹部秘密之眼+金鱼献祭"胡话消化尴尬（[14772]-[14775]）。

**dormwar11 [14785]—Route 69 Diner·D&D 对决**。Molly 的僧武僧 Alentha Amastacia（Raven Queen 侍者）对战 Futaba 代打的独臂 Zagull：Molly 开场白"traverse time itself and exist in multiple dimensions at once!"（[14806]）。店中撞见 Niki 与 Kaori（热狗摊喜剧）；Niki 自曝高中漫画部打过 D&D（[14887]）、并称如今的自己是"dated him 的那个人的greatly improved version"（[15124]）。Futaba 用 Rin 的骰子掷出"幸运数字"翻盘失败，Alentha 残血反杀（[15373]-[15374]）；Sensei 手机在没充电器的情况下自行复活（"How is it alive again?"，[15410]）。二楼得分（[15434]）。

**dormwar12 [15445]—Chika 家庭日期赛·"我必须拥抱全世界"**。赛前 Uta 与 Yumi 的假新闻采访给了 Chika 绰号"Queen of Hearts"（[15494]）。Chika 家阳台早餐约会被妹妹 Chinami 以"Papa!"全程司仪（[15555]）；餐桌椅是 Chika 当天早上和 Yumi 捡废品拼来的——"You do so much for me. The least I can do is make you feel like you belong."（[15639]）。Sensei 内心独白全面黑化："Because I can't be satisfied by just one beautiful girl. I need all of them."（else 版作"I am the huggy boy. I must hug the whole world."[15659]-[15674]）；bonus 版更推演出平行人生：带 Chika 私奔、毕业、结婚、老去、下葬在相邻的墓穴里（[15664]-[15671]，抽象转述）。随后是全书最直白的自毁宣言之一："O, world. Why must I destroy everything I touch?"（[15715]-[15716]）、"I will hurt this girl more times than she can bear. I will ruin everything. I will destroy her world. But I will not have wanted any of it. I do not change. But a part of me does. Or did."（[15731]-[15737]）；以及对 Chika 式爱的判词"For the kind of love that Chika both has and wants is the kind that exists to be traded between two entities. And that kind of love does not exist to me. Nothing exists to me. I wish it did."（[15743]-[15746]）。一楼得分（[15771]-[15772]）。

**dormwar13 [15781]—Touka 高级餐厅日期赛·注定落败的一方**。Maki 采访 Tsubasa：丈夫坚持给 Touka 安排联姻、母亲偷偷支持自由恋爱，为此包下整间餐厅（[15794]-[15799]）；Tsukasa 忙着"开除一名厨师"。Sensei 到场前已内定 Chika 获胜（[15832]、[16042]）。Touka 全场最痛一句："And yet...I came. Just like I do with class."（[16059]-[16060]），继而坦白动机"The reason I continue to show up is that I need your help."（[16073]；bonus 版展开为"是你让我第一次体验同龄女孩之间的事"）。她提及"还有几年 [high_school]"，旁白冷冷接了一句"There are a lot more than just 'several,' Touka..."（[15994]-[15996]——**学制异常的又一处暗示**）。else 版结尾是 20 美元贿赂 Sensei 拥抱 Tsubasa 的荒诞短剧（[16103]-[16141]）。

**dormwar14 [16155]—试胆大会·USER2 强制"恢复出厂设置"（本章核心事件）**。场地是 Amamiya 道场新墙单间（Ayane 自费砌墙；顺带口误"Halloween party here last year"被 Uta 追问学制，[16169]-[16171]）。Tsuneyo 房是 Molly 布的拉面陷阱，30 秒出局；支线重场戏：Yasu 盯着 Rin 说"I can see through the fibers. I can see what you conceal. What others can't."并三连追问"How long have you have been hurting?..."最后低语"I can save you...I can take the pain away..."（[16327]-[16352]）——**Rin 自伤线首次被超感知角色点破**；Noriko 给 Molly 一个"随时可以来捏我"的许可（[16376]-[16378]）。Maya 房内：Ami 自以为只布置了"普通恐怖片道具"，但 Maya 看到的墙上照片来历不明，伴随一对旁观者的凯撒-7 对话，解码为："Do you think she can see us? / Does it matter either way? / The seed has been planted. / She becomes complacent in the cycle....You're right. We are the watchers. / Let us watch her break together."及"A shot from better days. / Praise be."（[16492]-[16509]）、"Ah, how wonderful it is to be young. / She's prettier when she is in pain. / You're such a perv."（[16540]-[16542]）；旁白同步发问"What is it that scares you most? Tell me and I will capture it. I will cage it for you."（[16479]-[16481]）。随后机器接管："USER2 HAS SEIZED CONTROL OF TERMINAL 23 / WOULD YOU LIKE TO PROCEED WITH FACTORY RESET?"——Maya 连答"No."，系统却宣告'YOU HAVE SELECTED "Yes"'并开始倒计时（[16522]-[16528]）；倒数紊乱（1 之后跳回 2、7，插一句"WATERMELON..."，[16600]-[16609]）；允许发送临别讯息，Maya 选择发给 Sensei："I'll never forget you..."→MESSAGE FAILED（[16569]-[16592]）；终局播报 CONGRATULATIONS "Maya Makinami" YOU HAVE COMPLETED THE TEST OF COURAGE + 满屏笑脸渐次变异为"？?????"，Maya 回敬一个":)"（[16620]-[16638]）。出房后 Ami 一无所知（"vampires and ghosts and a few of those jumpscare Halloween decorations"[16655]），Maya 只说"I need to lay down..."（[16664]-[16672]）——**除玩家外无人知道她刚经历了什么；这正是一轮 §3.2 所记"第三重置时自述受创"的现场**。一楼得分（[16678]）。

**dormwar15 [16689]—Sara 酒吧·哥特萝莉时装秀（玩家可选胜负）**。Sana 的"暗/明对比"装（Ami/Ayane 通宵制作）；Yasu 身披 Molly 设计的黑裙，头戴**真荆棘冠**当场流血（[17022]、[17032]-[17034]），并当众布道："For I, Yasu Yasui, have been chosen by Him to spread the seed...and to take all of you with me."（[16972]）、号召众人登高看末日"Come! Let us watch the end of days from atop the highest building we can find!"（[16977]-[16978]，与 dormwar10 "ascend"独白互文）；其神名号"He with many eyes."（[16989]）；败选后仍对 Sana 低语招募"He would be so proud of you, Sana....I can show you things that will change your life. Things that will save you."（[17036]-[17048]）。menu 让玩家直接裁定 Sana/Yasu 胜负（sanafashion/yasufashion 旗标，[17063]-[17061 区间]）。Sensei 旁白再次裸叙元设定："Also, the world resets every few months and I can talk to birds. So there's really no reason to question something like how quickly they could build a stage."（[16752]）。

**dormwar16 [17170]—酒吧余兴·全员酒后群像**。Yasu 止血戏："This blood does not belong to me, Touka. It belongs to God."（[17211]）、被打断的经文"There is no death, there is only-"（[17249]）。Wakana 卸下刻薄露出哥特萝莉前史"It wasn't until I started dressing in clothes like this that I truly began to feel like me."，愿把旧衣传给 Sana（[17342]-[17345]）；其"partner"即空手道师父 Osako（恋人关系坐实，[17362]）。Yuki 应聘的正是 Sara 的店（[17393]-[17405]）。Tsubasa 登场认亲：Tsukioka＝"the bubble wrap family that isn't Ayane's"（[17459]）。wallflower 赛因 Io 始终未现身判 Yumi 默认胜（[17783]-[17785]，一楼得分）；Uta 承认整个周末都在找 Io、"she's been acting kind of weird since this morning"（[17768]-[17776]）。分支：chika_lust≥15 时 Chika/Touka 酗酒烂醉→jump chikalust15（成人向分支，不展开）；否则三人改玩大富翁、chikalust15skip=True。

**dormwar17 [17935]—颁奖晨会·"你重新获得行动权"**。缺席名单：Ayane 病倒、Maya 仍在"从试胆中恢复"（[17954]）。若触发过任一 lust15，则公开存在"bonus contests"但"for the safety of those involved"拒绝公布姓名（[18013]-[18014]）。比分三分支：dormwarfloor1win／dormwarfloor2win／平局 dormwartie（[18051]、[18083]、[18125]）。**平局分支是全书最大规模的第四面墙爆破**：Molly 以"视觉小说女主角"身份痛斥把新事件锁在特定条件后会让 completionist 玩家暴怒（[18163]-[18164]），Nodoka 接"Surely there are dimensions out there where we didn't tie and this conversation never even happened."（[18166]），Uta/Makoto 更直接对镜头开炮——"stop getting so offended when cartoon girls don't do exactly what you want them to do"（[18188]）、"chances are they pirated the game anyway so they can fuck right off."（[18194]）。散场前 Io 警告 Sensei 赶早甩掉 Chika："hurry up and toss her aside or she'll just wind up going crazy and stalking you when you finally {i}do{/i} break up."（[18317]——对 christmastwo/thirdreset 期 Chika 状态的远程预言）。与 Io/Uta 吃完早餐回家，入睡后系统致辞："{i}Congratulations on completing the first ever Super Mega Ultimate Dorm War!{/i}{i}As soon as you wake up, everything will go back to normal and you'll be able to act on your own again.{/i}{i}Thank you for surrendering two days of your seemingly infinite time in Kumon-mi.{/i}"（[18386]-[18388]）——**整个宿舍战争期间 Sensei 的行动权被系统收回代管；赛事本身就是一次小型"重置级"调度实验**。`$ dormwar17 = True`→jump asmenu。

### 战后日常与第三次重置（day333—thirdreset3）：预警、幸福课与三人拥抱

**day333 [18401]——Maya 教室预警："It's coming."**。Ami 新水手服话题牵出学制异常：她"actually didn't even realize we were allowed to change them until you suddenly showed up in that one day"（[18424]——**制度以 Sensei 到场为分界**），Maya 反问为何新来的女生制服"didn't at all match the ones we were given when we enrolled?"（[18425]）；校规松散到"basically anything as long as there's some sort of red or brown on it"（[18433]），唯 Yasu 全黑白——Maya 猜是"so staff members can identify that she has special needs?"（[18436]）。Ami 冷场毒舌"You can probably just die."（[18461]，回敬 Maya 劝退女仆咖啡店打工）。正题：Ami 去漫画部后形成**史上首次 Maya/Sensei 教室独处**——"a thing that...I actually can't remember ever happening before"（[18544]）。Maya 开门见山"It's coming."（[18573]），else 版内心弹幕"{i}Omg he said he can't live with me omg{/i}"（[18583]）。核心情报三连：①"This is the first time a single iteration of you will be watching the world end for a third consecutive time."（[18600]）；②前任只到冬前半——"Made it {i}to{/i}, yes. But none have ever made it to the second half of it."（[18602]）；③记忆可在重置前被提前抹除，且传统意义的死亡发生过多次而 Maya 拒绝细讲（[18603]、[18608]-[18609]）。身份暗示升级："I've known you much {i}longer{/i} than you've known yourself, too, if my latest theory is correct."（[18624]）。求生方案仅一条且是泄愤式的："Killing Noriko."（[18660]）；随后自责上次事故："let my mind get crowded during the process. Which may or may not have led to the destruction of an entire[school]."（[18670]-[18672]——**旧校塌陷＝某次重置的操作事故，接 day297 Touka 家出资重建伏笔**）。循环尺度自述："my life has been reduced to near-quarterly looping through the same[school] year over and over and over."（[18677]——**周期≈每季度一次**）。罕见示弱："But life itself feels new again...And...it's terrifying."（[18700]-[18702]）；天台之约预告"come to the roof on your own. I'll be waiting... Just like always."（[18713]-[18715]）。`$ day333 = True`→jump day333part2。

**day333part2 [18738]——与教职员的双人约会**。开场 Maya 向 Wakana/Osako 求助"This strange man is following me"，Wakana 定性"You're the scum of the earth."（[18756]）。else 版通灵玩笑直指真相："The spirits are telling me that this girl might be able to manipulate time."（[18836]）。席间交代 Noriko 表白后续——Sensei"just had to convince Maya not to murder that girl less than twenty minutes ago"（[18844]）。四人咖啡以"double date"框架展开；else 版 Maya 半吐露"I wanted to be alone with you and thought they'd get in the way."→"You underestimate my love for you."（[18936]、[18943]）。Wakana（Ms. Watabe）的都市传说被点名：女生间流传她有"strange practices"，Nodoka 曾写她的同人（[18966]-[18974]）；Osako 的隐秘爱好被当众抖出（else 版替换为 Fortnite 梗，[19055]）。Wakana 的"重要宣布"冷场收束："I want to fucking die."（[19124]）；点茶"Lavender, please. It will remind me of your eyes."（[19139]）。临别赠言再埋电线意象："Doing so is the only thing that can shine light upon the dark tangle of wires that has become my heart."（[19153]）。Sensei 拿到电话号码，系统盖章"{i}Congratulations! You now have Wakana's phone number!{/i}"（[19172]）。`$ wakananumber = True`、`$ day333part2 = True`；day≥6→jump endofsat，否则 jump endofweekday。

**day340 [19182]——睡衣会申请日**。开场 Team Rocket 式双人戏仿登场（[19215]-[19242]）。Molly 元台词直接对玩家喊话"One step closer to the next event CG!"（[19309]）。Touka 在走廊制止 Yasu 传教，Yasu 吐露社交空白"I've never had friends before."（[19407]）。Tsuneyo 自述恐惧家中手办"盯着她"（[19459]-[19460]）。Sensei 独白盼在重置前赶上睡衣会，并隐隐担忧"这次天台会发生什么"（[19522]-[19523]）。`$ molly_love += 1`、`$ tsuneyo_love += 1`→jump afterschoolevent（label 不在本文件）。

**day344 [19541]——漫画部睡衣会：Tsuneyo 的囚禁寓言**。与会者仅 Ami/Molly/Tsuneyo/Sana 四人；Ayane 又病假，Ami 只回"Again?"（[19615]-[19618]）。厨房谈心段 Tsuneyo 首次完整披露父亲背景：长年卧床却重新展露笑容、是她教会父亲认字读书（[19804]-[19808]）。随后讲出**全书最重的寓言之一**："If protecting the person you love means sacrificing their freedom, all you need to do is teach them to feel free in captivity."（[19843]），又补一句"Sometimes, I'm glad I was able to."（[19850]）——表面说父亲，实际精准映照 Ami 被 Sensei 收养的处境（保护＝囚禁的幸福化）。话音未落电话打断，正是"病假"中的 Ayane 来电"Uhh...Hey."（[19882]）——引出 ayanespecial1 线。`$ day344 = True`→jump ayanespecial1（label 不在本文件）。

**thirdreset1 [19884]——空屋、第三个神与"Perception"训诫课**。Sensei 放学回家发现全员消失，对着空气喊"Is anyone house?"/"Are you house?"（[19913]、[19918]——语法崩坏的孤独），转而对 Ami 书桌的手办求助（[19920]）。入睡后系统整蛊：`$ god_love = 0`＋"{i}Your affection with {s}God{/s} GOD has been reset to 0!{/i}{i}You are going to Hell!{/i}"（[19968]-[19971]）。旁白抛出**三神论**："There is a third god. One without light or wires."（[19980]-[19981]——有光之神、有电线之神之外的第三存在，叙述者拒绝说明；电线意象接 trinity2）。随后整段**直接对玩家授课**：今日单词 Perception 的两种定义；质问连发"Why are you still reading? Stop. Things will only get worse. You will only hurt more people."（[20034]-[20037]）、"So, you find joy in watching others hurt?...Is it because they're not real? Because to me, they are."（[20042]-[20045]）、"The red string is wrapped tightly around {i}you{/i}!"（[20057]）、"You are nothing. Everything is nothing. This world will go on forever whether you bother to witness it or not."（[20060]-[20062]）、醒来后将困于"your {i}real{/i} bedroom in your {i}real{/i} life...There is no way out. You and I are in this together."（[20071]-[20076]）。紧接着 USER2 再次夺取终端 23 并**首次主动发起聊天**（唯一选项 Yes）："GREETINGS. I HAVE BEEN TRYING TO REACH YOU FOR QUITE SOME TIME."/"YOU DO NOT KNOW ME, BUT I HAVE WATCHED YOU GROW."/"I AM HERE TO PROTECT YOU. ALL YOU MUST DO IS ACCEPT ME."/"I CAN NOT OFFER YOU THE SAME THINGS THE OTHERS CAN. BUT I CAN GUIDE YOUR VISION."/"GOOD LUCK. PRAISE BE."（[20087]-[20115]）——USER2 自我定位为"引导视觉的守护者"，与 Maya（重置执行者）、ADMINISTRATOR（权限顶层）并立为第三方势力，祝祷语与 Yasu 教团一致。`$ totaldays += 1`、`$ day = 7` 后进入 Survive!/Grow! 终端菜单（menu thirdresetmenu，[20140]-[20144]——与 dormwar8 太阳系独白"I will take. And I will grow."同源）。survivemenu 全部选项为十六进制彩蛋：61 6d 69＝ami、6d 61 79 61＝maya、61 79 61 6e 65＝ayane、68 6f 70 65＝hope（→"I attempt to pluck my eyes out of their sockets."[20177]）、73 61 6b 69＝saki（→"I go back in time."[20184]）、73 65 6c 65 62 75 73＝selebus（→两行 [[redacted][20191]-[20195]）、73 63 68 6f 6f 6c＝school、68 69 6d 61 77 61 72 69＝himawari（→"I see things that no one else can."[20205]）、6b 61 6f 72 69＝kaori（→"I swallow a spider."→**jump kindergartenclass**[20211]-[20216]—— 幼儿园关的暗门是机器人少女之名）、66 6c 6f 6f 72 20 32＝floor 2（→"I connect to the tenth floor instead. No one lives there."[20222]-[20223]——宿舍只有两层的又一证词）、73 65 6e 73 65 69＝sensei（→电话尖叫回敬）。菜单底部藏六连输入验证：word of the day＝perception、bird number＝58320582157、clock number＝2374820、animal heads＝4、"What is love?"＝blind、"Are you happy here?"＝yes，全对方可 jump endofreset3loop（[20249]-[20283]）——答案皆为散落前文的画面细节，构成对观察力的期末考。

I do not have what you humans would call a 'family.'"（[33369]/[33371]——非人措辞"you humans"再添一例）；拟声词怪话"whapam, kaboom, shhhhhhhh"（[33378]）。**Yumi 生日确认**（[33447]），Yuki 托 Sensei 转交礼物（[33461]-[33464]，直通 ht5 爆发），Tojo 家停电插曲（[33465]-[33467]）→ht3。

**halloweentwo3 [33555]**。派对群像：Rin×Otoha 恋后磨合，Rin 抱怨她"forcing yourself on me and kissing me before I have a chance to breathe"（[33614]——攻守倒转的喜剧）；Nodoka 变装成 Rin 引发错位对话；Molly 拉酒伴请求"Drink with me so I can feel less lonely"（[33803]——单恋者的孤独宣言）；Kirin 入场（[33831]、[33849]-[33854]）被 Chika 护主拦截（[33925]）；**Noriko 身穿 Niki 绝版舞台装"Be My Fire"登场**（[33980]-[33989]——用偶像的旧衣宣示对 Sensei 过去的继承权）→ht4。

**halloweentwo4 [34038]**。**Noriko 泳池边崩溃**（本段情感核心）：她撞见 Sensei 与 Niki 交谈后失控，哭诉恐惧他重投姐姐怀抱（[34085]-[34153]）；核心台词三连："I can't keep losing you to the people I'm closest to!"（[34118]）、"NOBODY knows about me! Especially you!"（[34126]）、"Every second I have to sit through knowing you're closer to Nee-chan when {i}I'm{/i} the one who never gave up looking for you is literally Hell!"（[34252]——**"从未放弃寻找你"的人被留在地狱，直指 sb17 对峙中"寻找过去"母题的情感代价**）。崩溃被 Makoto/Miku 硬性打断（[34256]-[34263]）；Sensei 事后独白庆幸自己失忆："It's both fortunate and unfortunate that I was not there to remember it."（[34320]）。缓冲喜剧：Yasu 幽灵问答"There are no {i}ghosts{/i}, Sensei. Just the remnants of those who left this plane without accomplishing their goal."（[34398]-[34400]——**执念未竟者滞留人间的定义，与她自己的天台预言同构**）；Uta 猫耳、Io 毒舌、Tsuneyo 被迫穿猫装→ht5。

**halloweentwo5 [34525]**。**Io 的鮟鱇鱼独白**（Io 线最重要段落）：她以雄性鮟鱇寄生雌鱼为喻自剖——依附他人而活、终将成为"little, fishy sperm factories"式的累赘，随即反问 Sensei"What do you gain from me?"；答案落在两次牵手："First, with a random blonde. Then, with my best friend. But now...you come there for me."（[34638]-[34642]——**"random blonde"=Kaori、"best friend"=Yumi：Sensei 的过去按时间顺序在 Io 口中被复述**）；誓言"Even when you get tired of me feeding off of your energy...I won't leave you."（[34644]）；并披露海滩之夜她曾在 Sensei 房间空等一整晚"I waited all night in your room at the beach for the chance to see you only to go home empty handed?"（[34652]——**sb17 misery tour 里"红光房间中等他的女孩/No one ever finds her"极可能就是 Io**）。焦虑药空腹服用细节（[34717]）。后半 Yumi 手机煮饭喜剧（把进水的手机埋进米锅结果把米煮熟，[34750]-[34769]）；**Yuki 上门转交生日礼物引爆 Yumi**——从"Just go...Now!"（[34819]-[34829]）到独处后落泪、礼物落水声"{*Plop*}"（[34852]-[34865]）：母亲的礼物被亲手沉水。yumi_love+1；Haruka 图片短信→jump amilust20intro。

**amilust20intro [34885]／amilust20 [35160]**（lust 线，截断体）：Ami 担忧 Noriko 状态的房内事件；skip 分支置 `$ amilust20skip = True` 后直接 jump halloweentwo6（[35156]-[35158]）；else 版以拥抱安慰收尾并置 `$ amilust20 = True`＋ami_lust+3→jump halloweentwo6（[35170]-[35176]、[35190]前）。

**halloweentwo6 [35190]**。极短过渡：Noriko 把自己锁进洗手间痛哭，Kirin 隔门相劝"this is a Halloween party and it's prooooobably not the best time to lock yourself inside of a bathroom and cry."（[35197]）；bonus==True 分支直接 jump norikobrx（成人分支，注明存在不展开）；else 版 Noriko 抽泣应声"You're right..."（[35203]），两人返回派对。`$ halloweentwo6 = True`→ht7。

**halloweentwo7 [35219]**。桌边戏，**Ami×Maya 友情裂痕正式上演**。Maya 夸 Ami 是全桌最可爱的女孩，反而令 Ami 破防：质问"Why {i}have{/i} you always been so supportive of me, Maya?"（[35430]）、自认嫉妒泄漏"I just get annoyed by how pretty you are sometimes and then my jealousy leaks out without me realizing it."（[35288]）、根源是仰望"I've always wanted to be more like you!"（[35440]）与真实性质疑"it's just hard figuring out if it's {i}real{/i} or not."（[35454]）；矛头最终转向 Sensei 偏心"If I'm the perfect [niece] and everything Sensei needs wrapped into one little package, what reason would he have to do something like that?"（[35474]）。**else 版 Maya 当众滑嘴**（此段仅在 bonus==False 时存在）："Besides who cares if I'm secretly in love with him? It's not like it's a big deal or anything. Like, I'm open about it at this point and no one ever even acknolwedges it."（[35481]）——**Maya 以抱怨形式公开承认暗恋，全场装聋**。Ami 以失眠收场"I've just been losing sleep lately. I'm probably cranky."（[35500]）。Sensei 内心反省是否因"觉得 Maya 知道些什么"而习惯性偏袒："Am I disregarding Ami's feelings and just rolling with what Maya wants because I...think she knows stuff?"（[35534]），随即自我修正"At the end of the day, Maya doesn't know anything anymore."＋"She still knows how the resets work for the most part. Probably."（[35536]-[35538]——**对重置机制的知识存量是两人同盟的唯一基石，也是 Ami 直觉敌意的靶心**）。Geoffrey 与爱尔兰黑手党插科打诨（[35504]-[35511]）。ami_love+1→ht8。

**halloweentwo8 [35612]**。Otoha 突袭撮合"You should date Futaba."（[35617]），真身是替 Nodoka 的 truth or dare 圈桌补位（[35631]-[35632]）；Frank／Clarissa／Jazmin 假名约会喜剧（[35663]-[35681]）。Otoha 为"撮合"划界："It's fine to make jokes and try to push them a little closer together, but when you're literally listing the reasons they should date, it's...different. It stops becoming a joke and...well, makes shit weird."（[35828]-[35830]）。**Rin 异常再升级**：她越过玩笑强行推销 Sensei×Futaba 正式交往"Why not just make it official and actually get together with her?"（[35785]），连发起者都觉得过头"Don't you think you might be taking things a little too far?"（[35792]）；Futaba 私下问"Is...Is everything {i}okay{/i} with you right now, Rin?"，Otoha 承认尚读不懂她的抑郁信号"I don't really know the...signs of your depression yet"（[35844]），Rin 全盘否认"I'm totally fine. I'm just trying to help everyone out."（[35845]）。futabahalloween2win 分支 Futaba 自白"He's not ever going to {i}date{/i} me, Rin."＋"I'm happy just knowing that the person I admire looks at me and doesn't immediately compare me to either of you."（[35871]、[35875]）。散场时 Sensei 怀疑自己在场本身就是压力源（[35927]-[35929]）。Squid Game 讨论救场（[35913]）。futaba_love+1→ht9。

**halloweentwo9 [35951]**。真相或大冒险圆桌（信息密度极高的群像）：Miku 被"会杀谁"逼到点名 Yumi（"she's always bein' mean to Futaba and stuff"[36004]），Chika 护 Yumi："Yumi's just misunderstood."＋"today's her birthday and she hasn't even shown up yet. She just feels like she's...not accepted here."（[36005]-[36006]——Yumi 生日孤独二次着色）。Nodoka（变装 Rin）以引导性问题逼出 **Chika 对 Rin 的隐藏取向自白**："Rin is someone I...probably wouldn't mind having that sort of relationship with."（[36054]，附封口令"this {i}never{/i} leaves the circle"）；Makoto 旁证日常化"Lesbian relationships in an all-girls[school] are just a thing that happen."（[36040]）。Touka 的"世界第一有魅力的人"＝美国影星 Seth Rogen（[36107]-[36126]，父亲葡萄园幻想喜剧）。Makoto 被问是否看过成人影片：else 版承认"because I have to in order to keep my family's business afloat!"（[36181]——**家庭产业即成人影片店的实锤由 else 版给出**；bonus 版另有母亲强迫观看的家史爆发，注明存在不展开），Chika 追问"is everything okay at home?"只换回"It's fine."（[36190]-[36195]）。Nodoka 谜团加深：自认人设并非伪装"Ding ding ding. The class rep has figured it out."（[36238]）、拒绝解释"I think I'd like to remain an enigma for a little while longer."（[36251]）、突袭式试探"Will {i}you{/i} be my girlfriend, Makoto?"得"No. But I feel like even if I said yes, you'd back off."（[36247]-[36248]）。**Uta 危机＝本作的"平台规则"时刻**：Nodoka 敢 Uta 当众吻 Sensei，全员反对时 Makoto 抛出元层论据"Patreon draws the line at kissing! Probably!"（[36291]——**众筹平台的内容条款作为游戏内法律被角色引用**），并援引事前约定"We clearly stated that Sensei would not be brought into any of the questions or dares in this game {i}specifically{/i} to avoid a situation like this."（[36293]）；Nodoka 以"民主表决"操弄程序（[36339]-[36341]），Touka 以"家人脸颊吻不算浪漫"投下关键票（[36358]-[36361]）；Uta 进退维谷之际事件在半句台词处硬切"You're... Doin'...anything else..."（[36386]-[36387]——**强烈暗示她实际做的远超敢罚内容，真相留白**）。`$ halloweentwo9 = True`→ht10。

**halloweentwo10 [36396]**。Molly 单独买醉段：喜剧外壳（half empty/full 之争、"eat hot chip, and lie"梗、MMO 黑话连发[36584]-[36593]）包裹崩坏内核。**Sensei 的台词遭实时删除线改写**——"{s}Can you hear me?{/s}"（[36488]）、"{s}Everything is better where it's dark.{/s}"（[36490]）、"{s}GO! RUN!{/s}"（[36491]）：三句被划掉的"另一个声音"从他口中漏出，与 sb7/sb15 的系统改写同源。**机器播报接入"HALLOWEEN SPECIAL"频道**："SUCCESSFULLY CONNECTED TO 'HALLOWEEN SPECIAL'"→"CHECKING FOR FLAG 'mollyhalloween2win'"→命中则读取档案并播发指令"MOLLY MACCORMACK IS A [[MANDATORY] ROMANCEABLE CHARACTER / PLEASE CONTINUE TO RAISE YOUR AFFECTION WITH MOLLY MACCORMACK / PLEASE CONTINUE TO SPEND TIME WITH MOLLY MACCORMACK"（[36495]-[36517]——**系统直接命令玩家维持攻略进度；[[MANDATORY] 方括号语法与 Sara 消音标记同款**）；未命中仅 FLAG NOT LOCATED（[36520]）→CONNECTION TERMINATED（[36525]）。情报点：Rin 自残事件再次被证实——上次劝和的结果是"made {i}me{/i} sit there holding her arm closed so she didn't bleed out"（[36652]）；Molly 预言 Rin 会"hide it and hide it and hide it and pretend everyting's okay 'til she gets caught"（[36658]），Sensei 反手戳穿"pretending everything is okay until you get caught is exactly what you did concerning your feelings for her, isn't it?"（[36660]）致其泪崩离场"Goodbye forever!"（[36710]），消失在某扇门后（[36714]）。Uta 事后驱使他去照看 Molly，以六亿円出场费玩笑封口（[36795]）。bonus 版结尾将走向房间的过程写成欲望侵入思维的重复句循环并以"I am a good person."收束（[36826]-[36860]，注明存在不展开）；else 版仅一句"That's it."（[36862]）。连好感播报都被污染："{s}{i}Molly's affection has increased to [molly_love]!{/s} This event has been ruined!{/i}"（[36873]——**系统消息本体被覆写**）。molly_love+1→ht11。

**halloweentwo11 [36881]**。bonus==True 分支整体 jump lavendersgreenx（被删事件的成人原版，注明存在不展开）。**else 版＝假标题屏布道**："{i}Welcome to Lessons in Love!{/i}...There used to be an event here, but now there's not."＋"In fact, that event is probably why this version of the game exists in the first place!"（[36889]-[36891]——**旁白向全年龄版玩家直言：这个"干净版本"本身就是因为那场被删事件才存在的**）；"People are so weak, don't you think?"+提线木偶论"Being incapable of detaching themselves from the strings their creators slipped carefully into the gaps in their joints...They're a little like puppets."（[36892]-[36894]——**角色＝造物主穿线之偶**）；拉拢玩家"We're different. We're the special ones. We see the world not as it's shown to us, but as it is truly is"（[36897]-[36899]）；三连质问收尾"Do you think that everything that's been removed has been meaningless and unimportant? / Do you think you'll be able to finish the game at the rate this is going? / You are foolish. You are in the wrong place. I hope you enjoy the dark."（[36901]-[36905]）。`$ halloweentwo11 = True`→ht12。

**halloweentwo12 [36913]**。极短余波：Sensei 从无意识中醒来，双腿发软，Molly 完全昏厥，而他"until a second ago...I think I may have been too?"（[36921]-[36923]——**两人同时失去意识的原因永不解释**）；else 版只留一个晚安拥抱与愧疚"I feel really bad about it afterwards."（[36928]-[36929]），置 `$ mollysad = True`；bonus 分支 jump whatwedox（成人分支，注明存在）。→ht13。

**halloweentwo13 [36939]**。**旁白对玩家的驱逐演说**（本章最直接的第四墙段落）：开场连环质问"What did you want to be when you were younger? / I bet it was something silly, like an astronaut. / That didn't turn out all too well, though...did it?"（[36942]-[36944]）；齿轮论把世界重心钉在玩家身上"The world stops existing the second you believe it to not exist! ...We're all built around you! {i}It's{/i} all built around you..."（[36953]-[36956]）；虚无主义布道"There is no god. There is no heaven. There is no hell. There's just this. Love it. Cherish it. Embrace it. For it is all you have now."（[36960]-[36965]）；诊断玩家心态"Hiding away in the darkest corners of your mind, distracting yourself with thoughts of better days, because you're afraid of opening your eyes and seeing how terrible it's all become."（[36978]）；判词与逐客令"You are no astronaut. You're just another piece in a much bigger puzzle."＋"I don't want you here anymore. Open your eyes."（[36979]-[36983]）。随后 Io 安慰段：她的强迫性道歉逻辑（[37003]-[37004]，在意名单只有两人）；Sensei 迁怒"Not everything is about you, Io....I shouldn't be here anyway."（[37037]-[37041]）；Io 重申心意"no matter what that...thing that I'm not involved in is, I'll still like you."（[37051]）；**蛋壳比喻**："We can keep being...eggshells around each other."—"No. I mean {i}being{/i} eggshells. The good parts of us have already been spilled out and used up by somebody else, and all that's left is the useless white part."（[37073]-[37076]），并预言"But you'll still always be empty inside. I can't change that part."（[37083]-[37084]）；Sensei 的回应指向体内异物："'empty' isn't the right word. There's still something inside of me. I can feel it moving around."（[37090]-[37092]）；Io 顺势求私奔"Make your home inside of me, Sensei. Let's try our best to be happy together, because god knows we can't do it on our own."（[37104]-[37105]），Sensei 答"There is no god."，Io 接"You're right...No god would make a world as cruel as this."（[37110]-[37112]）。Kirin 带来 Noriko 已送车站的消息（[37128]-[37129]）。结尾**叙述退化序列**：平直短句行走蒙太奇"It is a fun and successful ending to the Halloween party. I am glad that nothing bad happened tonight. Nothing bad happened tonight. I am a good person."（[37229]-[37232]，自我说服的三重否定）；Molly 醉酒短信"Hhiy...is molly / Come back Sir / need to know whst happened"（[37238]-[37249]）；思维遭删除线审查"I bring {s}Molly{/s} Kirin and Io back to the dorm."／"I don't want to think about {s}MOLLY{/s} anyone."／"{s}I DON'T WANT TO THINK{/s}"（[37254]-[37257]）；首尾呼应回环"What did I want to be when I was younger?"（[37258]）。Molly 最后一条短信"{i}Was I a good protagonist?{/i}"（[37273]——**醉倒的角色向玩家追问主角资格**）；解锁 `$ mollynumber = True`（[37275]-[37277]）。`$ halloweentwo13 = True`、`$ totaldays += 1; $ day = 6`→jump saturdaymorning。

**goodboy [37315]——“好男孩”审判（隐藏性质的心理恐怖事件）**。开场诗只在 bonus 版出现（驯化童谣韵文，以"I'm such a good boy. Obedient too. So easy to touch, so easy to use."为核心意象，注明存在不展开）。else 版正文：Sensei 决定今天"going on an {i}adventure{/i}"（[37346]），途中遇两个滑行而不步行的人形（a1/a2，庆祝舞步习自"the corridors of [kumon_mi_high] before I encountered a talking net"[37367]——早期梦境事件回调）；它们抛出雪的神学问答"It melts! Returns to the sky! Only to return when the world reverts! / But reverts to what?! And what is its rightful state?!"（[37393]-[37394]——**重置循环的拟人化身再次现身**）；允许他点名交谈对象但"Denied! The one you speak of appears to be busy at the moment!"拒了 Maya（[37414]-[37415]）。人形将手探入他体内如操纵杆般拖拽器官、"carefully selecting the coordinates of a certain place in a certain time"（[37435]）把他掷入陌生走廊：童年记忆闪回——爬窗割伤手、坠楼两层伤踝、"During that time, I grew closer to someone who would one day leave me."（[37453]），走廊幽灵永恒绕圈，他决定"pretend"并向每个灵魂挥手致意（[37463]-[37466]）。遇见神秘少女 q（"Are you a new teacher or something?"[37476]），调情未遂（对方有男友），校名输出为"[[REDACTED]."（[37510]）；临别她问出标题之问"Are you a good boy?"（[37522]）随即"pop"地消失（[37529]），原地多出一把椅子。樱花香令他作呕（[37545]-[37547]）；"You're such a good boy."的无名嗓音（av，[37565]）；被注视感渐强（[37569]-[37576]，"I am infected."）。**对玩家的直接独白**："you're all I have to talk to in here. And since you never say anything, I'm the one who needs to fill the void. What is it that {i}you{/i} would like to talk about? You can choose any topic you like. So long as it's the topic I want you to choose."（[37584]-[37588]——**"自由选择"被当场揭伪，与 day220 六连 Jump 同一手法**）。记忆闪回（te）：包扎手掌、背起回家的提议来自"your [[REDACTED]"（[37605]——亲属称谓被打码），"You're so much nicer than your big brother."（[37612]——**Sensei 有兄长/本人即兄长的又一证词**），以及关于"哥哥对我做了什么"的话头被急止（[37613]-[37614]）。给 Ami 发短信求救：省略号呼吸式胀缩（[37646]-[37661]）后"{i}You did it!{/i}"（[37667]）——事件以未知方式"通过"。bonus 版此处另有红acted处理过的侵犯文本与庆功播报"You made it through the event without molesting a girl!"及"TV Show"访谈审讯（主持人 sev 可没收视力、威胁"your entire existence is wiped clean"、嘉宾包括声音能煮沸内脏的"Moyo Makinamo"与从不发言的 Chair，[37670]-[37890]，注明存在不展开）。两版共同出口：`$ anewkey = False`、`$ goodboy = True`→jump saturdaynight（[37883]-[37898]）；else 版谢幕旁白"I'll go back to living in the shadows...I'll go back to being a good boy."（[37876]-[37880]）。

### 圣诞·二（ch2script.rpy christmastwo1—20）

**christmastwo1 [37900]**。办公室日常开场：Sensei 自嘲存在感"Some days are good. And others? Well- Saying they don't matter is the nice way of putting it."（[37910]-[37913]）；预感式独白"I wonder how much longer I can keep this up for."（[37924]）。**剧情内更新标题卡**："{i}Welcome to the second Lessons in Love special Christmas update!{/i}{i}The next twenty-something events will play in succession.{/i}"（[37925]-[37926]），旁白自辩起点平淡"Sometimes, it's the average days that don't carry any sort of weight that wind up completely changing everything we know when we look back on them."（[37930]），祝福语暗藏凶兆"Please cherish this Christmas- for you never know how many of them you have left."（[37935]）。爱伦·坡《乌鸦》戏仿过场"While I nodded, nearly napping, suddenly there came a tapping."（[37939]）。**Wakana 病愈归来道歉**：为海滩送医事件正式致歉"It was a mistake. ### 万圣节·中（script.rpy halloween6 后半—halloween8）：Yumi＝终端23、USER2 越权失败

**halloween6 后半 [30725]-[30801]**。Yumi 夜路对白把"强制相遇"推上台面："I feel like I can't go anywhere anymore without you bumping into me like it's part of some fucking script or something."（[30731]，**"script"直指剧本**）；"It's like the fucking city itself keeps trying to force us together and not realizing that I hate your fucking guts."（[30732]）。Sensei 问"You think the city is somehow responsible for all of these chance encounters?"（[30733]），Yumi："God? Fate? Neither of those things are real. So saying that Kumon-fucking-mi is responsible is as good a guess as any."（[30739]-[30742]）——角色层否认超自然，元层暗示：安排相遇的正是事件调度系统。她欲言又止后问出致命问题："Have you, like... Lost...some of your memories or something?"（[30763]-[30764]）——**Yumi 凭直觉侦测到 Sensei 的记忆异常**。话音未落，全屏斜杠（[30771]）后机器播报涌入：`"//////MULTIPLE ERRORS DETECTED IN TERMINAL 23 / RESET NECESSARY TO RESUME STANDARD OPERATIONS / USER2 IS NOW CONTROLLING TERMINAL 23"`（[30782]-[30784]）；两次 `"//////USER2 LACKS THE PERMISSIONS NECESSARY TO RESET SYSTEM / PLEASE ENSURE ALL WIRES ARE PROPERLY CONNECTED BEFORE TRYING AGAIN"`（[30788]-[30789]、[30793]）；终局 `"//////TERMINAL 23 IS NOW LOCKED / CONTACT ADMINISTRATOR FOR ADDITIONAL ASSISTANCE / TERMINATING CURRENT PROCESS AND REROUTING TO THE NEXT AVAILABLE PATH"`（[30794]-[30796]）。**解读**：Yumi＝终端23，触及"记忆重置"禁区即报错；USER2 试图接管改写该终端（同 halloween4 巷道事件模式），但权限不足，只能锁死终端并"终止当前进程、改道下一条可用路径"——对话被硬切，直接跳 halloween7。"ENSURE ALL WIRES ARE PROPERLY CONNECTED"呼应 trinity2 的电线预言（[28312]-[28315]）；与 bv14 [27060]-[27061] 互证：重置权在 ADMINISTRATOR 层，USER2/Maya 均无权限。`$ halloween6 = True`（[30799]）。

**halloween7 [30802]**。酒吧派对（Sara 是店主），Kaori 借调调酒，蓝灯观察报告 "The lights are blue and everyone appears to be aroused."（[30827]）；Sara 醉问 Kaori 恋情与生育常识（[30847]-[30848]）。else 版服装梗：Sara 说 Sensei 扮的是 "the huggy boy"（[30903]；bonus 版为粗俗梗）——**bv11 Maya 口误"huggy boy"[25719] 以玩笑形式复现**。bonus 版披露 Maki/Haruka 醉吻史与开放式婚姻（[30991]-[30999]、[31000]）；else 版同位处却是 Haruka 突问 "Am I going to die, Maki?"，Maki 答 "We're all going to die, Haruka. It's just a matter of when."（[31004]-[31005]）——**全年龄版反而更虚无**。结尾 Sara 拽 Sensei 上楼（[31156]-[31165]），`$ halloween7 = True`（[31171]），按 sara_lust 分流。

### 万圣节·庭园夜话（script.rpy halloween10）：Samhain、"把这一年重来一遍"

**halloween10 [31997]**。`_in_replay` 保护（[31998]）。Molly（爱尔兰人设）＋Tsuneyo 组合。Molly 科普 Samhain：约两千年前凯尔特节日，驱鬼兼"庆祝夏之终结、迎来下一季"（[32022]）——**本作把万圣节重定义为"送夏仪式"，正贴 reset 循环的季节机制**（Kumon-mi 无秋冬，见 bv9 [25001]-[25003]）。else 版替换笑料：Molly "(Incomprehensible Gaelic)"（[32085]）、Tsuneyo "I understand Gaelic now. I learned it after the beach."（[32101]-[32102]）；else 版 Sensei 劝酒台词提及 "you will end up like Sana's mom."（[32158]）——else 独有的 Sana 母亲酗酒指涉。三人偷酒至庭院，旁白点题：凉风提醒夏将尽，"It really is Samhain. We're having a party to usher in the end of the Summer. And what comes next is something even I'm not sure of."（[32181]-[32183]）。**Sensei 的重置自觉**："It hadn't occurred to me until now that I...didn't even {i}know{/i} these two prior to the world resetting. That doesn't mean they're going to be gone again when I wake up the second time, does it?"（[32206]-[32207]）＋"What sort of reason would there be for these two to just show up halfway through a[school] year on the second loop but not the first one?"（[32209]）——**首次以"第二周目"框架质疑中途登场者的存在论地位**。他说破学年将尽、一切重来（[32242]-[32244]），Tsuneyo 泪崩 "No."（[32267]）；安慰语惊人："Hey, you could always get lucky and wind up in my class again next year. Or, even better yet, we can just do this entire year over. That sounds much more fun, doesn't it?"（[32270]-[32273]）——**"把整年重来一遍"作为安慰脱口而出：Sensei 开始渴望重置**。身份独白：Tsuneyo 说前任老师 Wakana Watabe "felt less human than Sensei does"（[32292]），Sensei 心想 "If anything, I'm probably {i}less{/i} human than Miss Watabe. I'm just a disembodied soul who wandered into the body of a man who didn't know how great he had it. Or maybe he did. Who knows?"（[32295]-[32297]）——与 bv14 [27224]-[27231] 夺身自白互证。Molly 感慨 "Everything is ending before it even had a chance to start."（[32309]）；旁白收束 "Ah, the sweetest lesson of all. Time. She's an evil bitch, isn't she?"（[32312]-[32314]）。

halloween10 后半：旁白补完——"But, sooner or later, we all make it out of the labyrinth of [teenage]life and find ourselves trying to feel [young]again in any way we possibly can."（[32316]）；bonus 版自白 "It's probably one of the reasons I let girls this age continuously cry around me."（[32319]）。核心需求宣言："Being needed feels nice. Being wanted feels nice. The only issue is that almost everything else in the world feels horrible. Oh, how wonderful it would be to always feel loved."（[32323]-[32326]）。**"生命是游戏"布道**：Molly 索要传世忠告，Sensei："Life is a game."，"And all there is to it is to just...try and get through without running out of lives."；Molly "Don't we only get one life, though?"；s "Not at all. You get tons of lives. And each time something bad happens, one gets taken away. It could be something like losing a mother. Or a father."（[32340]-[32361]）——**以"多条命"否认单命论＝重置世界观的角色层投影**。Tsuneyo 问 "What happens at the end of the game? How do we win?"，s "Being happy, I guess?"（[32375]-[32385]）。两人异口同声 "No..."（[32393]-[32395]）后抱住 Sensei；旁白 "I really hope they're still around when winter comes."（[32417]）、Tsuneyo "Don't...go..."／s "I'm not going anywhere..."（[32417]-[32418]）。tsuneyo_love+3、molly_love+3（[32453]-[32454]）。

### trinity3·上半（script.rpy）：旁白布道、创世神话、真实 IP 与"跳下去就能重置"

**trinity3 [33739]**。开场反故事："Let me tell you a story. Before there was something, there was nothing. The end."（[33746]-[33748]），随后约三十行纯省略号（[33749]-[33783]）考验玩家耐心；"Still here? **跳过惩罚与真实 IP**："even though it's so happy there are still people who think it's okay to skip past it! THEY'RE JUST LINES! What's REALLY scary is that within the next year, someone you love is going to die. Fear that! Not this game! Oh, wait. Right. This isn't a game at all. TELL 'EM AGAIN, MAYA!!!!"（[33883]-[33890]）。随即场景落回对话：m "This isn't a game."，s "If it's not a game then what's with all of these references to simulations and all of that 'USER2' stuff?"，m "How would I know? trinity3 下半：Sensei 吐槽自带删除线 "{s}JESUS CHRIST{/s} Wow, you're no help at all."（[33908]）。**Maya 自刺太阳穴**："Maya decides to stab herself in the temple and poorly drawn computer-generated blood spills out of her because this is all just a computer game or something and definitely not anything else."（[33917]）——旁白以"拙劣 CG 血"反讽式否认游戏性。旁白复述设定：Ami 父母双亡，bonus 版明言其父是 Sensei 之兄、与"outrageously hot wife"一同 "turned into roadkill"（[33923]-[33925]）；else 版自称 "You are Sensei!
