OR EVEN BETTER YET, what if it was GOD who made that tree...'（[33934]-[33941]）；**"GOD is THREE gods and they all joined forces to plant the seed that made your cool new sex-world／HUG-world"（[33952]/[33955]，双版本同文）——三神合植世界观正式定名**。对玩家的质问："But when was the last time you stopped and thought about what happens when their stories come to an end? If everyone's story comes to an end at once, that means your story comes to an end, too. Right?"（[33962]-[33963]）。**无名死胎（ub）登场**："Wrong. Your story will go on because you're meant to fix this world! You're the only one who can truly kill God!"（[33976]-[33977]）；自嘲 "I'm unimportant and was stillborn anyway, so you can just completely disregard my existence"＋"there just aren't a lot of openings for stillborn children"（[33981]、[33988]）——**死胎角色=婴儿/雏鸟死亡母题（babyfinches 前兆）**；情报来源 "One of the three gods filled me in before this scene"（[33985]）。ub 请示上级后 **HO 第三次现身**：ho "WE MEET AGAIN"（[34005]），旁白交还视角并补刀 "I wonder who was speaking before?"（[34008]——暗示前一段叙述者另有其人）。问答：s "How do I get home?" ho "YOU DONT / YOU STAY HERE FOREVER"（[34011]-[34012]）；"Is Maya really dead?" ho "WAS SHE EVER ALIVE?????"、"IS SIMPLY SEEING ENOUGH TO CONFIRM THE EXISTENCE OF ANYTHING????"（[34014]、[34016]），随即松口 "THE GIRL DOES LIVE / SHE HAS LIVED MORE LIVES THAN YOU COULD POSSIBLY IMAGINE"（[34021]）。**HO 的自我宣告（Dickinson《Hope》化用）**："I HAVE ONLY LIVED ONE LIFE BUT IT HAS BEEN AND WILL BE AN ETERNAL ONE / I AM THE THING WITH FEATHERS / I AM THE SOUND OF THE CICADAS"（[34023]-[34025]）——**蝉声=bv9 海滩之问的应答者[25162]-[25164]**；"I AM THE HOPE INSIDE OF YOUR HEART"（[34032]）后急转 "And you are nothing. You will always be nothing. So why not live for something greater? Why not live for me?"（[34033]-[34036]）。空间定性："Down here, only those I believe in can open their eyes. This is the bottom of everything. Where time is infinite. You may sleep here."（[34042]-[34045]）；bonus 版附加献祭条款 "All I ask is that you surrender both yourself and them to me."（[34049]）。吞舌与机械肺："I breathe through the hand of a false god. Or a true god. God is dead. Or at least he's supposed to be. Or used to be."（[34057]-[34061]）。语言失效段：Sensei 只能发出键盘乱码 "edfihgeirhgjwerfihgbdfbhefhurghwghidfw!!!"（[34066]），"My words go unrecognized...They mean nothing."（[34071]）、"Everything means nothing and nothing means everything."（[34076]）；"The world fades to black as I begin to lose hope."（[34077]——hope 小写与 HOPE 实体形成文字游戏）。HO 预告离别 "THIS WILL BE OUR LAST MEETING FOR QUITE SOME TIME / I HAVE MORE IMPORTANT MATTERS TO ATTEND TO"（[34080]-[34081]），旁白猜其事务与电线有关（[34083]），Sensei 产生电线缠颈窒息妄想（[34084]-[34085]）；收束句 "It is times like these that I wish I could love. Maybe one day, if I try hard enough, I will be able to."（[34087]-[34088]）。**USER3 接管终端23**："///////////////////////USER3 HAS ASSUMED CONTROL OF TERMINAL 23"…"can you see this?"，选单 "Yes"→"i'm so glad"+"i was very concerned"（[34092]-[34100]）——USER3 全小写、语气亲昵忧虑，与 USER2 的系统腔截然不同；且**终端编号同为23**（halloween6 中 Yumi 被锁的正是 TERMINAL 23[30794]），梦境中的接管对象疑似 Sensei 本体。`$ trinity3track = True`、`$ trinity3 = False`（[34116]-[34117]，自清以备重触发），按 day 分支推进周目（[34119]-[34131]）。

### 崩坏五连·上（script.rpy day214—day217 前半）：西瓜 Maya、末日预告与 Makoto 崩坏

**day214 [34134]**。教室崩坏群像开场：Makoto 昏睡、Maya 与 Chika 缺席、Molly/Tsuneyo "disappeared out of nowhere"、Yumi 翘课（[34140]）。Ayane 抱西瓜上课坚称是转世的同学："This is our good friend, Maya Makinami."（[34150]）——**bv11 西瓜殉道[26052]-[26058] 的回响：Maya 缺席即由西瓜替身补位**；Ami 崩溃 "Am I the only normal person still left in this world?!"（[34163]）。else 版插入袜子稻草人与 s "This game is getting too weird."（[34179]）。Molly/Tsuneyo 回归并披露已找校长申请下学年分班，结果"未批准也未否决"（[34330]-[34336]），Molly 宣言 "This is still her introductory arc!"（[34337]）；Tsuneyo "The two of us have not even lost Virginia yet."（[34342]）＋万智牌 Damnation 玩笑 "It destroys all creatures. They can't be regenerated either."（[34351]-[34352]）。**元叙事**：Molly "A typical visual novel protagonist wouldn't connect all of those dots for at least another twenty hours."（[34359]）、Tsuneyo 的原型自认 "my archetype is 'The one without a real shot.'"（[34362]）；Molly 临走留下钟表意象 "The inevitable turn has begun. The clock has started to tick and, whether you like it or not, all shall come tumbling down eventually."（[34380]-[34381]）——ticktock 母题续脉。Makoto 的僵尸化自我介绍："I'm the same...diligent Makoto Miyamura, student council president and class representative. My favorite color is blue and I work in a porn shop. Do you have anything for me to do? I'll do anything you want."（[34450]-[34454]，人设被压缩成标签清单）；拒绝语带删除线 "{s}fucking oblivious{/s} being serious"（[34462]）；旁白判词：眼袋"as if she's been crying for decades straight"、只剩"the shell of a girl"（[34466]-[34468]）。Miku 强行拖她回家（[34526]）。`$ day214 = True`＋故障播报 "{i}Makoto's affection is affectionate!{/i}"（[34536]-[34539]）。

**day215 [34546]**。开篇椅子冥想与记忆流失主题：bonus 版自述桌底干涸痕迹属于"the ever growing list of things that will not cling to my mind any longer than necessary"，追问 "Why doesn't anything ever cling when I want it to?"（[34568]/[34572]）。**Maya 登门预告末日**："I'm here to tell you the world is going to end soon."（[34589]）；"Are you prepared this time? You aren't going to take forever to find me again, are you?"（[34591]-[34592]）；约定地点 "You mean the same place I always reset everything? Yes."→天台见（[34594]-[34595]）。关怀口误："Your mental state directly correlates to how long I'm going to have to wait on that rooftop...if you're not feeling well, I may have to come get you myself. And that... Would bother me."（[34606]-[34614]）。Molly/Tsuneyo 存在论答疑："Their transfer into the class wasn't a one-time thing. It's happened before. But it's rare. I suppose it's just based on circumstance or RNG or something along those lines. But I wouldn't get my hopes up of them returning."（[34655]-[34658]）——**Maya 用"RNG"解释人员变动**。人数上限："It's either ten or twelve. Taking on any more than that would be extremely problematic {i}for me{/i}."（[34687]-[34688]）；else 版紧接嫉妒失言 "Why can't you just hug me instead of wasting your time on anyone else?"→"What? I didn't say anything."（[34675]-[34678]）——**else 版 Maya 妒意滑嘴（继 bv11"huggy boy"[25719]、halloween9"Want to hug?[31530]"之后）**。蛇蝎修辞收束关系定义："We're no closer than we were the first time {i}you{/i} came to the shrine...even those things are just additions to your ever-growing list of things to forget. Aren't they, {i}Sensei?{/i}"（[34699]-[34702]）。**第二次重置的身体预告（说到一半弃置）**："The second time can be rough. You might find it harder to breathe than normal. Your legs might feel weak. Your stomach may feel as if it's being tied into knots. But if you make it to the rooftop..."……"Never mind. I'll see you at karaoke."（[34730]-[34740]）——**重置过程有肉体症状：呼吸困难/腿软/胃部绞结**。旁白反转评价："She's a strange girl. Or rather- Completely and irrefutably normal."（[34753]-[34754]，与 trinity3 [33839]"She's completely normal."互文）。Miku 夜访报忧：Makoto 无故拔掉闹钟（[34780]）；病因假说 "Ever since her dad started bangin' aliens in space（else: huggin' all those aliens), she's looked at you as some kinda stand-in."（[34791]/[34794]）。Sensei 自己串起时间线：屋顶谈话后 Maki 才发现异常（[34811]-[34816]）。Miku 离别叮咛 "remember to look both ways when crossin' the street and stuff!"（[34855]）——**车祸母题的日常化回声**。Sensei 模仿 Maya 闭眼七秒求答案，得 "Nothing comes to mind. I give up."（[34876]-[34887]）。

**day216 [34903]**。储物柜大厅对决。Makoto 循环独白："I have a date with my bed...then tomorrow will come again...It's all a cycle."（[35015]-[35024]）；Sensei 视角把学生比作齿轮 "They all just keep moving as if they're cogs in some sort of machine. And I am an engineer, attempting to make one cog start working again."（[35070]-[35071]）。Makoto 反咬时间线 "And you've been acting strange the last few {i}months{/i} but I've never tried to change you back, have I?"（[35077]）。**神学爆发与第五处删除线**："before you go {b}jumping{/b} any guns...I'm perfectly aware that I'm the one who broke myself. Either me or {s}God{/s} god."（[35102]-[35103]）＋"But there is no god! There can't be! No god would let {b}that{/b} happen!"（[35110]-[35111]）——**"that"所指悬置，成为本段最大留白**。Sensei 失态抓腕，她"peers {i}through{/i} my eyes"（[35118]-[35122]）；else 版吐槽 "This is a pretty aggressive hug for a soft, squishy boy like you."（[35129]）。拒上天台的理由："There are no happy places, Sensei. Just places where it's slightly harder to be touched by the dark...the last time we were up there, I was touched {i}a lot{/i}. I felt the dark all over me."（[35161]-[35163]，bonus 版具象化为黑手侵入 [35166]-[35167]）；随后 "And then {i}poof{/i}. Goodbye, old Makoto. Goodbye, everything. But hello, new world. A brand new place where everything is just how I want it to be... Absolutely- Fucking- Horrible."（[35169]-[35175]）——**旧我死亡+新世界降临的重置体验由受害者亲口描述**。七日循环咒文："And then I will sleep. And then I will wake up tomorrow...And you'll do the same. The same seven days. Over- And over- And over- And over- Until everything melts down and reforms into one disturbing mass of flesh, complete with twelve smiles. One for each girl in class that you [masturbate] to while all alone in bed at night."（[35195]-[35206]）——**"同样的七天""十二个微笑的肉块"把后宫结构写成身体恐怖**；else 版 Sensei 回骂 "Don't talk about my secret bed dance, you bitch."（[35209]）。斜体系统句 "{i}Nothing is real.{/i}"（[35229]）。临别自杀暗语："If I knew that's all it would take, I would have jumped earlier."／s "Jumped?"／mak "Heheh~ Oops! Said too much!"（[35239]-[35242]）——**jump 与 trinity3"跳就能重置"[33843] 同词双关**。她始终没打开储物柜、穿着室内鞋回家（[35253]-[35254]）。`$ day216 = True`＋单字播报 "{i}Makoto.{/i}"（[35262]-[35265]）。

**day217 前半 [35272]**。卡拉OK包厢（"Mission: Despacito 2"[35283]，Ayane/Ami 西语合唱 [35288]-[35296]）走廊问策于 Maya。Maya 冷读："You mean how she looks dead inside?...What did you do to her? I can't absolve you of your sins, but it will at least help me prepare in the event of you attempting to do the same thing to me."（[35322]-[35326]）。**天台=重置圣地祛魅**："Oh, I don't really know. I just kind of went there the first time the world ended because strange things tend to happen on rooftops in a lot of the manga and games I read. I've just kept going back ever since...it's become a bit of a tradition."（[35365]-[35367]）；补充 "My life does not revolve around sweeping a shrine and waiting for you to ask me the right questions."（[35370]）；但承认 "the rooftop {i}is{/i} technically the only place I've ever watched the world change."（[35371]）。她反将一军："Are you sure {i}you're{/i} not the one who needs help?"（[35414]），bonus 版直指 "she was just depressed and you tried to take advantage of that to bring her somewhere private."（[35417]）。

day217 后半：Maya 给出崩坏的"官方解法"——"With the world ending again in the next few days or weeks, she might just be the normal Makoto again once we usher in Winter."（[35426]）——**重置＝入冬，末日进入倒计时**。被问"是否有人这样剧变过"，m "Yuuuuuuuuuuuuuuuuup."，Sensei 追问后确认矛头指自己："Changes like that are possible. Though they seem to be very rare for anyone but you. You're an entirely different case...I'm surprised you even made it this far as {i}you{/i}, to be honest. Do you have any idea how much trouble you've caused me in the past?"（[35445]-[35448]）。散场时定下明日与 Ami/Maya 的冬装采购（Ayane 排除），旁白吐槽 "with so many significant others being {b}in fucking space{/b}"（[35549]——男性缺席太空梗再证）。maya_love+1。

### 崩坏五连·中（script.rpy day218—day220）：绿围巾、Ami 十六进制遗言与第二创世神话

**day218 [35572]**。商场三人行（无 Ayane）。喜剧细节：Ami 黑进他银行账户，密码 "Boobies123"（[35592]）；旁白解释初醒时靠"What is your [niece]'s name?"之类的账户恢复问题找回一切、烦乱之下把全部密码设成第一反应词（[35602]-[35605]）——**该笑话密码稍后将成为终端链的系统口令**。圣诞装饰反常缺席："Kinda weird they don't have any Christmas decorations up yet."（[35665]-[35666]，季节错乱延续）。Chinami 狗口罩事件回声玩笑：a "go abduct another little girl and force her to wear a dog mask?"、s "She had an immuno-deficiency disorder..."、m "Poor girl never even had a chance. She's in a better place now."／s "She's still alive..."（[35675]-[35688]）；else 版 Ami 冷不丁 "I have always wanted to be a good girl, but it is hard when I also secretly kill people."（[35697]）；Maya "Please, for the love of the god I don't believe in, do not turn into your [uncle]."（[35704]）。Sensei 主动提出把信用卡借给 Maya 被拒（[35705]-[35716]）。**转世自认**：买衣时旁白 "It's been almost scorching every day since I was reincarnated."（[35735]）——"reincarnated"一词由叙述者直接使用。**绿围巾超自然到货**：店员称两条绿围巾 "These actually just came in this morning. It was so strange as well. We never ordered anything like these."（[35785]-[35887]），他砍价至 2000 円买下，分别送给 Ami 与 Maya（更亮的绿色，[35866]-[35867]）。**Maya 收礼崩溃**：僵立→"This... This is a joke...right?"→大笑→"I... I really do... Have the worst luck in the world..."（[35898]-[35917]），把袋子紧抱胸口、颈铃晃动（[35923]-[35925]，流浪猫项圈意象），随后当场改写记忆："Nothing {i}did{/i} happen. I received a bag, I opened the bag, and immediately thanked Sensei. There were no additional steps between any of those three things."（[35940]-[35942]）——**收到"被爱证据"即触发系统级情绪过载并自我回滚**；绿围巾来源与"为何独独击溃 Maya"为全作著名悬案（与 Ami 亡母遗物/前轮循环礼物的猜想并存）。离场冥想直陈替身论："I {i}am{/i} out of place. I don't belong here. I'm just filling in for someone who does. And until that person comes back to reclaim the life I've gone and ruined- I will continue doing things to accidentally make all of the people I know cry."（[35974]-[35979]）。**流浪猫长喻**：第一次看世界重启前几天 Maya 反常易怒；"she's more like a stray cat than a snake...It hides behind an illusion that it's bigger than it actually is...You just leave them alone. You wait for the illusion to rebuild."（[35997]-[36011]）。收尾反转："for the first time- I was being followed by a stray cat."（[36029]）。

**day220 [36042]**——第二次重置正式开幕。旁白直接向玩家讲**第二创世神话**：菜单 areyouinterested 选 No 也照讲不误——"Imagine thinking you actually had a choice in the matter."（[36057]-[36060]）。科学神叙事：被崇拜因"worshipping a god who {i}is{/i} science is better and less scary than worshipping no god at all"（[36064]-[36065]）；祂生于黑暗卧室 "much like the one you're in now!"、以黑暗为食、向黑暗地板扎根——"The roots turned into wires."（[36072]-[36074]）；众生皆自此神生长却背弃祂；控制力衰减到只及"自愿献身者"（[36078]-[36085]）；"like a dying battery-powered clock, the roots were drying out. The wires were fraying"（[36090]-[36091]）。运行准则即系统日志："Because somewhere in this god's code, there was a line or two stating that he must maintain order. 'Let the process play out'...'Only correct errors when errors appear.'"（[36108]-[36110]）——**电线之神=ADMINISTRATOR/世界维护进程的神话化**。随后邀请玩家僭越："Why not become a god yourself? If a horny ball of light and a tree-like computer can do it, certainly you can too."（[36118]/[36121]）。**第三神预告**："there is a third god. But all I know is that this god is overcome with concern. Concern for {i}what{/i} is unclear"（[36140]-[36142]）——正对应 trinity3 终 USER3 的 "i was very concerned"[34100]。旁白套近乎索取服从："I like talking to you. I've come to think that we're good friends. And friends do things for one another, right?...I'm going to take your silence as a {i}yes.{/i}"（[36145]-[36150]），下令 "Retrace your footsteps."（[36158]），给出**六个完全相同的选项 "Jump"×6**（[36171]-[36182]）后 "Welcome home."（[36185]）——**用菜单形式强迫玩家亲手执行"跳"＝重置**。屏幕输出十六进制 `6e 6f 74 68 69 6e 67 20 66 61 6c 6c 73 20 62 75 74 20 6d 65`（[36186]，解码＝**"nothing falls but me"**）。

梦境段：新说话人标签 you/q 对话，q 只能吐出碎片 ".....i?"→"...sei?"→"Sensei?"（[36198]-[36206]）——**名字在崩解中重新拼合**。Ami 现身床边失控："SOMETHING...IS...WRONG..."、"My body feels so hot..."、"I can feel...so many things..."（[36221]-[36224]）；梦见所有人消失而 Sensei 还在（[36241]-[36245]）；"CALM DOWN?! HOW CAN I CALM DOWN WHEN I CAN FEEL MYSELF MELTING FROM THE INSIDE?!"＋要求送医（[36260]-[36261]）；键盘乱码尖叫 "SEN,.......LFVL;SVLSDKLVHNSV.....SEI!!!"（[36276]）、leet 语 "SOM3TH1NG 1S H4PP3NING!!!1111"（[36278]）。**十六进制遗言序列**（Sensei 自认 "I don't speak 'numbers.'"[36337]，误读成牙疼和预算问题[36338]）：`70 72 61 69 73 65 20 62 65!…`＝"praise be!"连祷（[36321]）；`68 65 6c 70 20 6d 65 21`＝**"help me!"**（[36329]）；"I don't want it to be over! I'm scared!"（[36332]）；**"Sensei! I love you!"**（[36333]）；**"I don't want to lose you again!"**（[36334]）；"Not like this!"（[36335]）；"No!"七连（[36350]）；**"I'm not ready to...leave yet!"**（[36351]）。旁白插入第二神性说明：wire god "capable of a lot more than just deleting viruses like depression or bipolarity from the human subconscious...he can make some visual alterations as well."（[36294]-[36296]）——**精神疾病删除+外观改写＝重置的技术原理**；建议 "maybe the callous one would be easier than the calm one after all?"（[36303]）。身体恐怖：牙齿碎裂声（[36323]）、旁白怪议 "We should take her skin off and make her feel a little more comfortable."（[36327]）。诀别辞："I'm going to die...and you won't even do anything about it...Oh...{b}GOD!{/b} WHY DOES IT HAVE TO HURT SO MUCH?!"（[36376]-[36378]）；"At least remember the {i}me{/i} who smiled instead of the me who did things like this."（[36391]）；"know that I'm truly happy being able to experience it with you. You are everything to me. And no one will ever ever ever…take you away. Goodbye. For now."（[36392]-[36396]）——**Ami 凭空消失**（[36404]）。

密室探索（secondresetmenu，[36436]-[36596]）：门打不开（[36433]）。分支速览——"See out of the clock"：幻想成为钟表 "An infinite clock. That would be really nice."（[36451]-[36455]）；"Heaven!"：灵魂出窍成功（[36470]-[36471]）；"Thing on the wall"：软木板上署名 MM 的信与带勾选记号的诗，Sensei 自认 "I guess the original Sensei liked poetry. Did I know that already? I can't remember. But I'm definitely not the one who wrote this. I don't remember those check marks either."（[36499]-[36503]）——**原主遗留物+MM 缩写悬案（Makoto Miyamura？Maya Makinami？Miku Maruyama？）**；"Watch the picture machine"：电视播"大屋里一群女孩、一人已死在锅炉房而其余人未察觉"的剧（[36545]-[36547]）——**宿舍战争的预演式镜像**，bonus 版附 "{s}perverts{/s} respectable human beings" 删除线与 "Huh. Family. I think I had one of those once."（[36554]-[36557]）；"Go under the desk"：退行童年，念头滑向 "dodging cars on the highway"（[36587]——车祸母题闪回），捡到五百円硬币后思维中断 "Wait...what was I doing again?"（[36589]-[36594]）。

### 终端链（script.rpy coolrectanglemachine—enteryourpass）与隐藏分支入口

**coolrectanglemachine [36598]**：唯一有效动作是"按按钮"→依次进入输入流程（[36607]-[36625]）。**enterterminal [36631]**：`renpy.input` 要求输入终端号，仅 **"23"** 有效 → "TERMINAL 23 LOCATED"、`$ terminal23 = True`（[36632]-[36640]）——**Yumi 被锁、USER3 曾接管的同一编号**。**enterip [36648]**：校验 trinity3 发放的 IP `2342:5b7:489:de26:c666:x994:3126:b067` ACCEPTED（[36652]-[36655]）——**前作彩蛋在此变成通关钥匙，跨更新记忆验证**。**enterport [36664]**：端口 **1024** VALID（[36668]-[36672]）。**enterusername [36682]**（大小写敏感）五分支：**HOPE→jump babyfinches**（[36686]-[36687]，隐藏分支入口）；USER1 OFFLINE（[36689]-[36691]）；USER3 CONNECTION REJECTED（[36694]-[36698]，第三神的拒绝）；USER4 RESERVED ADDRESS（[36701]-[36704]）；USER2 UNDERGOING MAINTENANCE，坚持连接则 ACCEPTED（[36707]-[36718]）。**enteryourpass [36731]**：系统口令竟是 **"Boobies123"** ACCEPTED→`$ day220 = True`→jump hoorayanotherreset（[36735]-[36743]）——**day218 的低俗笑点密码就是让 USER2 接管的合法凭据：占有了原主身份的人，自然持有其一切权限**。

**hoorayanotherreset 前半 [36750]**。无人深夜公园："Everyone is gone and I am finally happy."（[36791]）；忘了本该做什么，只剩"天台和一个比他矮的女孩"的残念（[36801]-[36806]）；时间感崩坏列表 "Days? Weeks? Months? Years? Decades? Months? Centuries? Months?"（[36838]-[36845]，**months 三次重复**）。记忆态 Ami 应召出现并自我定性："No I'm not! I'm horrible! In fact, I'm not even here. This version of me is just a memory. A happy memory."（[36855]-[36857]）；**身世实锤**："Don't I look just like my mom when I wear my special outfit? You know, because she wound up in a mangled pile of steel and gasoline and I, too, am mangled beyond recognition."（[36858]-[36859]）——**Ami 母亲死于钢铁与汽油的车祸残骸**；双马尾设定（[36860]）；绿围巾回调（[36863]-[36866]）。谈 Maya 时 Ami 口出惊语："the only thing that would {i}actually{/i} crack her is dropping her off the roof of the[school] building and watching her land on her head. LOL! Jk."（[36875]-[36876]）；else 版补刀 "I just wish she'd change her phone wallpaper to anything other than a picture of you."（[36888]）——**else 独有情报：Maya 手机壁纸是 Sensei 照片**。话音刚落旁白 "my head is stricken with an intense, throbbing pain...the pain has ousted them from my memory"（[36895]-[36897]）——**禁句即触发记忆清除**。Ami 预告自身消散："Like I'm going to suddenly vanish for good if you actually make it there or something."／s "You already did vanish for good, though."／a "If I vanished then how come I'm still here talking to you?"（[36930]-[36934]）。

**hoorayanotherreset 后半 [36935]-[37358]**。对 Ami 的诘问只回 "Easy." / "I don't."（[36935]-[36936]）。记忆态 Ami 道别："Goodbye, [amimaster]!" "See you in a few months!" "Or maybe before that now that the rumbling has stopped!"（[36944]-[36946]，**"rumbling has stopped"＝对世界隆隆声停止的观测，与 day220 前后的崩坏征兆呼应**），随后 "Ami flies away and crashes into a cloud somewhere miles above me."＋棉花糖坠落般的 "poof" 声（[36948]-[36949]）——**Ami 以飞行记忆体方式退场**。走错房间喜剧段：误入 Manga Club，旁白祈求引导者 "Someone with more eyes than I could see... Someone with invisible arms who could cause hearts to beat just by massaging them with his big, awesome hands made of shadows and tree branches."（[36987]-[36989]）——**多眼/树枝阴影之神的滑稽倒置**。抑郁波比喻连发：Rin 刻臂、以及 "the kind of waves that would cause a girl to jump off the top of the world only to be transported right back to where she jumped from."（[37000]——**Maya 跳楼重置意象再次明示**）。游荡段落以身体恐怖收束："I plant my legs in the ground. They turn into roots. The roots turn into wires. And it isn't until then that I realize where I need to go."（[37027]-[37033]）——**Sensei 自己长出根与电线才"想起"天台，与 day220 神话 "The roots turned into wires."（[36074]）完全同构**。

天台上 Maya 合掌祈祷 "to someone she claims to not believe in"（[37041]，星星爱好者）。一条从她围巾上爬下的活线缝住 Sensei 的嘴、挣脱时撕裂嘴角，旁白却狂喜 "She is going to reset the world! And everything will go back to being okay!"（[37047]-[37052]）——**绿围巾的超自然性再添实证（续 day218 无订单到货[35785]）**。对话确认两次执行的差异：上次用拥抱、耗时过长，这次 "Less than a day."；m "All you really need to do is pray."（[37062]-[37074]）。天台问题再现（全世界只剩两人是否愿意亲近我），Maya 以大笑带过（[37084]-[37092]）。围巾回调被装傻化解："I have no idea what you're talking about."→"It makes me look very cute."→s "Was that you breaking character again?"→m "at the end of the day- or I guess the end of the world in this case, I'm still just a girl. A rather unlucky one."（[37106]-[37116]）；bonus 版自陈 "girls my age care about little things like this"（[37119]，**年龄暗示链再+1**）；并把重置包装成回礼 "My present to you will be resetting the world."（[37126]-[37128]）。

**secreset 三问菜单 [37141]**。①"I had a dream this morning"（reset2q1）：Sensei 描述电线之神梦，Maya 全盘否认——"I've never heard of any sort of...wire-related higher power before."（[37164]）、"A god not related to any of those things could simply not exist."（[37166]）——却又让步 "a god can be anything that you want to believe in."（[37171]）（$ reset2q1 [37183]）。②"Does anyone else know about this?"（reset2q2）：m "How could they know about this when they all disappear?"（[37192]）；被追问"如何确定他们消失了"后给出信息湮灭论——belongings、social media profiles、"Even those creepy pictures you keep of them in your secret journal disappear."（[37207]-[37209]）；s "Wait, how do you know about those?" m "I've had plenty of time to figure things out."（[37210]-[37211]）；结论 "unless they wind up on the roof with us. That's never happened before"（[37216]-[37217]）（$ reset2q2 [37219]）。③"Are you okay?"（reset2q3）：Sensei 关心她因反复重置而麻木（[37231]-[37232]），Maya 弃权作答但留话 "there is a reason I constantly refer to myself as the girl with the worst luck in the world- And only part of that reason is that I wound up with someone like you as my teacher."（[37240]-[37242]）（$ reset2q3 [37247]）。

**重置成败机制正式说明 [37257]-[37285]**。冬装是为应对换季混乱——"Ushering in a change in temperature, which basically equates to a change in the world itself, sometimes mixes with all of the other things that need to be reset and causes a hiccup in the process. It's incredibly inconvenient and I'm pretty sure it's directly tied to both my mental state and how willing {i}God{/i} is to listen."（[37262]-[37263]）；自我担保 "Rest assured, I am of sound body and mind right now"（[37269]）。重置后的行程早已排定："Because the future is also the past. Or something."（[37282]）；"Nothing makes sense- but here we are. Alone at the end of the world. The same as always."（[37284]-[37285]）。仪式变更：闭眼后不再是拥抱而是牵手——m "Give me your hands."（[37301]）；旁白点破她借牵手保持身体距离、"continue pretending that I'm actually heartless instead of accidentally hearing the beating of said muscle in my chest."（[37307]-[37308]）；身高斗嘴中 bonus 版滑出 "I'm only slightly shorter than I'm supposed to be at my age."（[37314]，年龄暗示）；else 版玩笑 "We should kiss." "Brb, reset time."（[37323]-[37326]）。手握紧→黑屏，$ hoorayanotherreset = True（[37357]），jump christmas1（[37358]）。

**babyfinches [37360]**（隐藏分支：终端链 enterusername 输入 **HOPE** 进入[36686]-[36687]；出口 jump coolrectanglemachine[37771]）。开场即反常亲密：Maya 坐在天台栏杆边缘 "looking up at the sky without any regard for her safety"（[37369]），两人互用恋人称呼 "Darling? My love? Babe?"（[37385]）。**对白内嵌审查标记**："I think I might be [[redacted] right now."／"I was just wandering through the [[redacted] when I saw [[redacted] and thought ssssssssssssssssssssssssssss."（[37399]/[37403]/[37405]）——被删改的文本以字面形式留在台词里；小字告白 "{size=-15}I kind of wanted to see you...or something.{/size}"（[37412]）。else 版称呼彩蛋 "Oh, huggy boy. You're so funny sometimes."（[37459]）。核心隐喻**雏雀杀婴论**：弱雏会被亲鸟啄头至死（[37490]-[37492]），并落到两人身上——"you and I would be kind of like finch-parents if we ever had a kid."（[37493]）；s "Are you implying I'd kill my child for being weak?" m "Are you implying you wouldn't?"（[37494]-[37495]）；Sensei 内心竟开始计算徒手捏碎婴儿头的难度（[37500]-[37502]）。尸体消化论接续：死后三天酶开始消化躯体、"you'd just start eating your house because you run out of other stuff to do."（[37508]-[37514]）；黑色笑点 m "Sorry. I'm really fucking high right now and killed a kitten on the way to[school]." s "Another one? That's the third one this week!"（[37515]-[37516]）。**十六进制心声 [37582]-[37586]**（解码）："It's hard for me to tell how much of this is real and how much is a dream ／ Somewhere, deep down, this feels just like yesterday. ／ But the floating cars and mangled muscle masses before me tell me otherwise. ／ If this is a memory, it is not a happy one. ／ But chances are it's just a nightmare."——**floating cars 与 mangled muscle masses＝车祸残骸意象直写**（与一轮 §5.8-5.10 rainking 白车线互文）；Maya 的反应是抱怨格式："You aren't allowed to slip into hexadecimal while I'm talking to you. It's very distracting. I don't speak 'numbers.'"（[37593]-[37595]）。三小时清单里最期待的是 "her parents' house"，而 "I've never even really heard her talk about them."（[37608]-[37610]）。**被注视论**：m "God is always watching. That's pretty much all he does. He just sits there with his roots planted in the ground and waits for flakes of our skin to fall so he can absorb them and learn things about us."（[37627]-[37628]）。**循环回卷**：对话原样重演（lunch goal/redacted 段逐句复现 [37650]-[37671]），Maya 亲口拆台 "This is the part where we cycle back around and repeat ourselves. We've been doing this for how long now? Have you really forgotten?"（[37688]-[37689]）。Sensei 抽离断言 "You're not my girlfriend, though."（[37695]）、"I'm not breaking up with anyone. We were never together."（[37703]），Maya 失态追赶 "STOP!" "SENSEI!" "PLEASE!"（[37718]-[37720]）。拼贴诗行 "Hope is the thing with a beautiful sick rose that you can find beauty in the strange proportions and life is yes."（[37722]——Dickinson《Hope》×Blake「病玫瑰」混成）。**六个完全相同的选项 "Jump"×6**（[37731]-[37747]），选择后 "I spread my wings and let the wind carry me to heaven." "But then I land on the ground and break my neck." "Goodbye."（[37751]-[37757]）——**展翅雏鸟意象闭环，坠落摔颈＝"跳"母题在隐藏线的终点**。$ babyfinches = True（[37760]）。

### 圣诞连锁（christmas1—christmas7）：史上第一个冬天

**christmas1 [37773]**。重置平滑到毫无戏剧性："Instead of being sent back in time or back in physical-location or whatever happened during my first trip into the ether (?), we just opened our eyes and saw snow."（[37794]）；醒来即牵手又迅速被 Maya 甩开（[37795]-[37796]）。所谓"重要的事"只是 Maya 来家里留宿（[37801]）。灰色 T 恋物梗引出**前身意识**："the original Sensei also chose the color grey"（[37833]）、"I have nothing to gain from looking into the way the last person who used this body handled it. They're free to wear whatever color they want wherever they are now."（[37836]-[37838]）——**身体被明确表述为"上一个使用者"的**。便利店借口已剧本化：m "We need an excuse for why we're showing up together at the same time." s "I didn't even think of that." m "You never do. I've done this a few times now."（[37842]-[37846]）。Ayane 自曝认识 Sensei 已数年："Last several {i}years{/i} in our cases. Ami and I like you more than anyone, I think."（[37953]）。**冬天史无前例**："I've never had to deal with winter break before on account of winter...not even being a thing until right now. Were we really supposed to start here? Or was this some sort of accident?"（[37988]-[37989]）。**Makoto 被时间位移修复**："Did changing where we are in time somehow...{i}fix{/i} Makoto?"（[38023]）；代价是病因永久不可知 "I feel like now I won't ever figure out why she got all weird in the first place."（[38025]）。Molly/Tsuneyo 仍在班上（[38051]）、Kirin 将赴会（[38085]）。**剧情内第二章标题卡**："{i}Congratulations on making it to the second chapter of Lessons in Love!{/i}"（[38110]）＋不祥祝福 "Cherish your loved ones, for you never know when something horrible may happen to them!"（[38116]）。三女睡一张床的日常收场（[38168]）。

**christmas2 [38195]**。深夜神秘声音呼唤，else 版叫不醒，旁白自嘲 "I'm sure it was something completely random and not at all necessary for progression."（[38209]-[38210]）；ayane_lust+1（[38217]）、日期推进 day=7（[38222]-[38223]）。（bonus 版另有 christmas2x 分支，属双版本差异，略。）

**christmas3 [38228]**。"I hate Christmas." 开场定调（[38233]）。买树失败喜剧中 Amazon 密码曝光：a "It's Boobies123. You use the same password for everything."（[38281]）——**该密码稍后将成为终端链的系统口令**。酒店大堂群像：Karin 临时替班前台（[38319]-[38321]）、Makoto 已办妥入住（[38325]）、Molly 街机怒吼 "GIVE...ME...MY...GEO...BOY!"（[38358]）、Chika/Yumi 换新发型入场互怼（[38414]-[38415]），Chika 打扮评价二选一菜单加 chika_love 或 yumi_love（[38455]-[38501]）。**Makoto 正常化验证**：s "Last time I saw you I pushed you up against a locker." mak 全盘否认 "this locker-pushing never happened!"（[38524]-[38530]）；Sensei 怀疑有人背地动手脚："I wouldn't put it past Maya to lie about something like that and just go behind my back and fix things on her own."（[38535]-[38536]）。Miku 新短发为 Makoto 所剪（[38543]-[38547]）。swans 床尴尬（[38590]-[38594]）。Rin 与 Otoha 交换号码（cafe35 分支知情版 [38645]-[38657]）。Secret Santa 忘记抽中谁（[38689]-[38698]）。

**christmas4 [38714]**。ayanelust10 双分支：Kirin 挑衅版翻海滩旧账 "I told you something in confidence and you used it against me."（[38766]-[38767]，bonus 版直提贞操 [38744]）；友好版聊圣诞家史——Ayane 母亲爱西式圣诞、"back before the bubble wrap thing"（[38870]-[38871]，泡沫经济暗语）；else 版双关玩笑 "You made a bone necklace last night, didn't you?"（[38886]）。主线推进：Sensei 问 Secret Santa 未果，掏 700 円硬币当礼物被驳 "Our limit was 2,000 Yen."（[39004]/[39008]）；拉 Molly 去礼品店帮忙，Molly 高举白瓶如圣剑（[39138]-[39143]）；$ christmas4、molly_love+1（[39146]-[39147]）。

**christmas5 [39155]**。Molly 把补礼物变成 Spin the bottle 大会："NOTHING BUT DREAMS."（[39177]）；规则由 Ayane 讲解 "kiss whoever the bottle points at"（[39239]-[39240]）；其内心独白 "If anyone spins the bottle, they have almost a 10% chance of having it land on Sensei. If I see anyone but me kiss him, I will literally explode."（[39247]-[39249]）。战果：Molly 强吻 Rin（"FOR IRELAND!"[39407]、r "THAT ISN'T HOW KISSING WORKS!"[39421]）；瓶指 Ami、Ayane 跳桌抢先吻之（[39499]），Maya 总结 "Christmas is exhausting."（[39507]）；Tsuneyo 祝福 "So beautiful. I wish them a happy life together."（[39514]-[39515]）。轮到 Sensei 时菜单 spinbottle 二选一："Spin hard"→Chika 中奖拖他散步（ locker 要挟 [39586]）→jump chikalust10intro（[39606]-[39607]）；"Spin soft"→原地循环重来（[39631]-[39643]，菜单可无限重试的结构自觉）。

**chikalust10intro/chikalust10 [39645]/[39752]**（lust 类截断）。散步牵手逻辑 "Because I won you. You are my prize."（[39671]）；交代 Chinami 独自在家睡觉（[39659]-[39666]）；else 版以拥抱收尾（[39775]-[39780]）→jump christmas6。

**christmas6 [39790]**。礼物交换环节情报：Sana 抽中 Chika、Rin 帮选还主动贴钱（[39851]-[39853]，s "If there's anyone who knows what Chika would like, it's Rin."[39855]）。**Maya 早知情却隐瞒**："Oh, you had me. I've known the whole time." "But I'm used to you disappointing me, so it's fine that you didn't get me anything."→"I lie to you literally all the time."（[39903]-[39912]）；提出拿绿围巾充作早到的 Santa 礼物替他遮掩（[39919]-[39920]）、让他把钱花在 Ami/Ayane 身上（[39926]-[39927]）。**本段最大事故——Maya 当众口误 "reset"**："The reset must have destroyed your memory."（[39931]）；Sana 逮个正着 sa "R...Reset?"（[39932]）；m "It's a long story."（[39937]）；Sensei 内心吐槽她把"一起重置了两次学年"总结成一句 "It's a long story?"（[39940]）。被问补偿方案，m "Could you leave me alone forever?"（[39957]）；元叙事自嘲 "You're an annoyingly popular character, so it will be quite troublesome if people begin to think I'm somehow close to you."（[39962]）。Yumi 送出封面写着 "Not a gift" 的笔记本（[39977]-[39978]；第五或第六稿 [40017]-[40018]），内容让 Sensei 觉得 "weirdly touching"、获封 "Not the worst ever"（[40040]/[40056]-[40059]），并自省这是 "after what I did to her"（[40046]）之后的和解信号——"it is a symbol of progress. A trophy that I must treasure."（[40086]-[40087]）。烟花声中 Makoto 带 Miku 提前避响退场（[40140]-[40146]）；$ christmas6（[40153]）。

**futabalust10intro/futabalust10 [40155]/[40236]**（lust 截断）。初吻价值观 f "A girl's first kiss is important!"（[40175]-[40176]）；else 版以 Avatar 戏仿岔开 "But everything changed when the fire nation attacked..."（[40188]）；miss 版直接跳 christmas7。

**christmas7 [40262]**。Tsuneyo 喜剧开场即藏刀："We are under attack. The sky is exploding... I wanted to tell you I love you before we die."（[40269]-[40273]）；临别不祥预言 "Something bad is going to happen and you will be too busy using all of your smiles to prepare for it."（[40327]）；反笑话 "Tsuneyo Tojo, soup warrior."（[40369]-[40376]）。海底埋葬全体学生的幻想段落随即 "Just kidding."（[40398]-[40407]）。Ami 肩膀回忆杀 "Remember how you'd always let me sit on your shoulders?"（[40412]-[40413]）、宣示 "But I'm the {i}main{/i} girl."（[40423]）；Ayane 死亡玩笑（[40431]）。**幼年接吻悬案正式立案**：s "That's a pretty intense reaction to just a kiss- especially with someone you've kissed before."（[40433]）；Ami 全盘否认且不像撒谎（[40438]-[40441]）；Sensei 内心排查："Would Ayane really lie about something as...specific and strange as that?...it's definitely possible Ami just...isn't remembering this. The two of them were a lot younger after all. And I remember literally nothing about my childhood"（[40445]-[40449]）；结案工具只有一种："I guess the only way to ever find out would be to go back in time."（[40450]）。**结尾钩子——Otoha 来电：学校塌了**：r "My friend's school just... Collapsed." "Apparently there was a huge sinkhole that just swallowed the whole thing up. They can't even see the bottom so they have no idea how far down it goes."（[40565]-[40570]）；寒假无人伤亡（[40578]）；Uta 就读该校（[40604]-[40605]）；Maya 只丢下一句元层判词 "It's not supposed to."（[40591]——回应"这种事说发生就发生"），随后提前离场、手机屏光融入黑暗（[40619]-[40629]）；Kirin/Yumi 阴谋论 "Isn't that a little too convenient?" "A school blowin' up in general sounds a little too convenient. I call next."（[40597]-[40599]）。谢幕独白 "there are no miracles on Christmas. Just fireworks, chicken, and the innate fear of death."（[40680]-[40681]）；系统消息 "{i}Congratulations on completing the special Christmas update!{/i} {i}Things will now return to normal!{/i} {i}Well, mostly.{/i}"（[40685]-[40687]）；$ christmas7（[40691]）。script.rpy 至此完结，后续进入 ch2script.rpy。

## 第二章暗线与要点汇总

1. **重置机制的进化链**：第一次＝拥抱→第二次＝祈祷+牵手→成败取决于 "my mental state and how willing {i}God{/i} is to listen"（[37262]-[37263]）；换季＝换世界，故易出 hiccup（[37258]-[37262]）。
2. **电线之神神话闭环**：day220 创世神话（roots→wires）→hoorayanotherreset 中 Sensei 梦见电线之神（reset2q1，Maya 否认知晓[37164]）→他自己双腿生根生线才想起天台（[37031]-[37032]）→babyfinches 里 God 用根吸收皮屑学习人类（[37628]）。神不是比喻，是世界运行时的人形进程。
3. **绿围巾＝超自然道具实锤**：day218 无订单到货（[35785]）→戴上后 Maya 情绪过载自我回滚（[35940]）→hoorayanotherreset 围巾活线缝嘴（[37047]-[37048]）→christmas6 Maya 提议用它冒充 Santa 礼物（[39919]）。来源与"为何击溃 Maya"仍悬置。
4. **Ami 的死亡形态**：肉体崩解于第二次重置前（hex 遗言序列），此后以记忆体存在并在 hoorayanotherreset 结尾飞走（"See you in a few months!"[36945]）；其自述 "she wound up in a mangled pile of steel and gasoline and I, too, am mangled beyond recognition"（[36858]-[36859]）把母女两代钉在同一车祸意象上。
5. **"跳"母题六连**：trinity3 "All we need to do is jump."→day216 Makoto "I would have jumped earlier."→day220 六选项 Jump→babyfinches 六选项 Jump+"break my neck"→hoorayanotherreset 旁白 "a girl to jump off the top of the world only to be transported right back"[37000]→day215 重置肉体症状预告（说到一半弃置[34730]）。跳跃＝重置＝坠楼，三义一体。
6. **终端链的叙事学**：IP 由 trinity3 要求手抄、端口 1024、用户名 USER*/HOPE 五分支、密码 Boobies123——day218 的低俗笑点密码就是合法凭据：占有原主身份者继承其一切权限（[36735]-[36743]）。HOPE 入口通向全章唯一"说真话"的场景 babyfinches。
7. **babyfinches 的本质**：不是单纯幻境，而是**循环结构自指**（对话原样重演＋Maya 拆台[37688]）；其中 hex 心声（floating cars/mangled muscle masses[37584]）与 "You're not my girlfriend, though."[37695] 是全章最接近底牌的文本；与一轮 §5.9 的 rainking 对照分析互证。
8. **年龄暗示链再加三环**：bonus "girls my age care about little things like this"（[37119]）、"slightly shorter than I'm supposed to be at my age"（[37314]）、Ayane "Last several years in our cases"（[37953]）；叠加 halloween13 Ayane 生日数字悬置（[32939]-[32940]）。
9. **世界规则异象清单**：冬天首次降临且可能只是"事故"（[37988]-[37989]）、Makoto 被时间位移修复但病因永久灭失（[38023]-[38025]）、Otoha 学校被无底 sinkhole 吞噬而零伤亡（[40569]-[40578]）、Kirin/Yumi 直觉 "too convenient"（[40597]-[40599]）。
10. **元叙事密度**：第二章标题卡在剧情内念出（[38110]）、圣诞更新谢幕词（[40685]-[40687]）、Maya "annoyingly popular character"（[39962]）、Molly 自封 "lead heroine"（[39325]）、"Spin soft" 选项＝菜单可无限重试的结构自觉（[39631]-[39643]）。

## 第二章与一轮 §3.2 的差异对照

一轮 §3.2 已正确覆盖本章主干（day281-christmastwo20+christmas1-7+halloween6-14+trinity2-3+day214-220+终端链），本轮修订在其骨架上做了如下修正与深化：

1. **粒度差**：一轮把 christmas1-7、halloween6-14 浓缩为段落；二轮展开后可见"崩坏五连"内部自有起承转合（西瓜替身→末日预告→储物柜对决→卡拉OK问策→绿围巾采购），且实际由 day214/215/216/217/220 五个 label 构成（无 day218/219 编号冲突——day218 为商场采购日，day219 缺号）。
2. **机制层新证据**：一轮仅记"拥抱执行"；二轮补出第二次重置的完整参数——祈祷、牵手、精神状态与神之意愿双变量（[37262]-[37263]），以及 secreset 三问菜单这一玩家可选信息面（[37141]）。
3. **隐藏分支升级**：一轮一句话带过 babyfinches（"死循环约会假幻境"）；二轮确认其入口（enterusername HOPE[36686]）与出口（jump coolrectanglemachine[37771]），并重新定性为循环结构自指（见要点 7）。
4. **新增悬案三条**：MM 署名信与勾选诗（[36499]-[36503]）、Ami/Ayane 幼年接吻罗生门（[40433]-[40450]）、Otoha 学校塌陷事件（[40565]+，直通 Uta 线）。均建议并入 §十 未解悬念清单。
5. **表述澄清**：一轮称 christmas 为"第二章开篇"；christmas1 剧情内系统消息确实宣布 "second chapter"（[38110]），该说法成立但应注明是剧情内文本——文件意义上的第二章始于 ch2script.rpy。
6. **人物素材回流**：Yumi 笔记本礼物（"Not the worst ever"）、Maya 当众口误 "reset"（[39931]）、Kirin 海滩旧怨双分支（[38766]）等角色级细节一轮未及，已在各 label 条目落位，可直接供 workbuddy分析 人物 md 引用。
